### [48] [Static Program Slicing Using Language Models With Dataflow-Aware Pretraining and Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26961)
*Pengfei He,Shaowei Wang,Tse-Hsun,Chen,Muhammad Asaduzzaman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Sliceformer是一种基于小型语言模型（如CodeT5+）的程序切片新方法，通过数据流感知预训练和约束解码机制解决现有方法的数据依赖建模不准确和幻觉生成问题，实验表明其性能显著优于现有基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的程序切片方法存在两个关键缺陷：数据依赖建模不准确（无法捕获精确数据流关系）和无约束生成（产生幻觉性token和语句）

Method: 提出两大创新：1) 基于数据流图的预训练目标（数据流保留语句置换 + 数据流感知跨度损坏）；2) 约束解码机制（词汇约束 + 句法约束）

Result: 在Java和Python程序切片基准测试中，相较于基线方法取得一致改进，ExactMatch指标提升高达22%

Conclusion: Sliceformer在Java和Python程序切片基准测试中取得了最先进的结果，ExactMatch指标提升高达22%

Abstract: Static program slicing is a fundamental software engineering technique for isolating code relevant to specific variables. While recent learning-based approaches using language models (LMs) show promise in automating slice prediction, they suffer from inaccurate dependency modeling and unconstrained generation, where LMs fail to capture precise data flow relations and produce slices containing hallucinated tokens and statements. To address these challenges, we propose Sliceformer, a novel approach that reformulates static program slicing as a sequence-to-sequence task using small language models such as CodeT5+. Sliceformer introduces two key innovations that directly target the identified limitations. First, to improve dependency modeling, we design dataflow-aware pretraining objectives that leverage data flow graphs (DFG) to teach models data dependencies through dataflow-preserving statement permutation and dataflow-aware span corruption. Second, to eliminate hallucination, we develop a constrained decoding mechanism that enforces both lexical and syntactic constraints. We evaluate Sliceformer on Java and Python program slicing benchmarks, demonstrating consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines with up to 22% gain in ExactMatch.

</details>


### [49] [Adaptive and AI-Augmented Security Testing: A Systematic Survey of Program Analysis, Feedback-Driven Testing, and Hybrid Learning-Based Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27000)
*Michael Wienczkowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Modern software systems are increasingly developed within rapid continuous integration and deployment (CI/CD) pipelines, where ensuring security prior to release presents significant technical and organizational challenges. Traditional static and dynamic analysis tools provide valuable structural and behavioral insights, yet they often operate in non-adaptive workflows and produce large volumes of warnings requiring manual triage. Feedback-driven fuzzing and search-based testing approaches have demonstrated the power of iterative input refinement guided by execution signals, while large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automated test generation but frequently lack semantic grounding in program structure. This paper presents a systematic survey of adaptive and AI-augmented security testing research across five domains: (1) structural program analysis for vulnerability detection, (2) DevSecOps and continuous security testing, (3) feedback-driven fuzzing and search-based testing, (4) LLM-based automated test generation, and (5) emerging hybrid systems integrating program analysis with adaptive learning. We analyze fifty-five peer-reviewed studies drawn from a systematic search of four major databases yielding 22,088 raw records. Our analysis reveals a persistent disconnect between structural program representations (ASTs, CFGs, and CPGs) and adaptive testing mechanisms. We characterize this as structural-adaptive fragmentation: a systematic separation that neither paradigm individually addresses. No existing system incorporates human triage signals as feedback for refining structural models. We conclude by identifying five open research challenges and outlining a unified agenda for semantically grounded, feedback-driven, polyglot security testing frameworks.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Accuracy: LLM Variability in Evidence Screening for Software Engineering SLRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27006)
*Gilberto Sussumu Hida,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro,Erika Yahata*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地评估了12种LLM和4种经典分类模型在系统文献综述研究筛选任务上的表现，发现LLM存在显著的性能异质性，摘要可用性是决定性因素，且LLM与传统模型之间的性能差异不足以证明LLM具有普遍优势。研究建议LLM的采用应谨慎，需基于具体场景进行试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统文献综述中的研究筛选阶段成本高、容易产生不一致性，且风险不对称（假阴性可能损害有效性）。尽管LLM正被快速采用，但在筛选阶段LLM的表现，特别是在特定LLM的选择及其与传统模型的比较方面，证据仍然有限。

Method: 该研究分析了来自4个提供商的12种LLM（OpenAI、Google Gemini、Anthropic、Llama）以及4种经典分类模型（逻辑回归、支持向量机、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯），在2个真实系统文献综述（共518篇论文）上进行了实验。实验从3个关键维度展开：（i）LLM性能变异性，（ii）输入特征组合（摘要、标题、关键词）对LLM性能的影响，（iii）LLM与传统分类模型的真实收益对比。

Result: LLM表现出显著的异质性，即使在温度为零的情况下仍存在残余非确定性。摘要的可用性是关键因素：移除摘要会一致性地降低性能，而向摘要添加标题和/或关键词并未产生稳健的收益。与经典模型相比，性能差异不够一致，无法支持LLM具有普遍优越性的结论。

Conclusion: LLM的采用应基于操作和治理约束（可重复性、成本、元数据可用性），并通过试点验证和明确报告变异性及输入配置来支持。研究建议在实际应用前进行充分的性能评估，而非假设LLM必然优于传统模型。

Abstract: Context: Study screening in systematic literature reviews is costly, inconsistency-prone, and risk-asymmetric, since false negatives can compromise validity. Despite rapid uptake of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is limited evidence on how such models behave during the study screening phase, particularly regarding the choice of specific LLMs and their comparison with classical models. Objective: To assess LLM performance and variability in screening, quantify the impact of input metadata (abstract, title, keywords), and compare LLMs with classical classifiers under a shared protocol. Methods: We analyzed 12 LLMs from 4 providers (OpenAI, Google Gemini, Anthropic, Llama) and 4 classical models (Logistic Regression, Support Vector Classification, Random Forest, and Naive Bayes) on 2 real Systematic Literature Reviews (SLRs), totaling 518 papers. The experimental design investigated 3 critical dimensions: (i) LLMs performance variability, (ii) the impact of input feature composition (abstract, title, and keywords) on LLM performance, and (iii) the real gain of using LLMs instead of more traditional classification models. Results: LLMs exhibited substantial heterogeneity and residual non-determinism even at temperature zero. Abstract availability was decisive: removing it consistently degraded performance, while adding title and/or keywords to the abstract yielded no robust gains. Compared to classical models, performance differences were not consistent enough to support generalizable LLM superiority. Discussion: LLM adoption should be justified by operational and governance constraints (reproducibility, cost, metadata availability), supported by pilot validation and explicit reporting of variability and input configuration.

</details>


### [51] [LLM-Guided Runtime Parameter Optimization for Energy-Efficient Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27032)
*Katelyn Crumpacker,Dimitrios Nikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 使用大语言模型辅助优化LLM推理的运行时参数，通过特定反馈提示方法实现人机协作优化。该方法比传统搜索方法更快收敛，能耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs consume significant energy at scale; selecting optimal runtime parameters typically requires deep domain expertise or computationally expensive traditional optimization methods that can take days to converge.

Method: Human-in-the-loop flow with LLM-assisted runtime parameter optimization using specific feedback prompting methods; iterative parameter tuning through chat-based LLMs that can adapt to hardware constraints.

Result: Enhanced prompt template converged in 3.4 prompts vs 5.2 prompts (baseline), consistently achieved lower final energy per token, and outperformed Sobol sampling in convergence speed.

Conclusion: LLM-assisted optimization with enhanced prompting achieves faster convergence and lower energy consumption than baseline methods, offering a practical human-in-the-loop solution for energy-efficient LLM inference.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become an integral part of many real-world workflows. However, LLMs consume a lot of energy, which becomes a large concern in the scale of the demand for these tools. As LLMs become integrated into different workflows, different applications have arisen to deal with the challenge of running inference for these tools. This raises another issue of choosing the runtime parameter values for these services in order to minimize the energy consumption. Oftentimes this requires deep knowledge of the application or traditional optimization methods that can take days to find optimal values. In this work, we created a human-in-the-loop flow with LLM-assisted runtime parameter optimization in order to solve this issue. With human-created, specific feedback prompting methods, chat-based LLMs can iteratively find energy-efficient inference parameters faster than traditional search methods. LLMs can also tailor their solutions to different hardware setups and easily take into account other system constraints. The enhanced prompt template was able to converge below the threshold at an average of 3.4 prompts compared to the baseline, which converged in an average of 5.2 prompts, and consistently achieved lower final energy per token. The enhanced prompt template also outperformed Sobol sampling in convergence speed.

</details>


### [52] [Where did we fail? -- Reproducing build failures in embedded open source software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27075)
*Han Fu,Andreas Ermedahl,Sigrid Eldh,Kristian Wiklund,Philipp Haller,Cyrille Artho*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PhantomRun是一个统一的抽象层和公开可重用数据集，用于标准化CI构建日志的检索、存储和再现，解决了嵌入式系统CI构建日志难以复用的问题，在4628个失败CI运行中实现了91.8%的构建重建率和98%的结果保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式系统中的软硬件协同开发导致CI构建频繁失败，原因是跨编译、 board配置和工具链约束的复杂性。虽然CI构建日志包含有价值的诊断信息，但由于运行环境、工具链和日志格式的异构性，这些日志寿命短且难以复用。

Method: PhantomRun提供了一个统一的抽象层和可重用数据集，标准化了CI构建日志和元数据的检索、存储和再现。它具备两大核心能力：检索任意提交的构建日志，以及在受控环境中忠实地重新执行相应构建。

Result: 在4628个失败的CI运行中，PhantomRun重建了91.8%的构建，并在98%的评估案例中保留了执行结果。

Conclusion: PhantomRun通过暴露统一的机器可读格式的所有构建产物和元数据，实现了CI失败的纵向研究和可复现分析。经验评估表明，重现的构建与原始构建高度匹配，仅在时间戳或微小非确定性重排序上有差异，证明了大规模历史CI重建的可行性。

Abstract: Due to hardware-software co-development in embedded systems, continuous integration (CI) builds frequently fail because of complex cross-compilation, board configurations, and toolchain constraints. Although CI build logs contain valuable diagnostic information, they are short-lived and difficult to reuse due to heterogeneous runners, toolchains, and log formats. To address these challenges, we present PhantomRun, a unified abstraction layer and publicly reusable dataset that standardizes the retrieval, storage, and reproduction of CI build logs and metadata. Across 4628 failing CI runs, we reconstructed 91.8% of builds and preserved execution outcomes in 98% of evaluated cases.
  PhantomRun provides two core capabilities: retrieving the build log of any commit and faithfully re-executing the corresponding build in a controlled environment. By exposing all build artifacts and metadata in a uniform, machine-readable format, PhantomRun enables reproducible and longitudinal studies of CI failures. An empirical evaluation shows that reproduced builds closely match their originals, typically differing only in timestamps or minor nondeterministic reordering, demonstrating the feasibility of large-scale historical CI reconstruction.

</details>


### [53] [On the Effectiveness of Modular Testing with EvoSuite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27112)
*Elizabeth Dinella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对Java程序的模块化随机测试提出改进方案。现有EvoSuite测试工具的模块化模式因禁止调用非目标setup方法而效果不佳。EMOTE通过允许非目标方法调用并调整适应度函数聚焦目标方法调用链，成功将目标方法分支覆盖率提升15.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EvoSuite的模块化测试模式存在过度严格的限制，禁止调用非目标setup方法，导致测试生成器无法有效初始化程序状态，从而造成目标方法的分支覆盖率低下。

Method: EMOTE修改了EvoSuite的两方面：(1)放宽对非目标setup方法的调用限制，允许它们出现在测试前缀中；(2)修改适应度函数，将分支覆盖率贡献评估聚焦于目标方法的调用链。该方法借鉴了开发者编写的fuzz驱动程序的实践模式。

Result: 在SF100基准测试的子集上评估，EMOTE使目标方法覆盖率提升15.15%。

Conclusion: EMOTE通过允许非目标方法调用并调整适应度函数，成功将目标方法覆盖率提升15.15%，证明了放宽严格限制对于模块化测试的有效性。

Abstract: This paper explores the effectiveness of modular randomized testing for object oriented programs in Java. Modular testing involves testing individual components of a program in isolation. Often times, for effective test generation, a series of non-target setup calls must be included to obtain high coverage of the target component. In this work, we evaluate and improve modular testing with the EvoSuite test generator. We find that due to strict restrictions that disallow calls to non-target setup methods, EvoSuite's modular testing mode is ineffective and often results in low branch coverage. We propose \textsc{emote} (Effective Modular Testing with EvoSuite): an enhancement to EvoSuite that relaxes this restriction, allowing non-target methods to be included in the test prefixes. This modification draws inspiration from developer-written fuzz drivers, which often invoke setup methods to properly initialize the state before testing the target method. To ensure meaningful test generation, we modify EvoSuite's fitness function to focus branch coverage contributions on the call chain originating from the target method. \textsc{emote} is evaluated on a subset of the SF100 benchmark, showing a 15.15\% improvement in coverage of the target methods.

</details>


### [54] [CI-Repair-Bench: A Repository-Aware Benchmark for Automated Patch Validation via CI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27148)
*Rabeya Khatun Muna,Md Nakhla Rafi,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CI-Repair-Bench is a benchmark for CI-verified, repository-level program repair constructed from 567 real GitHub Actions CI failure instances across 103 repositories. Unlike existing benchmarks, it evaluates repairs exclusively through full CI re-execution under original workflows and categorizes failures into 12 CI error types. The best-performing LLM achieves 18.9% repair success rate, with effectiveness varying significantly by error type.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing program repair benchmarks fall short in the CI setting: they are largely test-centric, restrict repairs to source code, assume fixed execution environments, and evaluate under simplified CI workflows that do not reflect real repository-level validation.

Method: Constructed from real GitHub Actions executions, containing 567 CI failure instances from 103 repositories. Evaluates repair correctness exclusively through full CI re-execution under original workflows. Failures are categorized into 12 CI error types. Includes a reference CI repair workflow that analyzes CI logs to localize faults and generate candidate patches.

Result: Best-performing LLM achieves an 18.9% repair success rate. Automated repair is most effective for localized, tool-enforced failures (formatting, linting), while environment, dependency, and configuration-related failures remain challenging.

Conclusion: CI-Repair-Bench provides a realistic evaluation foundation for advancing research on CI-native automated program repair. Automated repair is most effective for localized, tool-enforced failures (formatting, linting), while environment, dependency, and configuration-related failures remain challenging.

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) enforces repository-level correctness through multi-stage workflows and is central to modern software development, yet diagnosing and repairing CI failures remains challenging. Unlike traditional program repair, CI failures frequently involve non-code artifacts, environment and dependency issues, noisy execution logs, and workflow-level constraints. Existing program repair benchmarks fall short in this setting: they are largely test-centric, restrict repairs to source code, assume fixed execution environments, and evaluate under simplified CI workflows that do not reflect real repository-level validation. We introduce CI-Repair-Bench, a benchmark for CI-verified, repository-level program repair constructed from real GitHub Actions executions. It contains 567 CI failure instances from 103 repositories and evaluates repair correctness exclusively through full CI re-execution under original workflows. Failures are categorized into 12 CI error types, enabling fine-grained, error-type-aware evaluation. To demonstrate benchmark usage, we include a reference CI repair workflow that analyzes CI logs to localize faults and generate candidate patches. Empirical results show that automated repair is most effective for localized, tool-enforced failures such as formatting and linting, while environment, dependency, and configuration-related failures remain challenging; the best-performing LLM achieves an 18.9% repair success rate. CI-Repair-Bench provides a realistic evaluation foundation for advancing research on CI-native automated program repair.

</details>


### [55] [Theory Under Construction: Orchestrating Language Models for Research Software Where the Specification Evolves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27209)
*Halley Young,Nikolaj Björner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comet-H is an iterative prompt automaton for LLM-assisted research-software development, addressing hallucination accumulation and desynchronization through a contextual bandit-based prompt selector, half-life carry-forward of unfinished work, and audit mechanisms for code-theory-documentation alignment. It was evaluated on 46 repositories, with A3 achieving state-of-the-art static-analysis performance (F1=0.768 vs. 0.364 baseline).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Research-software projects require the mathematical thesis, executable system, benchmark surface, and public claims to mature together, yet they often drift apart. Two LM-specific failure modes are identified: (1) hallucination accumulation, where claims exceed what code or theory supports and propagate across sessions, and (2) desynchronization, where code, theory, or the model's world model fall out of alignment.

Method: Comet-H is implemented as an iterative prompt automaton that orchestrates ideation, implementation, evaluation, grounding, and paper-writing as coupled coordinates of a single workspace state. The controller selects the next prompt by scoring it against workspace deficits using a small contextual bandit formulation, with prompts as arms, workspace context as features, and a hand-weighted linear scorer. Unfinished work is carried forward with a half-life decay mechanism. The system re-checks paper and README against code/benchmarks whenever documentation changes. Evaluated on 46 research-software repositories, with in-depth analysis on A3, a Python static-analysis tool built entirely within the loop.

Result: A3, built entirely within the Comet-H loop, achieves F1=0.768 on a 90-case benchmark, compared to a next-best baseline of 0.364. Across ~400 commits, audit-and-contraction passes dominate the later phases of every successful trajectory.

Conclusion: Comet-H provides a principled framework for using LLMs in research-software development, with its transparent scoring mechanism and half-life mechanism offering a practical approach to maintaining alignment across code, theory, and documentation. The method demonstrates that structured, auditor-like interventions are essential for successful LLM-assisted research workflows.

Abstract: Large language models can now generate substantial code and draft research text, but research-software projects require more than either artifact alone. The mathematical thesis, executable system, benchmark surface, and public claims must mature together, yet often drift apart. We identify two LM-specific failure modes: hallucination accumulation, in which claims exceed what code or theory supports and unsupported assertions propagate across sessions; and desynchronization, in which code, theory, or the model's own world model fall out of alignment.
  We propose Comet-H, an iterative prompt automaton that orchestrates ideation, implementation, evaluation, grounding, and paper-writing as coupled coordinates of a single workspace state. At each step, a controller selects the next prompt by scoring it against what the workspace currently lacks, carries unfinished follow-up work forward with a half-life, and re-checks the paper and README against the code and benchmarks whenever documentation changes. We frame prompt selection as a small contextual bandit problem over prompt families, with prompts as arms, workspace deficits as context, and a hand-weighted linear score. This transparent scorer, paired with a fading record of unfinished work, bounds long-horizon follow-ups, requires no learned policy, and makes each prompt choice legible from the workspace.
  We created a portfolio of 46 research-software repositories across two dozen domains. We study A3 in depth, a Python static-analysis tool built entirely within the loop, which reaches (F1 = 0.768) on a 90-case benchmark, compared with a next-best baseline of 0.364. Across approximately 400 commits, we find that audit-and-contraction passes dominate the later phases of every successful trajectory.

</details>


### [56] [Self-Evolving Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27264)
*Marco Robol,Paolo Giorgini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种BDI-LLM架构，使自主智能体能够通过结合BDI推理与大语言模型实现真正的自主演化。通过在推理循环中嵌入自动化演化模块，智能体可从经验中提取新需求并同步更新设计及代码。实验表明该方案可行，但在行为继承和稳定性方面仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体虽能适应环境变化，但受限于设计时固定的需求、目标和能力，无法实现真正的软件演化。

Method: 提出BDI-LLM架构，在BDI推理循环中嵌入自动化演化模块，通过该模块从经验中提取新需求并同步生成设计变更和可执行代码更新。

Result: 原型系统在动态多智能体环境中验证了可行性：智能体能够从极少先验知识出发，自主发现新目标并生成可执行行为。

Conclusion: 实验证明了LLM驱动演化的可行性与当前局限性，重点在于行为继承和稳定性的挑战。

Abstract: Autonomous agents can adapt their behaviour to changing environments, but remain bound to requirements, goals, and capabilities fixed at design time, preventing genuine software evolution. This paper introduces self-evolving software agents, combining BDI reasoning with LLMs to enable autonomous evolution of goals, reasoning, and executable code. We propose a BDI-LLM architecture in which an automated evolution module operates alongside the agent's reasoning loop, eliciting new requirements from experience and synthesizing corresponding design and code updates. A prototype evaluated in a dynamic multi-agent environment shows that agents can autonomously discover new goals and generate executable behaviours from minimal prior knowledge. The results indicate both the feasibility and current limits of LLM-driven evolution, particularly in terms of behavioural inheritance and stability.

</details>


### [57] [Pragmos: A Process Agentic Modeling System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27311)
*Pedro-Aarón Hernández-Ávalos,Luciano García-Bañuelos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个混合式过程建模框架，将LLM与领域专门工具相结合，通过结构化的迭代对话工作流辅助人类用户共同创建过程模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程建模方法存在局限：纯黑盒方案无法有效应对过程建模的固有复杂性；LLM在推理复杂依赖关系方面存在能力限制。

Method: 将建模任务分解为更小、可管理的步骤，每步生成中间产物并明确记录建模决策的理由；利用领域专门工具辅助LLM处理复杂依赖关系，基于行为关系逐步构建模型。

Result: 通过混合方法生成既合理又可理解的模型，模型通过透明、可解释的步骤逐步演进。

Conclusion: 提出了Pragmos原型系统，展示了LLM与人类用户协作共同创建过程模型的可行性。

Abstract: The advent of Large Language Models (LLMs) has significantly transformed tasks across Software Engineering. In the context of Business Process Management, LLMs are now being explored as tools to derive process models directly from textual descriptions. Existing approaches range from chatbot-driven systems that assist with iterative, text-based modeling to fully automated end-to-end modeling assistants. However, we argue that process modeling is inherently complex and cannot be effectively addressed through black-box solutions. Instead, we envision modeling as an open-ended conversational activity, best supported by an interactive, iterative process involving both humans and LLM. In our approach, the modeling task is decomposed into smaller, manageable steps. Each step results in intermediate artifacts and explicitly documents the rationale behind each modeling decision. During this process, we incrementally uncover simple behavioral relations that guide the construction of the model. Given the current limitations of LLMs in reasoning about complex dependencies, we complement them with specialized tools developed in the field to structure process models based on behavioral relations. This hybrid approach enables the generation of sound, yet comprehensible models that evolve through transparent and explainable steps. In this paper, we present our research agenda and introduce Pragmos, a prototype system that operationalizes this vision. Pragmos demonstrates how LLMs can collaborate with human users as both domain and modeling experts to co-create evolving process models through a structured and explainable workflow.

</details>


### [58] [ScaleBox: Enabling High-Fidelity and Scalable Code Verification for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27467)
*Jiasheng Zheng,Xin Zheng,Boxi Cao,Pengbo Wang,Zhengzhao Ma,Qiming Zhu,Jiazhen Jiang,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ScaleBox是一个用于LLM代码训练的高保真、可扩展代码沙箱系统，通过自动化特种裁判处理、并行执行和多节点协调等创新技术，在LiveCodeBench基准测试中展现了优越的验证准确性和训练稳定性，优于传统启发式匹配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码沙箱系统在并发工作负载下无法提供准确的验证和高效的执行能力，无法满足大规模代码训练的需求。

Method: ScaleBox引入三项核心技术：(1) 自动化的特种裁判（special-judge）生成与管理；(2) 测试用例间的细粒度并行执行配合无缝多节点协调；(3) 配置驱动的评估套件实现可复现的基准测试。

Result: ScaleBox显著提升了代码验证的准确性和效率。RLVR实验表明，在LiveCodeBench上性能显著提升，训练稳定性增强，明显优于启发式匹配基线方法。

Conclusion: ScaleBox通过提供可靠、高吞吐量的基础设施，显著促进了大规模代码训练领域的研究与发展，优于传统的启发式匹配基线方法。

Abstract: Code sandboxes have emerged as a critical infrastructure for advancing the coding capabilities of large language models, providing verifiable feedback for both RL training and evaluation. However, existing systems fail to provide accurate verification and efficiency under high-concurrency workloads. We present ScaleBox, a high-fidelity and scalable system designed to address these limitations in large-scale code training. ScaleBox introduces automated special-judge generation and management, fine-grained parallel execution across test cases with seamless multi-node coordination, and a configuration-driven evaluation suite for reproducible benchmarking. A series of experiments demonstrates that ScaleBox significantly enhances code verification accuracy and efficiency. Our further RLVR experiments show that ScaleBox substantially improves both performance on LiveCodeBench and training stability, significantly outperforming heuristic-matching baselines. By providing a reliable and high-throughput infrastructure, ScaleBox facilitates more effective research and development in large-scale code training.

</details>


### [59] [A Longitudinal Analysis of Good First Issue Practices and Newcomer Pull Requests in Popular OSS Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27532)
*Hirotatsu Hoshikawa,Hidetake Tanaka,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析37个GitHub仓库四年间的数据，揭示了GFI标签从2024年起显著下降的趋势，但新手对GFI问题的兴趣保持稳定，导致新手PR合并率从61.9%降至42.2%，反映出GFI引导机制的供需失衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目依赖有效的新手引导机制维持社区发展，GFI标签作为通用的入门任务标记被广泛采用，但随着开发实践演进，这些引导机制的变化规律尚不明确，对维护者和研究者都具有重要价值。

Method: 对37个热门GitHub仓库（30个使用GFI标签）进行纵向分析，涵盖406,826个issues和1,117个新手GFI pull requests，时间跨度为2021年7月至2025年6月的四年数据，采用统计检验评估时间趋势变化。

Result: （1）GFI标签比例在前三年保持稳定，但自2024年1月起显著下降，且项目间差异较大，与仓库年龄或编程语言无关；（2）新手对GFI问题的参与度稳定在约27%；（3）新手GFI PR的合并率从61.9%下降至42.2%；（4）PR描述长度和代码规模等初始特征与合并结果无显著关联。

Conclusion: 研究表明GFI标签的可用性与新手参与度之间存在日益扩大的差距，需维护者同时维持GFI标签标注和代码审查支持。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) projects rely on effective newcomer onboarding to sustain their communities. OSS projects widely adopt "good first issue" (GFI) labels to highlight beginner-friendly tasks. As development practices continue to evolve, understanding how these onboarding mechanisms change over time is important for both maintainers and researchers. This study analyzes 406,826 issues and 1,117 newcomer GFI pull requests across 37 popular GitHub repositories (30 of which use GFI labels) over a four-year period from July 2021 to June 2025. We find that while the proportion of issues with GFI labels remained stable during the first three years, it underwent a statistically significant decline beginning in January 2024, with substantial variation across projects not explained by repository age or programming language. Despite this supply-side decline, newcomer engagement with GFI issues remains stable at approximately 27%, suggesting that GFI labels maintain consistent attractiveness. Examining the outcomes of this engagement, we find that the merge rate of newcomer GFI pull requests declined from 61.9% to 42.2%. Initial pull request characteristics such as description length and code size show no significant association with merge outcomes, indicating that success is not predicted by the quantitative characteristics of the initial submission alone. Together, these findings reveal a widening gap between stable newcomer interest in GFIs and the declining availability and success of GFI-based onboarding, underscoring the need for maintainers to sustain both GFI labeling and review support.

</details>


### [60] [GenAI in Software Engineering: The Role of Technology Acceptance Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27642)
*Oscar Johansson,Jürgen Börstler,Nauman bin Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This review uses UTAUT as a lens to examine GenAI acceptance in software engineering, identifying three key research priorities: refining acceptance constructs for GenAI's unique nature, improving measurement practices, and adopting Bayesian analysis approaches. The paper argues UTAUT is a viable framework but suggests complementary theories may be needed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Organizations want to adopt GenAI in software development, but technology acceptance models like UTAUT remain underutilized in SE research. There's a need to understand barriers to GenAI acceptance at the individual level to facilitate adoption.

Method: Literature review of foundational UTAUT research, SE-specific applications, and emerging GenAI studies in SE. Synthesizes existing knowledge to identify research gaps and opportunities.

Result: Three research priorities: (1) refining UTAUT constructs to capture GenAI's unique and transformational characteristics; (2) improving operationalization for better construct validity and cross-study comparability; (3) incorporating Bayesian methods for small-sample inference with prior knowledge integration and scenario simulation.

Conclusion: UTAUT combined with Bayesian analysis is suitable for understanding individual-level barriers to GenAI adoption in SE, though additional theoretical frameworks should be explored.

Abstract: Context: Many organizations are keen to incorporate generative~AI (GenAI) into their software development processes. Technology acceptance models, such as the Unified Theory of Acceptance and Use of Technology (UTAUT), are traditionally used to identify individual-level barriers to the acceptance of new technologies and can facilitate the transition to GenAI. However, UTAUT has seen limited use within software engineering (SE) research. Objective: Using UTAUT as an example, to identify key areas for future research on GenAI acceptance, including the role of Bayesian approaches for data analysis. Method: We review foundational and SE-specific literature on UTAUT and analyze its emerging applications for GenAI in SE. Results: We identify three priorities for future research: (1) identifying and refining constructs to account for GenAI's nature and transformational impact; (2) improving operationalization practices to strengthen construct validity and cross-study comparability; and (3) incorporating Bayesian analysis to support small-sample inference by integrating prior knowledge, iterative model updating, and simulation of scenarios. Conclusion: UTAUT is a suitable candidate to combine with Bayesian analysis for practical insights on individual-level barriers to GenAI use in SE, but additional theories should be considered.

</details>


### [61] [Tail-aware N-version Machine Learning Models for Reliable API Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27647)
*Aoi Matsuda,Fumio Machida,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出N版本API推荐系统NvRec，通过利用多个不同版本的ML模型过滤尾部不可靠输出，提升API序列推荐的可靠性。在Java项目数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够有效平衡推荐准确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码数据集用于训练ML模型时呈现长尾分布，导致API推荐不可靠，特别是对于尾部使用频率较低的API方法。

Method: NvRec利用一组可用的ML模型并分析它们在各个API方法上的性能及其尾部属性，在推理时使用生成的模型配置文件过滤掉不可靠的API推荐并确定最终输出。

Result: 三版本NvRec中CodeT5、MulaRec和UniXcoder组合达到最高真实接受率83.8%、拒绝率80.7%；五版本配置达到83.1%的真实接受率、69.0%的拒绝率。

Conclusion: 五版本配置在真实接受率和拒绝率之间取得了更好的平衡，在限制仅高度可靠候选者的多数投票下，CodeT5、MulaRec和UniXcoder组合实现了83.8%的最高真实接受率和80.7%的拒绝率。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML)-based API recommendation helps developers efficiently identify suitable APIs to complement the application code. However, code datasets used to train ML models often exhibit a long-tail distribution, leading to unreliable API recommendations, especially for infrequently used API methods at the tail of the distribution. To address this issue, we propose N-version API Recommendation (NvRec), which leverages N different versions of ML models to enhance the reliability of API sequence recommendations by suppressing unreliable outputs entailing tail APIs. NvRec leverages a set of available ML models and profiles their performance on individual API methods with their tail properties. The generated model profile is used at inference time to filter out unreliable API recommendations and determine the final output. We implement NvRec using five API recommendation models, including CodeBERT, CodeT5, MulaRec, UniXcoder, and CodeT5+, and evaluate it on a public benchmark dataset constructed from compilable Java projects. For the three-version NvRec, we find that the combination of CodeT5, MulaRec, and UniXcoder achieves the highest true accept rate of 83.8%, with a rejection rate of 80.7%, when majority voting is restricted to highly reliable candidates. In contrast, the five-version configuration achieves its highest true accept rate of 83.1% with simple majority voting, while reducing the rejection rate to 69.0%. Overall, the five-version configuration offers a better balance between true accept rate and rejection rate.

</details>


### [62] [PuzzleMark: Implicit Jigsaw Learning for Robust Code Dataset Watermarking in Neural Code Completion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27677)
*Haocheng Huang,Yuchen Chen,Weisong Sun,Peizhuo Lv,Yuan Xiao,Chunrong Fang,Yang Liu,Xiaofang Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PuzzleMark is a robust watermarking method for protecting code dataset intellectual property through code complexity-based carrier selection, variable name concatenation patterns, and Fisher's exact test verification, achieving 100% verification success rate while maintaining high imperceptibility and resistance to detection attacks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: High-quality code datasets require significant resources but face severe unauthorized use risks. Existing digital watermarking methods rely on co-occurrence patterns, which are not robust and susceptible to detection and removal attacks.

Method: PuzzleMark proposes: (1) A carrier selection strategy using code complexity metrics to identify suitable watermark carriers; (2) A novel concatenation pattern replacing traditional co-occurrence patterns for enhanced robustness; (3) Two watermarking strategies via variable name concatenation; (4) Adaptive watermark embedding based on code characteristics; (5) Fisher's exact test for black-box watermark verification.

Result: PuzzleMark achieves 100% verification success rate and 0% false positive rate with negligible impact on model performance. It demonstrates strong imperceptibility with average suspicious rate ≤ 0.24 and average recall ≤ 30.41% against four state-of-the-art watermark detection methods.

Conclusion: PuzzleMark provides strong intellectual property protection for code datasets while maintaining high imperceptibility. It achieves 100% verification success rate, 0% false positive rate, negligible model performance impact, and demonstrates strong resistance to detection attacks with average recall ≤ 30.41%. The method offers new insights for future digital watermarking research.

Abstract: Constructing and curating high-quality code datasets requires significant resources, making them valuable intellectual property. Unfortunately, these datasets currently face severe risks of unauthorized use. Although digital watermarking offers a post hoc mechanism for copyright authentication, existing methods are predominantly based on the co-occurrence pattern, which is not robust and is susceptible to watermark detection and removal attacks. In this paper, we propose PuzzleMark, a robust watermarking method for code datasets. To reduce the risk of watermark exposure, PuzzleMark introduces a carrier selection strategy that leverages code complexity to evaluate the suitability of code snippets as watermark carriers, and selects those with high suitability for watermarking. To enhance the robustness of the watermark, PuzzleMark proposes a novel concatenation pattern to replace the traditional co-occurrence pattern, and implements two watermarking strategies through variable name concatenation. PuzzleMark adaptively embeds watermarks based on the inherent characteristics of the code, making it more stealthy while maintaining design simplicity. For watermark verification, PuzzleMark employs Fisher's exact test to verify suspicious models under a black-box setting. Experimental results demonstrate that PuzzleMark achieves a 100% verification success rate and a 0% false positive rate, with negligible impact on model performance. Both our human study and our evaluation using four state-of-the-art watermark detection methods show that PuzzleMark exhibits strong imperceptibility, with an average suspicious rate $\leq$ 0.24 and an average recall $\leq$ 30.41%, respectively. As a practical digital watermarking method, PuzzleMark provides strong protection for the intellectual property of code datasets and offers new insights for future research.

</details>


### [63] [Understanding Bugs in Template Engine-Based Applications: Symptoms, Root Causes, and Fix Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27692)
*Kai Gao,Yu Sun,Chang-ai Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对模板引擎应用（TE应用）bug进行了全面系统研究。通过分析跨15种模板引擎、5种编程语言的1,004个bug案例，识别出异常渲染是最常见症状（48.61%），语法误用、数据上下文不匹配和不兼容集成是三大主要根因，约67%的bug可在模板内修复，但超过20%需修改宿主端逻辑。研究为工具设计者、开发者和研究人员提供了基于实证的可行建议，并基于发现开发了Jinja引擎的原型调试工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板引擎是现代软件开发的重要组成部分，广泛应用于Web开发、基础设施即代码（IaC）和数据工程等领域。TE应用具有多语言组合、数据流不透明、延迟验证和复杂集成等独特架构特征，这些特征使得TE应用的bug诊断和修复面临重大挑战。尽管先前研究主要关注模板引擎安全性，但TE应用中的bug问题尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 研究团队收集并分析了1,004个跨15种模板引擎（涉及5种编程语言）的应用bug案例，通过系统化分类识别了TE应用bug的症状表现、根因模式和修复策略。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 症状分布：异常渲染结果（如意外输出或空白输出）最为普遍，占48.61%，常表现为难以诊断的静默失败；(2) 根因识别：共识别出17类根因，Syntax Misuse（语法误用）、Mismatched Data Context（数据上下文不匹配）和Incompatible Integration（不兼容集成）为三大主导类别；(3) 修复模式：67.92%的bug在模板内部即可修复，但超过20%需要修改宿主端逻辑以解决数据上下文问题。

Conclusion: 研究团队基于发现开发了针对Jinja引擎的两个原型工具，以辅助TE应用的开发和调试工作。同时为工具设计者、开发者和研究人员提供了可操作的建议。研究揭示了TE应用特有的挑战，包括静默失败、多语言组合、数据流不透明等问题，强调需要在工具层面和开发者实践层面共同改进。

Abstract: Template engines are indispensable components in modern software ecosystems, enabling the generation of structured documents and scripts across domains such as web development, Infrastructure as Code, and data engineering. However, the unique architectural characteristics of template engine-based applications (i.e., TE applications), including multi-language composition, opaque data flow, deferred validation, and complex integration, pose significant challenges for diagnosing and resolving bugs in TE applications. While prior research has primarily focused on template engine security, bugs in TE applications remain under-investigated. To bridge this gap, we present the first comprehensive study of TE application bugs. By analyzing 1,004 application bugs across 15 template engines in five programming languages, we identify the symptoms and root causes of TE application bugs and common patterns to fix them. Our findings reveal that Abnormal Rendering Result (e.g., unexpected or blank output) is the most prevalent symptom (48.61%), often manifesting as silent failures that are difficult to diagnose. We identify 17 root causes, with Syntax Misuse, Mismatched Data Context, and Incompatible Integration as the dominant categories. Furthermore, we find that while 67.92% of the bugs are fixed within the template, over 20% require modifications in the host-side logic to resolve data context issues. Based on these findings, we derive actionable implications for tool designers, practitioners, and researchers. To demonstrate the practical utility of our findings, we further develop two prototype tools for the Jinja engine to facilitate the development and debugging of TE applications.

</details>


### [64] [LLM-as-a-Judge for Human-AI Co-Creation: A Reliability-Aware Evaluation Framework for Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27727)
*Md Faizul Ibne Amin,Yutaka Watanobe,Daniel M. Muepu,Haruto Suzuki,Kenta Nanaumi,Md Mostafizer Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于评分标准的LLM评判框架，用于竞赛式人机协同编程评估，通过多指标协议和轨迹分析验证评判可靠性与协同效果。研究发现协同成功集中于早期阶段，但评判者间一致性仍有待提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM既作为开放性输出的评判工具，也作为AI辅助编程的协作伙伴，但在人机协同创作场景下的严格评估方法仍然缺乏——评判需具备可靠性、跨模型可比性及多轮交互可解释性。

Method: 构建了基于schema约束的评判输出框架，包含验证修复机制、按用户和问题分组以防止轨迹泄露、多指标协议评估（ROC-AUC、PR-AUC、MCC、LogLoss、Brier score、ECE、Cohen's k、Fleiss' k）以及轨迹级信号分析（轮次置信度、Success-at-Turn、time-to-success、revision churn、CodeBLEU）。

Result: 评判侧最佳指标：ROC-AUC=0.5937, PR-AUC=0.6904, MCC=0.5000, inter-judge一致性较低（Cohen's k=0.1592, Fleiss' k=0.0696）。协同创作成功集中于早期，首轮Success-at-Turn达0.8533，第6轮稳定于0.8641，修订行为呈异质性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了可审计、可复现的评估方法，将可靠性感知的LLM评判与轨迹分析相结合，为AI辅助编程和软件工程评估提供了实践模板。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly employed both as judges for evaluating open-ended outputs and as co-creation partners in AI-assisted programming; yet rigorous evaluation in human-AI co-creation settings remains underdeveloped as judgments must be reliable, comparable across models, and interpretable over multi-turn interaction. To address this gap, a rubric-driven LLM-as-a-Judge framework is presented for contest-style human-AI co-creation in coding and software engineering (SE). The framework is built around schema-constrained judge outputs, validation and repair mechanisms, grouped and split by user and problem to prevent trajectory leakage, and participant-level NONBLIND context. Multiple LLM judges are assessed through a multi-metric protocol covering discrimination (ROC-AUC, PR-AUC), thresholded decision quality (MCC), probabilistic reliability (LogLoss, Brier score, ECE), and inter-judge agreement (Cohen's and Fleiss' k). Human-AI co-creation is further examined through trajectory-level signals, including turn-wise confidence, Success-at-Turn, time-to-success, revision churn, and CodeBLEU. Co-creation success is found to concentrate early, with Success-at-Turn rising to 0.8533 at the first observed turn and stabilizing at 0.8641 by turn 6. Revision behavior, however, remains heterogeneous, suggesting that productive progress can emerge through either incremental refinement or broader restructuring. On the judging side, the best held-out scores reach 0.5937 for ROC-AUC, 0.6904 for PR-AUC, and 0.5000 for MCC test, while inter-judge consistency remains modest overall (mean pairwise Cohen's k = 0.1592, Fleiss' k = 0.0696). Taken together, this work offers an auditable and reproducible evaluation methodology that links reliability-aware LLM judging with trajectory-based analysis of human-AI co-creation, providing a practical evaluation template for future AI-assisted coding and SE.

</details>


### [65] [Multifaceted Hero Developers and Bug-Fixing Outcomes Across Severity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27754)
*Amit Kumar,Mahen Gandhi,Meher Bhardwaj,Hrishikesh Ethari,Sonali Agarwal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对77个Apache项目进行分析，发现英雄开发者的识别高度依赖所采用的贡献指标——技术指标与社交指标的交集很小，且两类英雄之间存在明显的跨维度不对称性，强调了多维度识别关键贡献者的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目常依赖少量高活跃度贡献者（英雄开发者），但现有研究对英雄开发者的界定高度依赖所选的贡献指标。代码指标与讨论指标捕捉的是贡献的不同面向，需要系统性地比较多种指标。

Method: 对77个Apache软件基金会项目进行测量敏感型研究，采用三种技术指标（提交数、涉及文件数、代码变更量）和两种社交指标（问题评论数、评论的问题数），分析英雄 prevalence、英雄集合重叠度，以及按严重程度划分的修复率和重新打开率。

Result: 英雄项目在所有指标下普遍存在，但被识别的英雄差异显著。技术英雄与社会英雄的Jaccard重叠仅为0.10；71.4%的技术英雄有强社交活动，但仅24.2%的社交英雄展现强技术活动；不同严重程度级别和结果指标下，英雄类别排名存在差异。

Conclusion: 英雄开发者的身份不是单一且指标无关的角色。多维度视角能更可靠地识别关键贡献者，并为开发者优先级排序和严重程度感知的缺陷分配提供更好支持。

Abstract: Open-source projects often rely on a small group of highly active contributors known as hero developers. Prior work shows that hero developers are common in many OSS and enterprise projects, yet who qualifies as a hero depends heavily on the chosen contribution metric. Code-based metrics identify implementation-focused developers, whereas discussion-based metrics highlight coordination and communication; these metrics capture distinct facets of contribution. We conducted a measurement-sensitive study of multifaceted heroism across 77 Apache Software Foundation projects using three technical measures (commit count, distinct files touched, churn) and two social measures (issue-comment count, number of distinct issues commented on). We examined hero prevalence, overlap among hero sets, and severity-wise bug-fixing outcomes via fix and reopen rates. Results show that hero projects are common under all measures, but identified heroes differ substantially across facets. The pooled Jaccard overlap between technical and social hero sets is only 0.10. Cross-facet asymmetry is evident: 71.4% of technical heroes exhibit strong social activity, while only 24.2% of social heroes show strong technical activity. Fix-rate and reopen-rate differences are modest, yet hero-category rankings vary across severity levels and outcome measures. These findings indicate that heroism is not a single, metric-independent role. A multifaceted perspective offers a more reliable understanding of key contributors and better supports developer prioritisation and severity-aware bug assignment.

</details>


### [66] [The Grand Software Supply Chain of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27781)
*Carmine Cesarano,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次将AI供应链作为核心分析对象，将其分解为四层架构并识别出可验证性、版本控制、可观察性和可追溯性四个关键结构性差距，通过对48个开源项目的实证研究揭示了现代AI系统供应链的庞大规模和潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统建立在软件完整性机制较弱的基础上，导致从数据获取到推理的每个阶段都暴露于安全风险中，需要将AI供应链作为核心分析对象。

Method: 将AI供应链分解为四个架构层（数据获取、模型训练、模型推理和跨切面基质），并在此框架下识别四个结构性差距：可验证性、版本控制、可观察性和可追溯性。

Result: 现有AI系统在这四个维度上均存在不足：存在无法被解析器强制执行的未声明行为耦合；无法回滚到已知可工作的组件状态；会静默降级而非显式暴露破坏性变更；系统的血缘关系难以追溯。

Conclusion: 通过对48个生产级开源项目的测量，发现AI软件供应链包含4664个直接依赖、11508个传递包和约3.92亿行代码，揭示了当前AI系统面临的严峻安全挑战。

Abstract: AI systems rest on software with low integrity mechanisms, leaving AI systems exposed across every stage from data acquisition to final inference. This paper makes the AI supply chain a first-class object of analysis, decomposing it across four architectural layers: data acquisition, model training, model inference, and a cross-cutting substrate. Within these layers, we identify four structural gaps that traditional supply chain mechanisms do not address: verifiability, versioning, observability, and traceability.Current AI systems fall short on all of them: they carry undeclared behavioral couplings that no resolver enforces; they cannot be reverted back to known working assemblies; they degrade silently rather than surfacing breaking changes; and their lineage can hardly be approximated. To illustrate the scale of the software supply chain of AI, we measure a reference stack of 48 production-grade open-source projects, which declares 4,664 direct dependencies, resolves to 11,508 transitive packages, and totals roughly 392M lines of code.

</details>


### [67] [Test Before You Deploy: Governing Updates in the LLM Supply Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27789)
*Mohd Sameen Chishti,Damilare Peter Oyinloye,Jingyue Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM更新管理的部署侧治理框架，包含生产契约、风险类别测试套件和兼容性门禁三个核心组件。探索性验证表明针对性测试可发现整体指标遗漏的回归，并将LLM更新管理定位为软件供应链治理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务通过提供商端更新持续演进，但未明确版本变更。这种静默更新会引入行为漂移，导致功能、格式、安全约束等方面的回归问题。现有方法主要关注回归测试或版本控制，缺乏部署方对不透明模型演进的兼容性治理机制。

Method: 提出了一个基于三组件的部署侧治理框架：(1) 生产契约（明确模型允许行为规则），(2) 风险类别测试套件（按部署风险组织的有针对性测试），(3) 兼容性门禁（阻止不满足安全和性能标准的更新）。通过多个LLM版本的探索性验证进行验证。

Result: 验证表明，针对特定风险领域的有针对性测试能够发现整体指标遗漏的性能回归问题。

Conclusion: LLM更新管理应被视为软件供应链治理问题。研究议程包括：系统化构建有效测试套件、在非确定性系统中设置可靠性能阈值、以及在提供商透明度有限的情况下检测和解释模型漂移。该框架为部署方提供了实用的兼容性控制机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as core dependencies in software systems. However, the hosted LLM services evolve continuously through provider-side updates without explicit version changes. These silent updates can introduce behavioral drift, causing regressions in functionality, formatting, safety constraints, or other application-specific requirements. Existing approaches focus primarily on regression testing or versioning but do not provide deployer-side mechanisms for governing compatibility during opaque model evolution. This paper proposes a deployment-side governance framework based on three components: clearly defined rules for how the model is allowed to behave (production contracts), focused testing organized by deployment risk categories (risk-category-based testing suite), and release checkpoints that block updates unless they meet defined safety and performance standards (compatibility gates). Through exploratory validation across multiple LLM versions, we provide evidence that targeted testing in specific risk areas can uncover performance regressions that overall metrics miss. We also identify several open research challenges, including how to systematically build effective test suites, how to set reliable performance thresholds in non-deterministic systems, and how to detect and explain model drift when providers offer limited transparency. Overall, we frame LLM update management as a software supply chain governance problem and outline a research agenda for putting deployer-side compatibility controls into practice.

</details>


### [68] [Feature-Centric Methodology for Analyzing Cross-Chain NFT Migration Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27805)
*Mohd Sameen Chishti,Damilare Peter Oyinloye,Jingyue Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于四层NFT架构和四阶段迁移分析方法的跨链NFT迁移兼容性评估框架，能够系统性地预测NFT功能在迁移后的保留、重设计或完全不兼容状态，并通过以太坊到Solana的案例验证了方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cross-chain NFT migration faces critical challenges due to architectural divergences among blockchain platforms. Existing protocol-level mechanisms cannot resolve deeper compatibility issues arising from fundamental differences in state organization, transaction execution, and ownership representation.

Method: Derives a four-layer NFT architecture (cryptographic, state-management, transaction-processing, ownership primitives) with upward dependencies; conceptualizes NFT as feature bundle; proposes four-phase migration analysis: feature specification, primitive dependency mapping, target profiling, compatibility assessment.

Result: Identified several incompatibility issues between Ethereum and Solana that hinder seamless NFT migration.

Conclusion: The four-phase methodology enables systematic prediction of feature compatibility before migration attempts, avoiding costly failed migrations.

Abstract: Cross-chain NFT migration refers to the process of transferring digital assets along with their associated functionalities and guarantees between distinct blockchain platforms. However, architectural divergences among these platforms introduce critical challenges, often resulting in features that fail to behave as intended. While protocol-level mechanisms can coordinate data transfer, they are insufficient to resolve deeper compatibility issues arising from fundamental differences in state organization, transaction execution, and ownership representation. Thus, the critical challenge lies in predicting which NFT features can be preserved, which require redesign, and which are fundamentally incompatible, prior to undertaking costly migration attempts. To address this challenge, we first derive a tailored four-layer NFT architecture based on standard blockchain stacks, distinguishing cryptographic, state-management, transaction-processing, and ownership primitives, with explicit upward dependencies. Building on this architecture, we conceptualize an NFT as a bundle of features and define successful cross-chain NFT migration as the preservation of these features. Grounded in this model, we propose a four-phase migration analysis methodology comprising source feature specification, primitive-level dependency mapping, target platform profiling, and compatibility assessment, which classifies each feature as natively preserved, partially mismatched, or completely mismatched. We evaluate this methodology through a proof-of-concept analysis of Ethereum-to-Solana NFT migration, identifying several incompatibility issues that hinder seamless NFT migration.

</details>


### [69] [An Empirical Evaluation of Code Smell Detection in Angular Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27893)
*Maykon Nunes,Emanuel Coutinho,Carla Bezerra,Ivan Machado*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过灰色文献综述首次系统整理了Angular特定代码气味的目录，识别出11种气味（6种跨框架），并开发了自动化检测工具，验证其高检测性能（准确率>0.88，F1: 0.89-1.00），为前端代码质量改进提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Angular项目规模扩大，开发者面临架构管理和代码模块化的挑战，导致代码气味出现。虽然React的代码气味已有研究，但Angular特定的气味及其表现形式缺乏系统认知。

Method: 通过灰色文献综述收集社区知识和技术讨论，识别并归类了11种Angular代码气味；实现了自动化静态分析工具，并使用人工验证的数据集和标准信息检索指标进行实证评估。

Result: 识别出11种Angular代码气味，其中6种与React共有；自动化检测工具表现优异，准确率超过0.88，F1分数达0.89-1.00；常见问题包括组件过度加载、逻辑重复和低效模板绑定。

Conclusion: 本研究首次从灰色文献中提取了Angular特定代码气味的目录，证明了自动化检测的可行性和有效性，为未来改善前端代码质量的实证研究和工具开发奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Angular is one of the most widely adopted frameworks for developing large-scale, dynamic web applications. As projects increase in scope and complexity, developers face growing challenges in managing architecture and maintaining clean, modular code. These challenges often lead to design flaws, commonly referred to as code smells. While React-specific smells have been cataloged in prior studies, limited knowledge exists regarding Angular-specific smells and how they manifest. This study investigates Angular code smells through a grey literature review, consolidating community knowledge and technical discussions. From the collected sources, 11 distinct Angular code smells were identified, 6 of which also occur in React-based systems, suggesting that some issues are cross-framework. Each smell was analyzed, exemplified, and grouped according to its technical characteristics. Based on the resulting catalog, we implemented an automated static analysis tool to detect Angular code smells. The tool was empirically evaluated using a manually validated dataset, and its effectiveness was assessed through standard information retrieval metrics. The evaluation results indicate high detection performance across all smells, achieving accuracy values above 0.88 and F1-scores ranging from 0.89 to 1.00. The findings reveal recurring issues such as component overloading, duplicated logic, and inefficient template bindings, reinforcing the relevance of systematic detection support. This study presents the first catalog of Angular-specific code smells derived from grey literature and demonstrates the feasibility and effectiveness of automated detection, providing a solid foundation for future empirical studies and tool development aimed at improving front-end code quality.

</details>


### [70] [From Mirage to Grounding: Towards Reliable Multimodal Circuit-to-Verilog Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27969)
*Guang Yang,Xing Hu,Xiang Chen,Xin Xi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文揭示了多模态大语言模型在电路图转RTL代码任务中的"幻影"现象：模型通过标识符语义而非视觉理解来生成代码，导致用空白图替换电路图后准确率不降反升。研究者提出C2VEVAL基准和VeriGround (4B)模型，通过匿名化训练和D-ORPO偏好对齐实现了真正的视觉接地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将电路图翻译成RTL代码是视觉到代码生成的极端可靠性测试。当前多模态大语言模型在处理电路图时可能依赖标识符语义而非真正的视觉理解，这种高度隐蔽的缺陷会直接损害AI辅助代码生成的可信度。

Method: 1) 构造C2VEVAL基准测试，采用Normal/Anony配对协议（Anony模式对图表和模块头部的所有标识符进行匿名化）；2) 提出VeriGround (4B)模型，采用标识符匿名化训练、拒绝增强和D-ORPO（决策导向ORPO）偏好对齐方法，上调生成-拒绝关键token的权重。

Result: C2VEVAL评估显示所有模型在Anony模式下分数急剧下降，证实Normal模式的高准确率很大程度上是"幻影"。VeriGround实现了Normal/Anony模式下46.11%/42.51%的Functional Pass@1，虚假拒绝率仅1.20%/0.00%，对空白图像保持>92%拒绝率。

Conclusion: VeriGround通过匿名化训练、拒绝增强和D-ORPO偏好对齐，在4B参数规模下实现了真正的视觉接地，在Normal模式下与GPT-5.4性能相当，在Anony模式下显著优于所有基线模型，且对空白图像保持>92%的拒绝率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to translate visual artifacts into code, from UI mockups into HTML to scientific plots into Python scripts. A circuit diagram can be viewed as a visual domain-specific language for hardware: it encodes timing, topology, and bit level semantics that are invisible to casual inspection yet safety critical once fabricated in silicon. Translating such diagrams into register-transfer-level(RTL) code therefore represents an extreme reliability test for vision-to-code generation. We reveal a phenomenon we call Mirage: replacing a circuit diagram with a blank image leaves Pass@k unchanged or even higher, because models bypass the visual input and instead exploit identifier semantics in the module header to retrieve canonical RTL templates. This constitutes a new, highly covert class of defect in AI-assisted code generation that directly undermines MLLMs' trustworthiness. To quantify the effect, we construct C2VEVAL and evaluate eight MLLMs under a paired Normal/Anony protocol in which Anony mode anonymizes all identifiers in both the diagram and the module header; Anony-mode scores drop sharply across all models, confirming that high Normal-mode accuracy is largely a Mirage. We then propose VeriGround (4B), trained with identifier anonymization, refusal augmentation, and D-ORPO (Decision-Focused ORPO) preference alignment that up-weights pivotal generate-or-refuse tokens. VeriGround achieves Functional Pass@1 of 46.11%/42.51%(Normal/Anony) with a False Refusal Rate of only 1.20%/0.00%, while maintaining >92% Refusal Rate on blank images. With only 4B parameters, VeriGround performs on par with GPT-5.4 under Normal and significantly outperforms all baselines under Anony, confirming genuine visual grounding.

</details>


### [71] [Beyond Code, We Are People: A Systematic Mapping of 25 Years of Literature on Soft Skills in Agile Development Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28101)
*Israely Lima,Lucas Moura Lourenço,Márcio Ribeiro,Ivan Machado,Carla Ilane Bezerra*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统文献映射法分析97篇论文，发现沟通、适应性、团队协作和领导力是软件工程中最关键的软技能，且与敏捷角色密切相关，但角色特异性软技能研究仍存在显著空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件开发是社会技术活动，人类因素直接影响质量、生产力和创新能力。软技能对团队效能和项目成功至关重要，但在敏捷方法盛行的过去25年里，缺乏对相关软技能的系统性梳理研究，限制了人因与技术要素的整合。

Method: 系统文献映射法（Systematic Mapping Study），检索2000年1月至2025年5月发表的97篇研究，涵盖主要科学数据库。

Result: 识别出反复出现的核心软技能：沟通能力、适应性、团队协作和领导力，并发现其与敏捷情境中不同角色的关联模式。主要敏捷方法（尤其是Scrum）也被识别出来。

Conclusion: 研究识别了关键软技能差距，特别是缺乏针对特定角色的软技能研究。成果可为研究人员、教育者和实践者提供参考，支持课程设计、培训策略和组织实践的优化，强化软件开发中社会与技术的融合。

Abstract: Software development is a sociotechnical and human-centered endeavor in which human factors directly influence quality, productivity, and innovation capacity. In this context, career development in computing goes beyond technical mastery, requiring competencies that enable professionals to deal with continuous change and collaborative demands. Among these, non-technical skills (soft skills) stand out, encompassing social, emotional, and communicational dimensions essential to team effectiveness and the success of software projects. Despite their recognized importance, there is still a need for a systematic mapping of the most relevant soft skills over the past 25 years, a period marked by the adoption of agile approaches in industry. This gap limits the integration of human and technical aspects in software development. This study presents a systematic mapping of the literature, analyzing 97 studies published between January 2000 and May 2025 across major scientific databases. The results identify recurring competencies such as communication, adaptability, teamwork, and leadership, as well as their association with different roles in agile contexts. The main agile approaches adopted, particularly Scrum, are also identified, along with key gaps in the literature, such as the lack of studies on role specific soft skills. The findings can support researchers, educators, and practitioners in designing curricula, training strategies, and organizational practices aligned with human factors, reinforcing the importance of integrating social and technical dimensions in the development of collaborative and innovative professionals.

</details>


### [72] [Claw-Eval-Live: A Live Agent Benchmark for Evolving Real-World Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28139)
*Chenxin Li,Zhengyang Tang,Huangxin Lin,Yunlong Lin,Shijue Huang,Shengyuan Liu,Bowen Ye,Rang Li,Lei Li,Benyou Wang,Yixuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Claw-Eval-Live，一个用于工作流智能体的动态基准测试，通过将公共需求信号驱动的信号层与可复现的发布快照分离，实现持续评估。评测105个任务和13个前沿模型后发现，当前工作流自动化可靠性仍远未解决，领先模型通过率仅66.7%，且排行榜排名不足以反映真实能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试在发布时冻结任务集，主要对最终响应评分，难以根据演变的工作流需求评估智能体，也难以验证任务是否被正确执行。

Method: 将可刷新的信号层（从公共工作流需求信号更新）与可复现的时间戳发布快照分离；使用ClawHub Top-500技能构建任务；记录执行轨迹、审计日志、服务状态和运行后工作区工件；采用确定性检查与结构化LLM评判相结合的方式进行评估。

Result: 领先模型仅通过66.7%的任务，无模型达到70%；HR、管理和多系统业务工作流是持续瓶颈；本地工作区修复相对更容易但尚未饱和；相似通过率可能掩盖整体完成度的差异。

Conclusion: 工作流智能体评估应该进行双重落地：新鲜的外部需求信号，以及可验证的智能体行动。

Abstract: LLM agents are expected to complete end-to-end units of work across software tools, business services, and local workspaces. Yet many agent benchmarks freeze a curated task set at release time and grade mainly the final response, making it difficult to evaluate agents against evolving workflow demand or verify whether a task was executed. We introduce Claw-Eval-Live, a live benchmark for workflow agents that separates a refreshable signal layer, updated across releases from public workflow-demand signals, from a reproducible, time-stamped release snapshot. Each release is constructed from public workflow-demand signals, with ClawHub Top-500 skills used in the current release, and materialized as controlled tasks with fixed fixtures, services, workspaces, and graders. For grading, Claw-Eval-Live records execution traces, audit logs, service state, and post-run workspace artifacts, using deterministic checks when evidence is sufficient and structured LLM judging only for semantic dimensions. The release contains 105 tasks spanning controlled business services and local workspace repair, and evaluates 13 frontier models under a shared public pass rule. Experiments reveal that reliable workflow automation remains far from solved: the leading model passes only 66.7% of tasks and no model reaches 70%. Failures are structured by task family and execution surface, with HR, management, and multi-system business workflows as persistent bottlenecks and local workspace repair comparatively easier but unsaturated. Leaderboard rank alone is insufficient because models with similar pass rates can diverge in overall completion, and task-level discrimination concentrates in a middle band of tasks. Claw-Eval-Live suggests that workflow-agent evaluation should be grounded twice, in fresh external demand and in verifiable agent action.

</details>


### [73] [Unsafe and Unused? A History of Utility Code in Mature Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28146)
*Brandon Keller,Kaitlin Yandik,Angela Ngo,Andy Meneely*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对7个大型开源项目的纵向分析，发现util文件（通常用于存放通用工具代码）实际上比普通文件更容易包含安全漏洞，风险高出约2.75倍。研究建议开发者应重新审视util命名约定的使用，避免创建名义上通用但实际未被复用且存在安全隐患的工具文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件开发中，util命名约定被广泛使用以表示代码的通用性和可复用性。然而，论文探究了一个关键问题：这些util代码在实际项目中究竟会如何发展？是否存在滥用util命名的情况，导致util文件变得不安全或未被实际使用？研究的核心目标是帮助开发者避免创建不安全且未被使用的util文件。

Method: 采用纵向挖掘方法，对7个开源项目（Linux kernel、Django、FFmpeg、httpd、Struts、systemd、Tomcat）的Git仓库进行分析。研究以30天为间隔对整个项目历史进行快照，共计1773个快照，覆盖147个项目年。同时在每个快照点进行重命名跟踪，以追踪util文件在整个代码库生命周期中的变化。

Result: 研究发现util文件涉及漏洞的可能性最高可达非util文件的2.75倍。此外，分析揭示了util使用率、复杂度、开发者协作与安全性之间存在潜在关联。研究还追踪了util文件在整个生命周期中的重命名情况，发现这些文件在项目中具有较长的存活期。

Conclusion: 尽管util命名约定旨在促进代码复用和减少工作量，但研究揭示util文件实际上更容易涉及安全漏洞（风险是非util文件的2.75倍）。这一发现表明，当前的util使用实践可能适得其反，研究的目标是帮助开发者避免创建不安全且未被使用的util文件。此外，util文件的普遍性和长期存在性为理解软件开发的socio-technical特性提供了有价值的视角。

Abstract: Filenames are a concise means of conveying information about source code to fellow developers. One such convention is util. Commonly understood to stand for "utility", filenames with the letters util are often an indication that the file contains code that may be broadly useful or reusable. Some projects use this convention heavily, for example, the Apache Tomcat server contains 925 files with util in the path name, which is 17.9% of all source code files in the tree. While the intent of the name may be to prevent duplicate code and reduce workload, what actually happens to util code over time? Do projects move away from util code as they mature? Are util files being used by fellow colleagues, or maintained and used by their author? The goal of our work is to help developers avoid creating unsafe and unused util files when developing their projects. We conducted a longitudinal mining study of the Git repositories of seven open source projects that have a long development history (Linux kernel, Django, FFmpeg, httpd, Struts, systemd, Tomcat). We analyzed how util usage, complexity, developer collaboration, and security are potentially correlated within these projects. Our longitudinal analysis was measured at 30-day intervals throughout the entire history of each project, resulting in 1773 snapshots over 147 project-years of development. We conducted rename tracking at every 30-day snapshot to examine util files over their entire lifetime in a codebase. For example, we found that a util file can be as much as 2.75 times more likely to be involved in a vulnerability than non-util files. While every project can adopt their own naming conventions, the ubiquity and longevity of util files shows a broader developer intent that is useful for understanding the socio-technical nature of software development.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

### [74] [A Randomized Controlled Trial and Pilot of Scout: an LLM-Based EHR Search and Synthesis Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26953)
*Michael Gao,Suresh Balu,William Knechtle,Kartik Pejavara,William Jeck,Matthew Ellis,Jason Thieling,Blake Cameron,Jason Tatreau,Tareq Aljurf,Henry Foote,Michael Revoir,Marshall Nichols,Matthew Gardner,William Ratliff,Bradley Hintze,Angelo Milazzo,Sreekanth Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究开发并验证了LLM驱动的EHR搜索平台Scout，通过随机对照试验证明其可将临床任务完成时间缩短约38%，同时维持输出质量，并在超过200名用户的试点中展示了广泛的应用场景。研究还强调了人类验证在评估AI输出中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中的临床文档和数据检索对临床医生造成显著的工作负担和职业倦怠，亟需解决方案。

Method: 开发了基于LLM的EHR搜索与综合平台Scout，采用自然语言查询并提供原文引用。进行前瞻性随机、评估者盲法的交叉试验，纳入7个临床专科的20名参与者完成200个结构化病例。同时进行超过200名用户、20余个专科的试点部署（3个月，6600+次交互）。

Result: Scout使任务完成时间减少37.6%，显著降低感知工作量（精神需求、努力程度、时间需求降幅最大）。非劣效性分析表明，Scout辅助任务在准确性、完整性和相关性方面与仅使用EHR相当。自动化评估的错误率较低，但人工复核显示许多被标记为错误的声明实际上在病历中有支持证据。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的EHR工具（如Scout）可以在保持输出质量的同时显著减轻临床和行政工作负担。研究强调了人类验证的重要性，因为自动化评估可能存在误判。

Abstract: Clinical documentation and data retrieval within Electronic Health Records (EHRs) contribute substantially to clinician workload and burnout. To address this, we developed Scout, an LLM-based EHR search and synthesis platform that enables clinicians to query EHR data using natural language. Each response includes citations linking each claim to the original data source, facilitating easy verification of generated content. We conducted a prospective randomized, evaluator-blinded crossover trial across seven clinical specialties (20 participants, 200 structured cases). Participants completed realistic clinical tasks using either Scout or the EHR alone, with outcomes including time to completion, NASA Task Load Index workload scores, and blinded expert adjudication of accuracy, completeness, and relevance. Scout reduced task completion time by 37.6% and significantly decreased perceived workload, with the largest reductions in mental demand, effort, and temporal demand. Non-inferiority analyses showed that tasks completed with Scout maintained accuracy, completeness, and relevance relative to tasks completed with the EHR-only. A concurrent pilot deployment across over 200 users and more than 20 specialties generated over 6,600 interactions in three months, revealing diverse clinical and administrative use cases. Automated evaluation using an LLM-as-judge framework identified errors at low rates. Subsequent manual review of a subset of outputs revealed that most claims flagged by the automated judge as errors were in fact supported by the patient chart, demonstrating the importance of human validation. These findings provide early trial-based evidence that LLM-powered EHR tools can meaningfully reduce clinical and administrative workloads while maintaining output quality.

</details>


### [75] [AgenticRecTune: Multi-Agent with Self-Evolving Skillhub for Recommendation System Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26969)
*Xidong Wu,Yue Zhuan,Ruoqiao Wei,Hangxin Chen,Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Xinyi Wang,Xue Wang,Luoshu Wang,Xinwu Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AgenticRecTune是一个基于LLM的多智能体框架，通过五个专业智能体（Actor、Critic、Insight、Skill、Online）协作，实现推荐系统多阶段配置的全自动优化，并引入可自我进化的Skillhub进行知识积累，解决传统配置调优中迭代成本高、领域知识分散、指标平衡困难等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐系统采用多阶段pipeline（预排序、排序、重排序），现有研究仅关注单模型优化，忽视了系统级配置优化的重要性。配置优化面临三大挑战：模型修改后需重新寻找最优配置导致迭代成本高、多阶段模型优化目标各异需领域专业知识、以及多在线指标的平衡与业务目标对齐困难。

Method: 采用多智能体框架（Actor、Critic、Insight、Skill、Online五个专业智能体），利用LLM（Gemini）的强大推理能力探索最优配置空间，通过Skillhub实现自我进化和知识积累。

Result: 提出了一个完整的多智能体协作框架，能够自主完成配置优化全流程，包括候选生成、过滤、A/B测试执行和结果分析。

Conclusion: AgenticRecTune通过多智能体协作实现了推荐系统配置的端到端自动化优化，显著降低了人工调参成本，并通过Skillhub实现了知识的持续积累和任务迁移。

Abstract: Modern large-scale recommendation systems are typically constructed as multi-stage pipelines, encompassing pre-ranking, ranking, and re-ranking phases. While traditional recommendation research typically focuses on optimizing a specific model, such as improving the pre-ranking model structure or ranking models training algorithm, system-level configurations optimization play a crucial role, which integrates the output from each model head to get the final score in each stage. Due to the complexity of the system, the configuration optimization is highly important and challenging. Any model modification requires new optimal system-level configurations. But each experimental iteration requires significant tuning effort. Furthermore, models in different stage operates within a distinct context and optimizes for different targets, requiring specialized domain expertise. In addition, optimization success depends on balancing competing multiple online metrics and alignment with shifting production development objectives. To address these challenges, we propose AgenticRecTune, an agentic framework comprising five specialized agents, Actor, Critic, Insight, Skill, and Online, designed to manage the end-to-end configuration optimization workflow. By leveraging the advanced reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs), specifically Gemini, AgenticRecTune explore the optimal configuration spaces. The Actor Agent proposes multiple candidates and Critic Agent filters out suboptimal proposals.Then Online Agent autonomously prepares A/B tests based on the proposed configurations set from the Critic Agent and captures the subsequencet experimental results. We also introduce a self-evolving Skillhub, which utilizes a collaboration between the Insight Agent and Skill Agent to summarize the history results, extract underlying mechanics of each task in recommendation system and update skills.

</details>


### [76] [Not All Memories Age the Same: Autodiscovery of Adaptive Decay in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26970)
*Mandar Karhade*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出用速度-波动率参数化的连续衰减曲面替代知识图谱中的统一衰减模型，通过生存分析自动学习领域级、上下文级和实体级三层遗忘参数，实验验证了该分层方法能有效捕捉不同知识类型的时序动态差异，性能显著优于统一衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱检索系统将所有事实视为同等时效，但不同类型知识表现出不同的时序动态特性，统一衰减模型（单一遗忘曲线）存在根本性错误设定。

Method: 提出分层框架，核心是以速度（观测频率）和波动率（观测值间嵌入距离）两个正交信号参数化连续衰减曲面；采用生存分析方法建模边生命周期，事件为值替代而非简单重观测；框架包含领域级、上下文级和实体级三个可学习层级。

Result: 合成数据实验成功恢复植入的分层参数（HDBSCAN ARI=1.0）；107篇维基百科文章和1163条患者记录的验证表明速度-波动率聚类自然涌现并与可观察的持久性模式对齐，近乎普遍呈现Lindy效应（Weibull形状参数k<1）；均匀衰减性能比无时序加权差18倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个可学习的分层遗忘框架，通过生存分析从数据中自动学习不同知识类型的时序动态，无需预定义分类体系。实验验证了方法的有效性，均质衰减相比无时序加权差18倍，分层方法在各层级均有可测量的改进。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs used for retrieval treat all facts as equally current. Existing temporal approaches apply uniform decay, using a single forgetting curve regardless of knowledge type. We show this is fundamentally misspecified: different knowledge types exhibit different temporal dynamics, and the core retrieval problem is not latency or throughput but identifying what is important at query time.
  We propose a hierarchical framework that replaces uniform decay with a continuous decay surface parameterized by two orthogonal signals: velocity (how frequently a concept is observed) and volatility (how much the value changes between observations, measured via embedding distance). The decay surface is decomposed into three learnable levels: domain-level parameters capture universal patterns (some predicates are inherently permanent, others inherently transient), context-level parameters capture setting-dependent variation, and entity-level adaptation personalizes decay to specific subjects. All parameters emerge from data through survival analysis on observed value lifetimes, requiring no predefined taxonomies or domain expertise.
  We formulate edge lifetime as a survival problem where the event is value supersession (a meaningfully different value replacing the current one), distinct from mere re-observation. Experiments on synthetic temporal knowledge graphs demonstrate recovery of planted hierarchical parameters (HDBSCAN ARI = 1.0).
  Validation on 107 Wikipedia articles and 1,163 patient records from the Synthea clinical EHR simulator shows that velocity-volatility clusters emerge naturally, align with observable persistence patterns, and near-universally exhibit the Lindy effect (Weibull shape k < 1). Uniform decay performs 18x worse than no temporal weighting. Heterogeneous decay recovers from this, with each hierarchy level contributing measurable improvement.

</details>


### [77] [T2S-Metrics: Unified Library for Evaluating SPARQL Queries Generated From Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26971)
*Yousouf Taghzouti,Tao Jiang,Camille Juigné,Benjamin Navet,Fabien Gandon,Franck Michel,Louis-Felix Nothias*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了t2s-metrics，一个用于SPARQL查询比较和执行评估的开源统一评估库，提供20多种涵盖词法、句法、语义、结构、执行和排序等维度的评估指标，旨在解决知识图谱问答系统评估中的碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识图谱问答系统的评估存在碎片化、不一致和可复现性有限等问题。现有基准测试主要关注查询精确匹配或答案级F1分数，忽视了句法有效性、语义忠实性、执行正确性、结果排序质量和计算效率等重要维度。

Method: 提出了t2s-metrics，一个开源、可扩展的统一评估库，专门用于SPARQL查询比较和基于执行的评估。该库提供20多种评估指标，涵盖词法、句法、语义、结构、基于执行和基于排序的维度，包括Token级P/R/F1、BLEU、ROUGE、METEOR、CodeBLEU变体、变量归一化指标(SP-BLEU, SP-F1)、基于图和URI的精确匹配指标、答案集指标(F1-QALD, Jaccard)、排序指标(MRR, NDCG, P@k, Hit@k)及LLM-as-a-Judge指标。采用模块化抽象层设计，将指标规范与实现解耦。

Result: 该库提供了超过20种可扩展的评估指标，涵盖多个维度，能够实现SPARQL问答系统的一致、透明和可复现评估。

Conclusion: t2s-metrics represents a necessary step toward systematic, standardized evaluation of SPARQL-based QA systems, facilitating deeper diagnostic insights into system behavior beyond simple answer correctness.

Abstract: The evaluation of Question Answering (QA) systems over Knowledge Graphs has historically suffered from fragmentation, inconsistency, and limited reproducibility. While significant progress has been made in semantic parsing and SPARQL query generation, evaluation methodologies remain diverse, ad hoc, and often incomparable across studies. Existing benchmarks typically focus on a small subset of metrics, such as query exact match or answer-level F1, neglecting syntactic validity, semantic faithfulness, execution correctness, results ranking quality, and computational efficiency. In this paper, we present t2s-metrics, an open-source, extensible, and unified evaluation library designed specifically for SPARQL query comparison and execution-based assessment. t2s-metrics provides a broad and extensible set of over 20 evaluation metrics, collected from the literature and practical evaluation needs, spanning lexical, syntactic, semantic, structural, execution-based and ranking-based dimensions. These include query-based metrics such as token-level Precision, Recall, and F1; BLEU, ROUGE, METEOR, and CodeBLEU variants; variable-normalized metrics (SP-BLEU, SP-F1); graph-and URI-based exact match metrics; as well as answer set-based metrics such as F1-QALD and Jaccard similarity; ranking metrics including MRR, NDCG, P@k, and Hit@k; and LLM-as-a-Judge metrics. Taking inspiration from the ir-metrics library for Information Retrieval, t2s-metrics provides a modular abstraction layer that decouples metric specification from implementation, enabling consistent, transparent, and reproducible evaluation of SPARQLbased QA systems. We argue that t2s-metrics constitutes a necessary step toward systematic, standardized evaluation in question answering over knowledge graphs and facilitates deeper diagnostic insights into system behavior beyond answer correctness.

</details>


### [78] [Budget-Constrained Online Retrieval-Augmented Generation: The Chunk-as-a-Service Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26981)
*Shawqi Al-Maliki,Ammar Gharaibeh,Mohamed Rahouti,Mohammad Ruhul Amin,Mohamed Abdallah,Junaid Qadir,Ala Al-Fuqaha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对RAG-as-a-Service (RaaS)中基于提示数量的不透明定价问题，提出Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS)作为透明且具成本效益的替代方案，包含Open-Budget CaaS和Limited-Budget CaaS两种变体，通过UCOSA算法在线选择相关文本块来丰富提示。实验表明UCOSA相比随机选择提升约52%，达离线方法的75%性能；LB-CaaS和OB-CaaS的成本效益分别比RaaS高出140%和86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在幻觉和缺乏可解释性问题，RAG虽可缓解但RaaS定价模式不透明——按提示数量计费而非考虑检索文本块的相关性或质量，导致用户成本效益低下且付费模式不透明。

Method: 提出Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS)框架，包含两种变体：(1) Open-Budget CaaS (OB-CaaS)：无预算约束地丰富提示；(2) Limited-Budget CaaS (LB-CaaS)：在预算约束下进行效用-成本权衡的在线选择。核心算法UCOSA通过在线方式，根据预算限制和效用-成本权衡，选择性地对提交提示的子集进行文本块丰富。

Result: 在NEP×AR指标上，UCOSA相比随机选择提升约52%，达离线方法的75%性能；在预算利用率方面，LB-CaaS和OB-CaaS相比RaaS分别提升140%和86%的性能-预算比。

Conclusion: CaaS框架及其UCOSA算法能有效提升检索增强生成的透明度和成本效益，在有限预算约束下仍能保持较高的性能表现，优于传统RaaS模式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized the field of natural language processing. However, they exhibit some limitations, including a lack of reliability and transparency: they may hallucinate and fail to provide sources that support the generated output. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) was introduced to address such limitations in LLMs. One popular implementation, RAG-as-a-Service (RaaS), has shortcomings that hinder its adoption and accessibility.
