For instance, RaaS pricing is based on the number of submitted prompts, without considering whether the prompts are enriched by relevant chunks, i.e., text segments retrieved from a vector database, or the quality of the utilized chunks (i.e., their degree of relevance). This results in an opaque and less cost-effective payment model. We propose Chunk-as-a-Service (CaaS) as a transparent and cost-effective alternative. CaaS includes two variants: Open-Budget CaaS (OB-CaaS) and Limited-Budget CaaS (LB-CaaS), which is enabled by our ``Utility-Cost Online Selection Algorithm (UCOSA)''. UCOSA further extends the cost-effectiveness and the accessibility of the OB-CaaS variant by enriching, in an online manner, a subset of the submitted prompts based on budget constraints and utility-cost tradeoff. Our experiments demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed UCOSA compared to both offline and relevance-greedy selection baselines. In terms of the performance metric-the number of enriched prompts (NEP) multiplied by the Average Relevance (AR)-UCOSA outperforms random selection by approximately 52% and achieves around 75% of the performance of offline selection methods. Additionally, in terms of budget utilization, LB-CaaS and OB-CaaS achieve higher performance-to-budget ratios of 140% and 86%, respectively, compared to RaaS, indicating their superior efficiency.

</details>


### [79] [Value-Aware Product Recommendation by Customer Segmentation using a suitable High-Dimensional Similarity Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26983)
*María Florencia Acosta,Rodrigo García Arancibia,Pamela Llop,Mariel Lovatto,Lucas Mansilla*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种价值感知的 product recommendation 方法，通过将收入贡献编码到用户-项目矩阵中，计算基于收入相似性的用户分段，提出三种盈利能力推荐策略，并验证了其在高维稀疏数据场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决用户-项目数据的高维性和稀疏性问题，同时显式考虑产品和用户对整体销售收入的贡献，以实现与盈利能力目标一致的推荐。

Method: 提出将收入贡献编码到用户-项目矩阵中，基于适当的距离度量直接计算客户相似性，根据购买篮子的收入相似性对用户进行细分，并提出三种基于收入份额、产品流行度和预期利润生成的推荐策略，同时比较了传统相似性度量与针对高维情境的新颖替代方案。

Result: 通过模拟实验和UCI在线零售数据集验证了方法的有效性，证明该方法能够支持盈利能力导向的推荐。

Conclusion: 该方法通过模拟实验和UCI在线零售数据集的真实应用得到验证，能够提供与盈利能力目标一致的推荐。

### 关键词：价值感知推荐、收入贡献、用户细分、高维稀疏数据、盈利能力优化

Abstract: This paper presents a novel value-aware approach to product recommendation that simultaneously addresses the high dimensionality and sparsity of user-item data while explicitly incorporating the contribution of each product and user to overall sales revenue. The proposed framework encodes revenue contributions in the user-item matrix and computes customer similarity directly on this basis using suitable distance measures. This enables the segmentation of users according to the revenue-based similarity of their purchase baskets and supports recommendations aligned with profitability objectives. We compare conventional similarity metrics with a novel alternative tailored to high-dimensional contexts and propose three recommendation strategies based on revenue share, product popularity, and expected profit generation. The effectiveness of the proposed method is validated through simulation experiments and a real-world application using the UCI Online Retail dataset.

</details>


### [80] [LUCid: Redefining Relevance For Lifelong Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26996)
*Chimaobi Okite,Anika Misra,Joyce Chai,Rada Mihalcea*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LUCid基准揭示当前个性化系统在与用户查询语义距离较远时表现急剧恶化，表明现有系统对"相关性"的定义与实际情境需求存在根本性错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身个性化方法仅通过语义邻近性来操作化相关性概念，导致其遗漏来自主题不相关交互中的重要用户信息，存在显著的应用缺口。

Method: 构建LUCid基准，包含1,936个现实查询及来自多达500个会话的交互历史配对数据。基于该基准在多种架构模型上进行实验测试。

Result: 当相关上下文必须从语义遥远的的历史中检索时，性能显著下降：最难实例上的检索召回率降至接近零；Gemini-3-Flash、GPT-5.4、Claude Haiku等最先进模型的响应对齐度也仅维持在约50%左右。

Conclusion: LUCid benchmark揭示了当前个性化系统与以用户为中心的情境相关性之间存在根本性不匹配，对系统在关键用户属性未被检测时的鲁棒性和安全性具有直接影响。该基准为系统性评估模型能否从历史交互中提取情境相关用户信息提供了方法支撑，是将个性化研究重新导向用户中心相关性方向的重要一步。

Abstract: Current approaches to lifelong personalization operationalize relevance through semantic proximity, causing them to miss essential user information from topically unrelated interactions. To address this gap, we introduce LUCid, a benchmark designed to measure situational user-centric relevance in personalization. The benchmark consists of 1,936 realistic queries paired with interaction histories from up to 500 sessions. Across multiple architectures, our experiments show significant performance collapse when relevant context must be surfaced from semantically distant history: retrieval recall drops to near zero on the hardest instances, and response alignment remains near 50% even for state-of-the-art models such as Gemini-3-Flash, GPT-5.4, and Claude Haiku. These results expose a fundamental mismatch between the notion of relevance encoded by current systems and the situational relevance required for personalization, with direct implications for robustness and safety when critical user attributes remain undetected. LUCid enables the systematic evaluation of whether current models can surface situationally-relevant user information from previous interactions, and serves as a step toward realigning personalization with user-centered relevance.

</details>


### [81] [Hypencoder Revisited: Reproducibility and Analysis of Non-Linear Scoring for First-Stage Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27037)
*Arne Eichholtz,Yongkang Li,Jutte Vijverberg,Tobias Groot,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对Hypencoder检索框架进行了系统性复现与扩展评估。研究验证了该框架在标准检索任务上的有效性，同时揭示其在硬检索任务上的局限性，并发现标准双编码器在查询延迟上仍具优势，而替代编码器集成与对抗鲁棒性表现高度依赖具体配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Killingback等人提出的Hypencoder检索框架进行系统性复现，并突破原始分析边界以全面评估其性能边界、实际部署效率及鲁棒性特性。

Method: 采用完全复现策略验证原始Hypencoder，并在三个方向进行扩展：(i) 集成替代预训练编码器；(ii) 与基于Faiss的双编码器管道进行查询延迟对比；(iii) 对抗鲁棒性评估。

Result: 复现结果支持Hypencoder在领域内和跨领域基准上优于双编码器基线，高效搜索算法显著降低延迟；硬检索任务上在DL-Hard和FollowIR领先但TREC TOT表现欠佳；替代编码器集成效果取决于编码器和微调策略；查询延迟方面标准双编码器仍更快；对抗鲁棒性上q-net非线性评分未显示持续劣势。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了Hypencoder的有效性，并通过三个扩展方向（替代编码器集成、延迟对比、对抗鲁棒性）深化了对其性能边界的理解。研究为Hypencoder框架的实用性和局限性提供了全面的实证依据。

Abstract: The Hypencoder, proposed by Killingback et al., is a retrieval framework that replaces the fixed inner-product scoring function used in standard bi-encoders with a query-specific neural network (the $q$-net), whose weights are generated by a hypernetwork from the contextualized query embeddings. This design enables more expressive relevance estimation while preserving independent query and document encoding. In this work, we conduct a reproducibility study of the Hypencoder and extend the original analysis in three directions. Our reproduction confirms that the Hypencoder outperforms a similarly trained bi-encoder baseline on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, and that the proposed efficient search algorithm substantially reduces query latency with minimal performance loss. On hard retrieval tasks, we find partial support: the Hypencoder outperforms the baseline on DL-Hard and FollowIR, but not on TREC TOT, where checkpoint incompatibility and fine-tuning sensitivity complicate full verification. Beyond reproduction, we investigate three extensions: (i)~integrating alternative pre-trained encoders into the Hypencoder framework, where we find that performance gains depend on the encoder and fine-tuning strategy; (ii)~comparing query latency against a Faiss-based bi-encoder pipeline, revealing that standard bi-encoder retrieval remains faster under both exhaustive and efficient search settings; and (iii)~evaluating adversarial robustness, where we find that the $q$-net's non-linear scoring does not provide a consistent robustness disadvantage over inner-product scoring. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/arneeichholtz/Hypencoder-reprod.

</details>


### [82] [A Gated Hybrid Contrastive Collaborative Filtering Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27117)
*Eduardo Ferreira da Silva,Mayki dos Santos Oliveira,Joel Machado Pires,Denis Dantas Boaventura,Maycon Maciel Peixoto,Cassio Serafim Prazeres,Gustavo Bittencourt Figueiredo,Miriam Capretz,Frederico Araujo Durão*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种门控混合协同过滤框架，通过自适应门控机制和对比学习将文本review信息融入自编码器协同过滤模型，并用成对BPR目标优化排名。实验证明受控语义融合对排名驱动推荐至关重要，在多个数据集上超越了先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数review-aware推荐模型针对评分预测进行优化，而非排名质量，这种不对齐限制了其在前N推荐场景（排名判别性至关重要）中的效果。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：（1）门控混合架构：将review-derived表示通过自适应门控机制逐层注入到基于自编码器的协同过滤模型中，动态平衡协同嵌入与基于主题的特征；（2）对比学习模块：对齐语义信号与协同信号以细化潜在空间；（3）训练目标：采用成对贝叶斯个性化排序（BPR）目标，明确优化排名行为，促进相关与非相关项目在潜在空间中的分离。在Amazon Movies & TV、IMDb和Rotten Tomatoes三个数据集上评估了五种配置变体。

Result: 在Hit Rate@10和NDCG@10指标上，该框架相比现有的review-aware基线模型取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 实验表明，受控的语义融合（controlled semantic fusion）对排名驱动的推荐任务至关重要。该框架通过结合自适应门控机制和对比学习目标，有效解决了传统review-aware模型在排名优化方面的不足，为top-N推荐场景提供了一种更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recommender systems increasingly incorporate textual reviews to enrich user and item representations. However, most review-aware models remain optimized for rating prediction rather than ranking quality. This misalignment limits their effectiveness in top-N recommendation scenarios, where discriminative ranking is essential. To address this gap, we propose a Gated Hybrid Collaborative Filtering framework that integrates review-derived representations into an autoencoder-based collaborative model. The architecture injects semantic signals layer-wise through an adaptive gating mechanism that dynamically balances collaborative embeddings and topic-based features during encoding. To further refine the latent space, we introduce a contrastive learning module that aligns semantic and collaborative signals. We evaluate the framework across five distinct configurations: Pure collaborative; Topic and Gated; Text and Gated; and the addition of contrastive objectives (Contrastive and Topic, and Contrastive and Text). To explicitly optimize ranking behavior, the model is trained with a pairwise Bayesian personalized ranking objective, which promotes separation between relevant and non-relevant items in the latent space. Experiments on Amazon Movies & TV, IMDb, and Rotten Tomatoes demonstrate consistent improvements in hit rate @10 and normalized discounted cumulative gain @10 over state-of-the-art review-aware baselines. Results highlight the importance of controlled semantic fusion for ranking-driven recommendation.

</details>


### [83] [LLM-Enhanced Topical Trend Detection at Snapchat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27131)
*Hangqi Zhao,Jay Li,Abhiruchi Bhattacharya,Cong Ni,Jason Yeung,Jinchao Ye,Kai Yang,Akshat Malu,Manish Malik*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Snapchat推出业界首个短视频平台生产级主题趋势检测系统，通过多模态主题提取、时序突发检测和LLM整合增强三阶段管线，实现新兴主题的精准实时识别，已在全球范围部署并驱动内容排名与搜索等下游应用优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频社交平台需在大规模场景下及时识别新兴主题趋势，以保持内容生态动态更新，但现有方法难以兼顾准确性、时效性与可扩展性。

Method: 融合多模态主题提取、时序突发检测与LLM驱动的整合增强三大模块，构建端到端自动化流水线。

Result: 连续6个月离线人工评估验证高精确率，生产环境全球部署有效提升内容新鲜度与用户体验。

Conclusion: 首个在短视频平台生产规模部署的端到端主题趋势检测系统，验证了多模态融合与LLM结合的技术路线在工业级应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Automatic detection of topical trends at scale is both challenging and essential for maintaining a dynamic content ecosystem on social media platforms. In this work, we present a large-scale system for identifying emerging topical trends on Snapchat, one of the world's largest short-video social platforms. Our system integrates multimodal topic extraction, time-series burst detection, and LLM-based consolidation and enrichment to enable accurate and timely trend discovery. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first published end-to-end system for topical trend detection on short-video platforms at production scale. Continuous offline human evaluation over six months demonstrates high precision in identifying meaningful trends. The system has been deployed in production at global scale and applied to downstream surfaces including content ranking and search, driving measurable improvements in content freshness and user experience.

</details>


### [84] [RAQG-QPP: Query Performance Prediction with Retrieved Query Variants and Retrieval Augmented Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27244)
*Fangzheng Tian,Debasis Ganguly,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAQG方法，通过从历史查询日志中检索相似查询并利用LLM生成查询变体来改进查询性能预测(QPP)。该方法解决了传统词项扩展方法产生低质量查询变体的问题，在神经排序模型上相比现有最佳方法提升达30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于查询变体的预测方法依赖输入查询的词项扩展，容易产生不连贯、幻觉和偏离主题的查询变体；且现有无监督QPP方法对神经排序模型效果不佳。

Method: 提出RAQG方法：1) 从历史查询日志中检索相似查询作为查询变体(QVs)；2) 利用大语言模型(LLMs)基于检索到的QVs生成更多查询变体，缓解日志查询有限的局限性。

Result: RAQG方法在神经排序模型(如MonoT5)上的预测性能比最佳现有QV方法提升30%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，使用RAQG增强的QPP在TREC DL'19和DL'20数据集上，针对MonoT5等神经排序模型，相比现有最佳QV-based预测方法提升高达30%。

Abstract: Query Performance Prediction (QPP) estimates the retrieval quality of ranking models without the use of any human-assessed relevance judgements, and finds applications in query-specific selective decision making to improve overall retrieval effectiveness. Although unsupervised QPP approaches are effective for lexical retrieval models, they usually perform weaker for neural rankers. Recent work shows that leveraging query variants (QVs), i.e., queries with potentially similar information needs to a given query, can enhance unsupervised QPP accuracy. However, existing QV-based prediction methods rely on query variants generated by term expansion of the input query, which is likely to yield incoherent, hallucinatory and off-topic QVs. In this paper, we propose to make use of queries retrieved from a log of past queries as QVs to be subsequently used for QPP. In addition to directly applying retrieved QVs in QPP, we further propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to generate QVs conditioned on the retrieved QVs, thus mitigating the limitation of relying only on existing queries in a log. Experiments on TREC DL'19 and DL'20 show that QPP enhanced with RAQG outperform the best-performing existing QV-based prediction approach by as much as 30% on neural ranking models such as MonoT5.

</details>


### [85] [NuggetIndex: Governed Atomic Retrieval for Maintainable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27306)
*Saber Zerhoudi,Michael Granitzer,Jelena Mitrovic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NuggetIndex通过原子信息单元存储模式革新RAG系统评估，解决了传统方法中文本片段检索与事实级评估的单位不匹配问题，并有效管理知识时序性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统存在三大缺陷：(1)检索对象与评估对象单位不匹配；(2)语料库更新时难以维护；(3)无法捕获被取代的事实或源冲突。

Method: 提出NuggetIndex系统，将原子信息单元（nuggets）存储为托管记录，每条记录包含证据链接、时间有效性间隔和生命周期状态，通过在排序前过滤无效/废弃nuggets实现时序感知检索。

Result: 相比段落和 unmanaged proposition 检索基线：nugget召回率提升42%，时序正确性提高9个百分点（未出现时间过滤基线中的召回崩塌），冲突率降低55%，生成器输入长度减少64%。

Conclusion: NuggetIndex通过原子化信息单元存储模式，有效解决了RAG系统的时序知识管理和事实一致性难题，在多项指标上显著优于基线方法，同时支持轻量级部署。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are frequently evaluated via fact-based metrics, yet standard implementations retrieve passages or static propositions. This unit mismatch between evaluation and retrieval objects hinders maintenance when corpora evolve and fails to capture superseded facts or source disagreements. We propose NuggetIndex, a retrieval system that stores atomic information units as managed records, so called nuggets. Each record maintains links to evidence, a temporal validity interval, and a lifecycle state. By filtering invalid or deprecated nuggets prior to ranking, the system prevents the inclusion of outdated information. We evaluate the approach using a nuggetized MS MARCO subset, a temporal Wikipedia QA dataset, and a multi-hop QA task. Against passage and unmanaged proposition retrieval baselines, NuggetIndex improves nugget recall by 42%, increases temporal correctness by 9 percentage points without the recall collapse observed in time-filtered baselines, and reduces conflict rates by 55%. The compact nugget format reduces generator input length by 64% while enabling lightweight index structures suitable for browser-based and resource-constrained deployment. We release our implementation, datasets, and evaluation scripts

</details>


### [86] [From Unstructured to Structured: LLM-Guided Attribute Graphs for Entity Search and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27410)
*Yilun Zhu,Nikhita Vedula,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法，将大语言模型驱动的属性图构建与图感知的大语言模型排序相结合，用于电子商务中的实体搜索。该方法在离线阶段构建可重用的属性图，在线阶段通过在结构化表示上推理来排名候选项，在零样本场景下无需训练数据即可实现超过5%的平均精度提升，同时将每个产品的令牌使用量减少57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional embedding-based approaches struggle to capture nuanced context-specific attribute relevance in e-commerce entity search, where product similarity varies across categories and contexts.

Method: The method consists of two stages: (1) An offline stage that extracts structured product attributes from unstructured text and constructs a reusable attribute graph with category-aware schemas; (2) An online stage that ranks retrieved candidates by reasoning over the structured graph representation rather than raw text.

Result: The approach reduces per-product token usage by 57% while improving ranking precision. Under zero-shot scenarios, it achieves over 5% improvement in average precision without requiring training data, and generalizes robustly across diverse product categories.

Conclusion: This approach demonstrates significant potential for real-world deployment, offering a practical solution for entity search in e-commerce that balances efficiency and accuracy.

Abstract: Entity search, i.e., finding the most similar entities to a query entity, faces unique challenges in e-commerce, where product similarity varies across categories and contexts. Traditional embedding-based approaches often struggle to capture nuanced context-specific attribute relevance. In this paper, we present a two-stage approach combining Large Language Model (LLM)-driven attribute graph construction with graph-aware LLM ranking. In the offline stage, we extract structured product attributes from unstructured text, and construct a reusable attribute graph with category-aware schemas. In the online stage, we rank retrieved candidates by reasoning over this structured representation rather than raw text, reducing per-product token usage by 57% while improving ranking precision. Experiments show that our approach outperforms multiple baselines under zero-shot scenarios, achieving a over 5% improvement in average precision without requiring training data, generalizes robustly across diverse product categories, and shows immense potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [87] [A Reproducibility Study of LLM-Based Query Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27421)
*Amin Bigdeli,Radin Hamidi Rad,Hai Son Le,Mert Incesu,Negar Arabzadeh,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对10种LLM查询改写方法进行了系统性复现研究，发现改写效果强烈依赖于检索范式，词法检索的改进不能可靠地转移到神经检索器，且更大的模型并不必然带来更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM查询改写研究在异构实验条件下进行，难以评估哪些发现可复现、哪些依赖于特定实现选择。需要系统性复现研究来澄清这一问题。

Method: 在统一且严格控制的实验框架下，对10种代表性LLM查询改写方法进行系统性复现和比较研究。评估覆盖两个LLM家族（两种参数规模）、三种检索范式（词法、稀疏学习、密集）以及九个基准数据集（TREC深度学习和BEIR）。

Result: 查询改写收益高度依赖于检索范式；词法检索下的改进不能一致地转移到神经检索器；更大的LLM并不总是带来更好的下游性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了先前LLM查询改写工作中报告的收益的稳定性和局限性。所有材料、代码和运行文件通过QueryGym工具包公开发布，以支持透明复现和持续比较。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now widely used for query reformulation and expansion in Information Retrieval, with many studies reporting substantial effectiveness gains. However, these results are typically obtained under heterogeneous experimental conditions, making it difficult to assess which findings are reproducible and which depend on specific implementation choices. In this work, we present a systematic reproducibility and comparative study of ten representative LLM-based query reformulation methods under a unified and strictly controlled experimental framework. We evaluate methods across two architectural LLM families at two parameter scales, three retrieval paradigms (lexical, learned sparse, and dense), and nine benchmark datasets spanning TREC Deep Learning and BEIR. Our results show that reformulation gains are strongly conditioned on the retrieval paradigm, that improvements observed under lexical retrieval do not consistently transfer to neural retrievers, and that larger LLMs do not uniformly yield better downstream performance. These findings clarify the stability and limits of reported gains in prior work. To enable transparent replication and ongoing comparison, we release all prompts, configurations, evaluation scripts, and run files through QueryGym, an open-source reformulation toolkit with a public leaderboard.\footnote{https://leaderboard.querygym.com}

</details>


### [88] [Reproducing Adaptive Reranking for Reasoning-Intensive IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27577)
*Mandeep Rathee,V Venktesh,Sean MacAvaney,Avishek Anand*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This work replicates GAR on the BRIGHT benchmark to show that iterative graph-based reranking can effectively address bounded recall in reasoning-intensive retrieval at minimal computational cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The classical retrieve-then-rerank pipeline suffers from bounded recall due to first-stage retriever limitations; current approaches that improve the retriever incur substantial training and inference costs, especially for reasoning-intensive queries.

Method: Replicate GAR (Graph-based Adaptive Reranking) on the BRIGHT benchmark, which addresses bounded recall by iteratively exploring a corpus graph during reranking, testing with both reasoning and non-reasoning reranking models.

Result: The quality of the reranker's signal plays an important role in identifying additional relevant documents within the corpus graph; GAR boosts effectiveness of reasoning-intensive retrieval across various models while contributing minimally to computational overheads.

Conclusion: GAR enables more practical deployment of retrieval systems for reasoning-intensive queries by boosting effectiveness across various models while contributing minimally to computational overheads.

Abstract: The classical cascading pipeline of retrieve--rerank suffers from a bounded recall problem, stemming from limitations of the first-stage retriever. Most current approaches address the bounded recall problem by improving the first-stage retriever, but this incurs substantial training and inference costs, especially to handle queries that require substantial reasoning. To circumvent the computational costs of reasoning-based retrievers, we replicate the findings of GAR, Graph-based Adaptive Reranking, on the BRIGHT reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmark. GAR addresses the bounded recall problem by modifying the reranking process itself through iterative exploration of a corpus graph, but it was previously only tested on models designed for topical and question-answering-style queries. Hence, reproduce GAR in reasoning-intensive settings with reasoning and non-reasoning reranking models. We observe that the quality of the reranker's signal plays an important role in identifying additional relevant documents within the corpus graph. Overall, we find that GAR boosts the effectiveness of reasoning-intensive retrieval across a variety of models while contributing minimally to computational overheads. Ultimately, this work enables more practical deployment of retrieval systems that can address reasoning-intensive queries.

</details>


### [89] [One Pass, Any Order: Position-Invariant Listwise Reranking for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27599)
*Ethan Bito,Yongli Ren,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了InvariRank框架，通过结构化注意力掩码和旋转位置编码(RoPE)实现排列不变性，解决大语言模型在推荐重排序中的位置偏差问题，实现单次前向传播完成评分，避免了传统方法需要多排列训练的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在推荐重排序中存在排列敏感性问题——同一候选集仅因呈现顺序不同就会产生不同的分数和排名。这与推荐的集合本质不匹配，限制了LLM重排序器的可靠性。

Method: InvariRank采用两大核心设计：(1) 结构化注意力掩码阻断跨候选者注意力，(2) 共享位置编码框架配合旋转位置嵌入(RoPE)抵消位置引起的评分偏差。模型在单一前向传播中评分所有候选者，无需传统排列不变性训练所需的多个排列组合。

Result: 在推荐基准数据集上的实验表明，InvariRank在保持竞争力的排序效果的同时，能够产生稳定的跨候选排列排名。

Conclusion: 架构层面的不变性设计是实现可靠、高效LLM推荐重排序的实用路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for recommendation reranking, but their listwise predictions can depend on the order in which candidates are presented. This creates a mismatch between the set-based nature of recommendation and the sequence-based computation of decoder-only LLMs, where permuting an otherwise identical candidate set can change item scores and final rankings. Such order sensitivity makes LLM-based rerankers difficult to rely on, since rankings may reflect prompt serialization rather than user preference. We propose InvariRank, a permutation-invariant listwise reranking framework that addresses this dependence at the architectural level. InvariRank blocks cross-candidate attention with a structured attention mask and negates position-induced scoring changes through shared positional framing under Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE). Combined with a listwise learning-to-rank objective, the model scores all candidates in a single forward pass, avoiding permutation-based invariance training objectives that require multiple permutations of a candidate set. Experiments on recommendation benchmarks show that InvariRank maintains competitive ranking effectiveness while producing stable rankings across candidate permutations. The results suggest that architectural invariance is a practical route to reliable and efficient LLM-based recommendation reranking. The source code is at https://github.com/ejbito/InvariRank.

</details>


### [90] [Purifying Multimodal Retrieval: Fragment-Level Evidence Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27600)
*Xihang Wang,Zihan Wang,Chengkai Huang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FES-RAG通过将MRAG重新定义为细粒度证据选择问题，从文档级选择转变为片段级选择，利用片段信息增益指标从高容量MLLM蒸馏出轻量级选择器，在减少上下文长度的同时显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRAG框架将检索到的证据视为不可分割的文档，隐含假设文档内所有内容同等重要，但实际上只有一小部分与查询相关，剩余内容会引入大量噪声导致性能下降。

Method: 将多模态文档分解为句子级文本片段和区域级视觉片段；引入FIG（片段信息增益）指标来衡量每个片段对MLLM生成置信度的边际贡献；将片段级效用判断从高容量MLLM蒸馏到轻量级选择器中，以低推理开销实现精确的证据选择。

Result: 在M2RAG基准测试中，FES-RAG始终优于最先进的文档级MRAG方法，CIDER指标相对提升最高达27%，同时显著减少了上下文长度。

Conclusion: FES-RAG通过选择更少但信息量更高的片段，在减少上下文长度的同时提高了事实准确性和生成连贯性，在M2RAG基准测试中实现了最先进的文档级MRAG方法相对改进了27%的CIDEr分数。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MRAG) is widely adopted for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with external evidence to reduce hallucinations. Despite its success, most existing MRAG frameworks treat retrieved evidence as indivisible documents, implicitly assuming that all content within a document is equally informative. In practice, however, sometimes only a small fraction of a document is relevant to a given query, while the remaining content introduces substantial noise that may lead to performance degradation. We address this fundamental limitation by reframing MRAG as a fine-grained evidence selection problem. We propose Fragment-level Evidence Selection for RAG (FES-RAG), a framework that selects atomic multimodal fragments rather than entire documents as grounding evidence. FES-RAG decomposes retrieved multimodal documents into sentence-level textual fragments and region-level visual fragments, enabling precise identification of evidence that directly supports generation. To guide fragment selection, we introduce Fragment Information Gain (FIG), a principled metric that measures the marginal contribution of each fragment to the MLLM's generation confidence. Based on FIG, we distill fragment-level utility judgments from a high-capacity MLLM into a lightweight selector, achieving accurate evidence selection with low inference overhead. Experiments on the M2RAG benchmark show that FES-RAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art document-level MRAG methods, achieving up to 27 percent relative improvement in CIDEr. By selecting fewer yet more informative fragments, our approach substantially reduces context length while improving factual accuracy and generation coherence.

</details>


### [91] [Position-Aware Drafting for Inference Acceleration in LLM-Based Generative List-Wise Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27747)
*Jiaju Chen,Chongming Gao,Chenxiao Fan,Haoyan Liu,Qingpeng Cai,Peng Jiang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PAD-Rec，通过位置感知embedding和门控机制增强draft模型，解决了speculative decoding在生成式推荐中忽略item内token位置和深度相关不确定性的问题，实现最高3.1倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: speculative decoding在生成式推荐中存在两个被忽视的实际问题：(1) token的语义取决于其在item内的槽位；(2) 不确定性随推测深度增加而增加。现有方法将item的多个semantic-ID tokens统一处理，限制了SD的加速效果。

Method: 提出PAD-Rec，一种位置感知的drafting方法，通过两个互补信号增强draft模型：(1) Item position embeddings编码每个token在item内的槽位；(2) Step position embeddings编码draft步骤以适应深度相关的不确定性。使用简单的门控机制协调这些信号：item slots使用可学习系数，draft steps使用context-driven gate。

Result: 在4个真实数据集上，PAD-Rec最高实现3.1倍wall-clock加速，相比强SD baselines平均提升约5%的加速，同时基本保持推荐质量。

Conclusion: PAD-Rec是一个轻量级模块，可轻松集成到标准draft模型中，添加可忽略的推理开销。在4个真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法最高可实现3.1倍的wall-clock加速，相比强SD baselines平均提升约5%的加速，同时基本保持推荐质量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based generative list-wise recommendation has advanced rapidly, but decoding remains sequential and thus latency-prone. To accelerate inference without changing the target distribution, speculative decoding (SD) uses a small draft model to propose several next tokens at once and a target LLM to verify and accept the longest prefix, skipping multiple steps per round. In generative recommendation, however, each item is represented by multiple semantic-ID tokens, often with separators, and current drafts typically treat these tokens uniformly. This overlooks two practical facts: (i) a token's semantics depend on its within-item slot, and (ii) uncertainty tends to increase with speculation depth. Without modeling these effects, SD's speedups can be limited. We introduce PAD-Rec, Position-Aware Drafting for generative Recommendation, a lightweight module that augments the draft model with two complementary signals. Item position embeddings explicitly encode the within-item slot of each token, strengthening structural awareness. Step position embeddings encode the draft step, allowing the model to adapt to depth-dependent uncertainty and improve proposal quality. To harmonize these signals with base features, we add simple gates: a learnable coefficient for item slots and a context-driven gate for draft steps. The module is trainable, easy to integrate with standard draft models, and adds negligible inference overhead. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show up to 3.1x wall-clock speedup and about 5% average wall-clock speedup gain over strong SD baselines, while largely preserving recommendation quality.

</details>


### [92] [NeocorRAG: Less Irrelevant Information, More Explicit Evidence, and More Effective Recall via Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27852)
*Shiyao Peng,Qianhe Zheng,Zhuodi Hao,Zichen Tang,Rongjin Li,Qing Huang,Jiayu Huang,Jiacheng Liu,Yifan Zhu,Haihong E*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对RAG系统中检索改进与推理增益不匹配的问题，提出RCR评估指标，发现检索质量被忽视导致RCR近线性衰减。创新性提出NeocorRAG框架，通过激活搜索、约束解码和证据链引导三阶段实现综合检索质量优化，突破质量-召回率权衡困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中检索性能提升未能有效转化为下游推理能力提升，存在关键评估缺口。

Method: 提出Recall Conversion Rate (RCR)指标量化检索对推理的贡献度；识别出现有方法的"质量-召回率"权衡困境。提出NeocorRAG框架：(1)激活搜索算法获取精细候选空间；(2)约束解码确保精确证据链生成；(3)证据链引导检索优化。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue、NQ等基准上，NeocorRAG在3B和70B参数模型上均达到SOTA性能，且token消耗不足可比方法的20%。

Conclusion: NeocorRAG提出了高效、无需训练的RAG增强范式，通过证据链实现综合检索质量优化，在多个基准数据集和模型规模上均达到SOTA性能，同时将token消耗降至可比方法的20%以下。

Abstract: Although precise recall is a core objective in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), a critical oversight persists in the field: improvements in retrieval performance do not consistently translate to commensurate gains in downstream reasoning. To diagnose this gap, we propose the Recall Conversion Rate (RCR), a novel evaluation metric to quantify the contribution of retrieval to reasoning accuracy. Our quantitative analysis of mainstream RAG methods reveals that as Recall@5 improves, the RCR exhibits a near-linear decay. We identify the neglect of retrieval quality in these methods as the underlying cause. In contrast, approaches that focus solely on quality optimization often suffer from inferior recall performance. Both categories lack a comprehensive understanding of retrieval quality optimization, resulting in a trade-off dilemma. To address these challenges, we propose comprehensive retrieval quality optimization criteria and introduce the NeocorRAG framework. This framework achieves holistic retrieval quality optimization by systematically mining and utilizing Evidence Chains. Specifically, NeocorRAG first employs an innovative activated search algorithm to obtain a refined candidate space. Then it ensures precise evidence chain generation through constrained decoding. Finally, the retrieved set of evidence chains guides the retrieval optimization process. Evaluated on benchmarks including HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue, and NQ, NeocorRAG achieves SOTA performance on both 3B and 70B parameter models, while consuming less than 20% of tokens used by comparable methods. This study presents an efficient, training-free paradigm for RAG enhancement that effectively optimizes retrieval quality while maintaining high recall. Our code is released at https://github.com/BUPT-Reasoning-Lab/NeocorRAG.

</details>


### [93] [SimEval-IR: A Unified Toolkit and Benchmark Suite for Evaluating User Simulators and Search Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27878)
*Saber Zerhoudi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SimEval-IR工具包用于评估用户模拟器，系统区分了行为真实性和测试器可靠性两个不同目标，发现常用的"类人性"检测方法对系统排序预测效果很差，而边际点击深度距离和Fréchet距离更具预测力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器在交互式信息检索中日益重要，但社区缺乏标准化评估工具。当前研究中，行为真实性（匹配真实用户行为）和测试器可靠性（产生有效系统排序）这两个目标常被混淆，尽管它们本质不同且有时相互冲突。

Method: 提出SimEval-IR工具包和基准测试套件，包含：（1）统一会话schema，适配器验证和显式损失计算；（2）三个可执行基准测试，分别评估行为真实性、基于RATE风格估计的测试器可靠性及两者关联分析；（3）在四个真实数据集（两种语言）和四个模拟器族上进行基线实验。

Result: 关键发现：文献中主导的"类人性"分类器-判别器检查对系统排序有效性几乎没有 pooled 预测能力（r=+0.09, n=48）；而边际点击深度距离和会话嵌入的Fréchet距离提供更强信号（|r|=0.43和0.40, p≤0.005）。

Conclusion: SimEval-IR提供了一个统一框架，区分行为真实性和测试器可靠性两个目标，使评估可测量。开源发布所有配置和脚本。

Abstract: User simulators are increasingly central to interactive information retrieval, yet the community lacks standardized evaluation tools. Simulators serve two objectives, behavioral realism (matching real user behavior) and tester reliability (producing valid system rankings), and these are often conflated despite being distinct and sometimes conflicting. We present SimEval-IR, an open-source toolkit and benchmark suite that makes this distinction measurable. SimEval-IR provides: (1) a canonical session schema unifying session search and conversational interactions, with validated dataset adapters and explicit loss accounting; (2) three executable benchmarks covering behavioral realism, tester reliability with RATE-style estimation, and an analysis linking the two; and (3) baseline results across four real datasets in two languages and four simulator families. Our key finding: the classifier-discriminator ''human-likeness'' check, the dominant realism test in the literature, has essentially no pooled predictive power for system-ranking validity ($r{=}{+}0.09$, $n{=}48$), while marginal click-depth distance and Fréchet distance over session embeddings give a much stronger signal ($|r|{=}0.43$ and $0.40$, $p{\leq}0.005$). SimEval-IR is released with all configurations and scripts to reproduce the reported analysis.

</details>


### [94] [Efficient Multivector Retrieval with Token-Aware Clustering and Hierarchical Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28142)
*Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TACHIOM是一种利用令牌级结构的多向量检索系统，通过分布感知质心分配和图索引技术，实现了聚类和检索阶段的大幅加速（分别可达247倍和9.8倍），在保持高精度的同时显著降低了部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多向量检索模型虽然效果领先，但部署成本高昂。现有基于k-means的解决方案存在计算复杂度高、大规模扩展困难，且训练过程中高频令牌占优、稀有区分性令牌被低估的问题。

Method: TACHIOM利用令牌级结构，通过分布感知的质心分配实现可扩展至数百万质心规模；结合基于图的质心索引与优化的乘积量化布局，在仅使用质心进行文档评分时即可保证准确性。

Result: 在MS-MARCOv1和LoTTE数据集上的实验表明，TACHIOM相比k-means实现最高247倍聚类加速，相比现有最优系统实现最高9.8倍检索加速，同时保持相当或更优的检索效果。

Conclusion: TACHIOM在保持同等或更优检索效果的同时，显著降低了计算和内存开销，为大规模多向量检索系统的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multivector retrieval models achieve state-of-the-art effectiveness through fine-grained token-level representations, but their deployment incurs substantial computational and memory costs. Current solutions, based on the well-known k-means clustering algorithm, group similar vectors together to enable both effective compression and efficient retrieval. However, standard k-means scales poorly with the number of clusters and dataset size, and favours frequent tokens during training while underrepresenting rare, discriminative ones. In this work, we introduce TACHIOM, a multivector retrieval system that exploits token-level structure to significantly accelerate both clustering and retrieval. By accounting for tokens' distribution during centroid allocation, TACHIOM easily scales to millions of centroids, enabling highly accurate document scoring using only centroids, avoiding expensive token-level computation. TACHIOM combines a graph-based index over centroids with an optimized Product Quantization layout for efficient final scoring. Experiments on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTE show that TACHIOM achieves up to $247\times$ faster clustering than k-means and up to $9.8\times$ retrieval speedup over state-of-the-art systems while maintaining comparable or superior effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Affinity Tailor: Dynamic Locality-Aware Scheduling at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27915)
*Jin Xin Ng,Ori Livneh,Richard O'Grady,Josh Don,Peng Ding,Samuel Grossman,Luis Otero,Chris Kennelly,David Lo,Carlos Villavieja*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出Affinity Tailor，一种基于关键洞察的用户空间引导内核调度系统：内核可通过将按需大小的拓扑紧凑CPU集合作为亲和性提示而非硬分区来保留共享CPU的工作负载局部性。用户空间控制器在线估算工作负载CPU需求，分配尽可能不相交且跨越最少LLC域的优选CPU集；内核使用该集合作为调度提示。部署于Google后，在chiplet和非chiplet系统上分别实现12%和3%的吞吐量提升，并表明未来调度器应将空间局部性作为一等目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大型多核系统运行多个共享CPU的工作负载时，Linux CFS等调度器为保持CPU忙碌会进行负载均衡，导致每个工作负载分散执行在多个核心上。这削弱了微架构层面的局部性：缓存、分支预测器和预取器的重用效率降低，工作负载间干扰加剧——尤其在基于chiplet的系统中，将执行分散到多个核心也意味着跨越多个LLC边界。严格的CPU分区是自然替代方案，但硬分区会在工作负载未充分利用保留CPU时造成容量闲置。

Method: 提出Affinity Tailor系统，采用用户空间引导的内核调度机制。核心设计：(1)将按需大小的拓扑紧凑CPU集合作为亲和性提示而非硬分区；(2)用户空间控制器在线估算各工作负载的CPU需求，分配与其需求匹配的优选CPU集合；(3)优选集合尽可能与其他工作负载的集合保持不相交，并尽量跨越最少的LLC域；(4)内核将该集合作为亲和性提示，将线程引导至这些CPU，同时在需要时仍允许在其他CPU上执行以保持利用率。

Result: 在Google部署后，相比Linux CFS：chiplet系统上几何平均每核吞吐量提升12%，非chiplet系统提升3%；更快的执行速度减少了内存占用，进一步获得每GB吞吐量3-7%的提升。

Conclusion: 未来调度器应将空间局部性作为一等目标，即使牺牲工作守恒原则。Affinity Tailor已部署于Google生产环境，在chiplet系统上实现12%的几何平均吞吐量提升，在非chiplet系统上实现3%提升，同时因执行速度加快使内存占用降低，进一步获得每GB 3-7%的吞吐量提升。

Abstract: Modern large multicore systems often run multiple workloads that share CPUs under schedulers such as Linux CFS. To keep CPUs busy, these schedulers load-balance runnable work, causing each workload to execute on many cores. This weakens locality at the microarchitectural level: workloads lose reuse in caches, branch predictors, and prefetchers, and interfere more with one another - especially on chiplet-based systems, where spreading execution across cores also spreads it across LLC boundaries. A natural alternative is strict CPU partitioning, but hard partitions leave capacity idle when workloads do not fully use their reserved CPUs.
  We present Affinity Tailor, a userspace-guided kernel scheduling system built on a key insight: the kernel can preserve locality for workloads that share CPUs by treating demand-sized, topologically compact CPU sets as affinity hints rather than hard partitions. A userspace controller estimates each workload's CPU demand online and assigns a preferred CPU set sized to that demand, chosen to be as disjoint as possible from other workloads while spanning as few LLC domains as possible. The kernel then uses this set as an affinity hint, steering threads toward those CPUs while still allowing execution elsewhere when needed to preserve utilization. Deployed at Google, Affinity Tailor delivers geometric-mean per-CPU throughput gains of 12% on chiplet-based systems and 3% on non-chiplet systems over Linux CFS. Furthermore, faster execution reduces memory residency, yielding per-GB throughput gains of 3-7%. Our findings suggest that future schedulers should treat spatial locality as a first-class objective, even at the expense of work-conservation.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Graphify: Automated Synthesis of Type-Safe Graph Backends via $O(S)$ GraphQL-to-Gremlin Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27223)
*Johannes Graf*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Graphify是一个端到端框架，通过可视化的方式建模图数据模式，并自动生成完全可用、类型安全的图数据库后端。该系统提出GraphQL到Gremlin遍历的正式映射，采用递归状态机算法（时间复杂度O(S)）将复杂GraphQL查询转译为单一优化的Gremlin查询，支持嵌套谓词、多键排序和分页等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph databases offer flexibility for managing interconnected data but lack strict schema enforcement, leading to runtime uncertainties and complex query development.

Method: The paper proposes a formal mapping from GraphQL artifacts to Gremlin traversal machine, using a recursive state machine algorithm that processes GraphQL ASTs with O(S) linear complexity relative to selected fields. The system generates a transpiler that converts complex GraphQL requests into optimized single Gremlin queries with features like nested predicates, multi-key sorting, and pagination.

Result: Empirical evaluation using MovieLens 100k dataset and formal complexity analysis demonstrate practical efficiency and theoretical robustness of the approach.

Conclusion: Graphify bridges the gap between flexible graph storage and type-safe API consumption, enabling graph interface generation in minutes.

Abstract: Graph databases offer unparalleled flexibility for managing interconnected data, yet the lack of strict schema enforcement often leads to runtime uncertainties and complex query development. This paper introduces Graphify, an end-to-end framework that enables developers to visually model graph data schemas and automatically synthesize a fully functional, type-safe backend. This paper proposes a formal mapping of GraphQL artifacts to the Gremlin traversal machine, supporting complete CRUD operations and arbitrarily nested queries. The system generates a transpiler capable of converting complex GraphQL requests into a single, optimized Gremlin query, including advanced features such as nested logical predicates, multi-key sorting, and pagination. At the core of the framework is a recursive state machine algorithm that processes GraphQL Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) with linear time complexity $O(S)$ relative to the number of selected fields. This paper demonstrates the practical efficiency and theoretical robustness of the approach through formal complexity analysis and empirical evaluation using the MovieLens 100k dataset. The result is a system that enables the generation of graph interfaces in minutes, bridging the gap between flexible graph storage and type-safe API consumption.

</details>


### [97] [Unified Data Discovery across Query Modalities and User Intents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27252)
*Tingting Wang,Shixun Huang,Zhifeng Bao,J. Shane Culpepper,Shazia Sadiq,Volkan Dedeoglu,Reza Arablouei*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: UniDisc是一个统一的数据发现框架，通过异构图和双视图邻居聚合学习跨模态统一表示，支持自然语言和表格两种查询模态，并在有限标注数据条件下利用数据湖中的上下文信号实现泛化的相关性评估，在7个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据发现方法仅针对单一查询模态（自然语言或表格）或特定用户意图设计，泛化能力受限。而实际应用需要同时支持多种查询模态和多样化意图（如开放域 enrichment、任务驱动推理、表问答和事实验证）。

Method: UniDisc构建异构图将自然语言语句和表格建模为节点，通过双视图邻居聚合（dual-view neighbor aggregation）和联合优化学习鲁棒的上下文感知表示。该表示支持检索阶段的灵活相关性估计，无需意图特定的表示或建模。

Result: 在7个数据集上的实验表明，UniDisc在自然语言查询和表格查询两种数据发现任务上均一致性地优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: UniDisc通过统一表示学习解决跨模态、跨意图的数据发现问题，在无需特定意图建模的情况下实现灵活的相关性估计，证明了基于上下文信号的表示学习在数据发现任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Data discovery - retrieving relevant tables from a data lake in response to user queries - is a fundamental building block for downstream analytics. In practice, data discovery must support different query modalities, including natural language (NL) statements and tables, and accommodate diverse user intents, ranging from open-ended enrichment to task-driven inference for applications such as table question answering and fact verification. However, most existing methods are designed for a single query modality or a specific user intent, limiting their generalizability. We propose UniDisc, a unified data discovery framework that supports both NL statements and tables as queries and generalizes across diverse user intents without intent-specific representations or relevance modeling. UniDisc learns a common cross-modal representation model that produces unified representations for queries of different modalities and candidate tables, enabling uniform relevance assessment across discovery scenarios. Since learning such a model typically requires large labeled collections of query-table pairs, which are expensive to obtain, UniDisc instead exploits contextual signals naturally available in data lakes. Specifically, it models NL statements and tables as nodes in a heterogeneous graph with multiple edge types, and applies dual-view neighbor aggregation and joint optimization to learn robust, context-aware representations under limited supervision. These representations support flexible relevance estimation during retrieval. Experiments on seven datasets show that UniDisc consistently outperforms strong baselines on both NL- and table-based discovery.

</details>


### [98] [SynSQL: Synthesizing Relational Databases for Robust Evaluation of Text-to-SQL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27261)
*Mohammadamin Habibollah,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出SynSQL框架，通过问题-模式对齐条件合成测试数据库来评估文本到SQL系统。框架包含模式选择、问题引导数据合成、约束感知批判三阶段。在10个模型和3个基准测试上的实验表明，合成数据库能揭示3-14%的额外错误，证明了现有评估方法的局限性，并为研究LLM在结构化环境中的能力提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到SQL系统的评估存在根本性缺陷：仅在单一静态数据库上执行预测SQL与金标准SQL进行正确性判断，但相同SQL在不同数据库实例下可能表现出不同的行为。这导致评估无法发现被基准测试人工产物（artifact）所掩盖的错误。核心问题是LLM能否直接从自然语言问题合成语义一致、模式一致的关联数据。

Method: SynSQL采用三阶段结构化生成框架：(1) 模式选择，从候选模式中选择相关表和字段；(2) 问题引导的数据合成，根据选定模式和自然语言问题生成数据库内容；(3) 约束感知批判与迭代细化，通过语义和关联约束验证生成数据的质量，确保schema一致性和问题相关性。整个过程将数据库构建形式化为结构化生成任务。

Result: 实验覆盖10个文本到SQL模型（Spider、BIRD、Spider 2.0），SynSQL生成的数据库揭示了相对于静态评估3-14%的性能下降，成功暴露了被基准测试伪影掩盖的错误。研究还分析了生成质量、约束遵守情况和失败模式。

Conclusion: SynSQL框架通过问题-模式对齐而非金标准SQL来合成测试数据库，在Spider、BIRD和Spider 2.0三个基准上揭示了10个文本到SQL模型3-14%的性能下降，有效暴露了基准测试伪影掩盖的错误。该工作将合成数据库生成定位为研究LLM在结构化环境中推理能力、可控性和鲁棒性的新视角。

Abstract: Evaluating text-to-SQL systems remains largely fragile: correctness is typically judged by executing predicted and gold SQL queries on a single static database, even though the same queries may behave differently under alternative database instances. This raises a broader language modeling question: Can large language models synthesize semantically meaningful, schema-consistent relational data directly from a natural language question? If so, such generation can serve as a controlled mechanism for stress-testing text-to-SQL systems beyond fixed benchmark databases. We introduce SynSQL, a framework that synthesizes test databases conditioned on question-schema alignment rather than gold SQL queries. SynSQL decomposes the task into three stages: (1) schema selection, (2) question-guided data synthesis, and (3) constraint-aware critique with iterative refinement, framing database construction as structured generation under semantic and relational constraints. Across ten text-to-SQL models on Spider, BIRD, and Spider 2.0, SynSQL-generated databases reveal performance drops of 3-14% compared to static evaluation, exposing errors masked by benchmark artifacts. We further analyze generation quality, constraint adherence, and failure modes, highlighting both the promise and limitations of LLMs in structured data synthesis. Our findings position synthetic database generation as a new lens for studying LLM reasoning, controllability, and robustness in structured environments.

</details>


### [99] [Index-Assisted Stratified Sampling for Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28141)
*Yunnan Yu,Zhuoyue Zhao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于在线聚合的索引辅助分层采样方法，通过两阶段采样框架解决近似查询处理中高方差数据导致的高采样成本问题。该方法在第一阶段同时执行在线聚合和优化未来采样成本，第二阶段使用优化后的分层继续聚合。实验表明该方法相比均匀采样最高提升3倍，相比基于扫描的分层采样最高提升98708倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时数据分析中对频繁更新数据的即席查询需要极低延迟。现有索引辅助S-AQP系统虽然支持在线聚合，但当数据分布方差大或选择性强时，为达到目标置信区间所需的采样成本过高，导致查询延迟增加。经典Neyman分层采样虽理论上可最小化样本量，但在索引辅助S-AQP系统中面临三个挑战：需要先验统计、优化成本高、以及基于样本量的采样成本模型不准确。

Method: 设计了索引辅助分层采样框架，采用两阶段采样策略。第一阶段样本同时用于在线聚合和优化未来采样成本；第二阶段使用优化后的分层继续在线聚合。针对索引采样成本模型，证明了最优分层和样本量分配策略。设计了贪心算法和动态规划算法以平衡优化成本与成本降低效果。

Result: 在多个真实和合成数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法持续获得良好加速效果。在极端情况下，相比索引辅助均匀采样实现最高3倍加速，相比基于扫描的分层采样实现最高98708倍加速。

Conclusion: 两阶段采样框架结合针对索引采样成本模型的最优分层策略，能够有效降低近似查询的采样成本，在保持置信区间保证的同时显著提升查询响应速度。

Abstract: Ad-hoc queries over frequently updated data in a flat schema are common in real-time data analysis applications and often require very low latency. Online aggregation can achieve so by providing approximate aggregation answers with confidence bound guarantees. It relies on the ability to draw samples online in a linear time to sample size rather than database size, which can be supported by index-assisted Sampling-based Approximate Query Processing (S-AQP) systems. However, the query latencies of approximate queries in these systems can still suffer from excessive sampling cost required to achieve a desired confidence bound, due to increased sample size for data with high variance in value distribution and selectivity. Classic stratified sampling methods with Neyman allocation can minimize sample size in theory, but several challenges prevent it from being applicable in index-assisted S-AQP systems, including requiring apriori statistics, high optimization cost, and inaccurate sampling cost model based on sample size. Towards that, we design index-assisted stratified sampling for online aggregation, which features a two-phase sampling framework. Samples drawn from first phase are used for both online aggregation and optimizing future sampling cost, while the second phase continues the online aggregation using the optimized strata. We prove optimal stratification and sample size allocation strategies for index-based sampling cost model, and design several greedy and dynamic programming based optimization methods to balance optimization cost and effectiveness in cost reduction. We evaluate our methods on several real-world and synthetic datasets and queries, and the results show ours consistently achieve good speedup and, in extreme cases, up to 3x speedup and 98708x speedup, when compared to index-assisted uniform sampling and classic scan-based stratified sampling respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985)
*Michael Cardei,Huu Binh Ta,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCMDM，一种用于掩码扩散模型的后训练自适应方法，通过在每个去噪步骤中整合模型自身先前的干净状态预测来改进生成质量。与需要从头训练的partial self-conditioning不同，该方法发现后训练场景下专门化refinement优于混合条件与非条件目标，在保持最小架构改动和无额外采样开销的前提下，在文本、图像、分子和基因组等多个领域取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型中，若token在逆向更新后仍保持掩码状态，其干净状态预测将被丢弃。该设计导致仍处于掩码状态的token只能从mask token本身重复推断，限制了跨步精细化(cross-step refinement)能力。现有partial self-conditioning方法虽能缓解此问题，但需要从头开始的昂贵模型训练。

Method: 提出自条件掩码扩散模型(SCMDM)，在每个去噪步骤中基于模型先前的干净状态预测进行条件化。核心发现：在后训练场景下，一旦模型的自我生成干净状态估计变得信息丰富，专门针对refinement的特殊化优于混合条件和非条件目标（这与训练时需要50% dropout的partial self-conditioning不同）。该方法仅需最小架构修改，无循环隐状态路径，无辅助参考模型，采样时无额外去噪评估。

Result: 在多个领域实现一致改进：OWT训练模型的生成困惑度降低近50%（42.89→23.72）；离散化图像合成质量显著提升；小分子生成和基因组分布建模的保真度增强。

Conclusion: SCMDM通过自生成干净状态估计的专门化 refinement（而非混合条件与非条件目标），在多个领域实现了一致的改进，证明了后训练自适应范式的有效性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete sequences by iterative denoising under an absorbing masking process. In standard masked diffusion, if a token remains masked after a reverse update, the model discards its clean-state prediction for that position. Thus, still-masked positions must be repeatedly inferred from the mask token alone. This design choice limits cross-step refinement. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a simple, yet effective, post-training adaptation for MDMs that conditions each denoising step on the model's own previous clean-state predictions. The resulting method, called Self-Conditioned Masked Diffusion Models (SCMDM), requires minimal architectural change, does not introduce a recurrent latent-state pathway, does not rely on an auxiliary reference model, and adds no extra denoiser evaluations during sampling. This is an important departure from partial self-conditioning approaches which requires expensive model training from scratch. In particular, the paper shows that partial self-conditioning, including the commonly used 50% dropout strategy for training self-conditioned models from scratch, is suboptimal in the post-training regime. Instead, once the model's self-generated clean-state estimates become informative, the specialization to refinement is preferable to mixing conditional and unconditional objectives. SCMDM is evaluated across multiple domains, demonstrating consistent improvement over vanilla MDM baselines, achieving nearly a 50% reduction in generative perplexity on OWT-trained models (42.89 to 23.72), alongside strong improvements in discretized image synthesis quality, small molecular generation, and enhanced fidelity in genomic distribution modeling.

</details>


### [101] [People-Centred Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991)
*Zheng Zhang,Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Tahir Hassan,Yuanhong Chen,David Rosewarne,Kevin Wells,Thanh-Toan Do,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PecMan框架，通过动态门控机制在临床医生工作量约束下联合优化医学AI的公平性、准确性和工作流有效性，并引入FairHAI基准评估三者权衡。实验证明PecMan性能优于现有方法，推动更值得信赖且临床可行的AI系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的医学AI诊断系统虽准确率高，但面临两大问题：1）性能偏差可能导致监管障碍；2）工作流集成不良会破坏临床流程、降低人机协作质量并减少临床医生采用AI的意愿。现有研究孤立处理公平性和工作流集成问题，忽视了它们的相互依赖性及临床环境的实际约束（如临床医生时间有限）。

Method: 提出PecMan框架，通过动态门控机制根据临床医生工作量约束将病例分配给AI、临床医生或两者协作；同时构建FairHAI基准用于评估准确性、公平性和临床医生工作量的权衡。

Result: 实验结果表明，PecMan在公平性、准确性和工作流效率的综合权衡上始终优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PecMan框架通过动态门控机制，在临床工作流约束下联合优化公平性、准确性和工作流效率，实验表明其性能优于现有方法，为更值得信赖且临床可行的AI系统提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-centric medical AI have produced highly accurate diagnostic systems, but the emphasis on data curation and performance metrics has not translated into widespread clinical adoption. We conjecture that this limited uptake stems from insufficient attention dedicated to the optimisation of fair performance across diverse patient populations and to workflow integration: performance biases can create regulatory barriers, and poorly integrated automation can disrupt clinical routines, degrade the quality of human-AI collaboration, and reduce clinicians' willingness to adopt AI tools. Prior work on workflow integration (e.g., Learning to Defer (L2D) and Learning to Complement (L2C)) and AI fairness has typically examined these challenges in isolation, overlooking their natural interdependence and the practical constraints of clinical environments, such as restricted clinician availability. We propose People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan), a human-AI framework that jointly optimises fairness, diagnostic accuracy, and workflow effectiveness through a dynamic gating mechanism that assigns cases to AI, clinicians, or both under clinician workload constraints. We also introduce the Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI) benchmark for evaluating trade-offs between accuracy, fairness, and clinician workload. Experiments using this benchmark show that PecMan consistently outperforms existing methods, paving the way for more trustworthy and clinically viable AI systems. Code will be available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [102] [When Continual Learning Moves to Memory: A Study of Experience Reuse in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27003)
*Qisheng Hu,Quanyu Long,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了外部记忆增强LLM智能体中，持续学习瓶颈从参数更新转移至记忆访问层面的现象，发现记忆表征与组织方式的设计存在前向迁移与遗忘之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存增强型LLM智能体看似通过外部记忆规避了参数更新的稳定性-可塑性困境，但作者认为该挑战并未消失，而是在记忆层面重新浮现。

Method: 提出(k,v)框架解耦外部记忆的两个设计轴心：经验表示方式与检索组织方式；在ALFWorld和BabyAI环境中进行顺序任务实验。

Result: 抽象程序性记忆比详细轨迹具有更强的迁移可靠性；负迁移对困难案例的伤害尤为严重；更细粒度的记忆组织并非普遍有益，可同时导致前向迁移强但遗忘严重。

Conclusion: 外部记忆并未解决持续学习问题，而是将其重新塑造为记忆表征与检索设计的挑战；精细化的记忆组织并非普遍有益，面向前向迁移的设计可能同时引发严重遗忘。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents offer an appealing shortcut to continual learning: rather than updating model parameters, they accumulate experience in external memory, seemingly sidestepping the stability-plasticity dilemma of parametric learning. We show that this challenge does not disappear but resurfaces at the memory level. Under a limited context window, old and new experiences compete during retrieval, relocating the continual-learning bottleneck from parameter updates to memory access. To study this phenomenon, we introduce a (k,v) framework that disentangles two fundamental design axes of external memory: how experience is represented and how it is organized for retrieval. Across sequential-task experiments in ALFWorld and BabyAI, we find that abstract procedural memories transfer more reliably than detailed trajectories, while negative transfer disproportionately harms the hard cases. Moreover, finer-grained memory organization is not universally beneficial: designs that yield strong forward transfer can simultaneously induce severe forgetting. Together, these results reveal that external memory does not resolve the continual-learning problem; it reshapes it into a problem of memory representation and retrieval design.

</details>


### [103] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairMind工具，通过整合因果公平性模型与LLM技术，实现自动化数据集公平性分析，在零样本设置下展示了对直接LLM分析的优势，并支持扩展到序数保护变量和连续目标场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数AutoML框架未充分考虑训练数据和预测结果中可能存在的不公平性问题，而公平性对于AI技术的普及应用至关重要。

Method: FairMind基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的"标准公平性模型"，通过反事实查询对目标变量、保护特征、混杂因素和中介变量进行因果效应分析。在数据预处理后，实现了封闭形式的效应计算，并利用LLM在零样本设置下生成准确的分析报告。

Result: 实验验证了相比直接由LLM进行公平性分析，FairMind在零样本设置下能够生成更准确的分析报告。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了FairMind系统，通过扩展到序数保护变量和连续目标，并引入新的分解结果，进一步拓展了公平性分析的适用范围，为AutoML框架的公平性保障提供了重要贡献。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [104] [Fidelity, Diversity, and Privacy: A Multi-Dimensional LLM Evaluation for Clinical Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27014)
*Guillermo Iglesias,Gema Bello-Orgaz,María Navas-Loro,Cristian Ramirez-Atencia,Mercè Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用LLM（DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3、Qwen3.5）基于ICD-10编码生成合成心理健康报告，评估显示生成的文本在临床一致性、词汇多样性和隐私安全性方面均表现良好，可有效扩展临床NLP训练数据来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量标注医疗数据（尤其是心理健康领域）严重稀缺，加之隐私法规限制数据共享，成为训练机器学习模型的瓶颈，因此需要探索合成数据生成作为替代方案。

Method: 采用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen3.5三种大语言模型，基于ICD-10编码条件生成合成心理健康评估报告，并通过语义保真度、词汇多样性和隐私/抄袭三个维度进行综合评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，所有模型均能生成临床一致性良好、词汇多样性丰富且隐私安全的合成报告。

Conclusion: 研究证明了LLM生成的合成报告能够有效扩展临床NLP训练数据规模，同时不危及患者隐私安全，为医疗数据短缺问题提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated medical data, particularly in mental health, poses a significant bottleneck for training robust machine learning models. Privacy regulations restrict data sharing, making synthetic data generation a promising alternative. The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in a data augmentation pipeline could be leveraged as an alternative in this field. In the proposed methodology, DeepSeek-R1, OpenBioLLM-Llama3 and Qwen 3.5 are used to generate synthetic mental health evaluation reports conditioned on specific International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision (ICD-10) codes. Because naive text generation can lead to mode collapse or privacy breaches (memorization), a comprehensive evaluation framework is introduced. The generated diagnostic texts are assessed across three dimensions: semantic fidelity, lexical diversity, and privacy/plagiarism. The results demonstrate that all models can generate clinically coherent, diverse, and privacy-safe synthetic reports, significantly expanding the available training data for clinical natural language processing tasks without compromising patient confidentiality.

</details>


### [105] [NORACL: Neurogenesis for Oracle-free Resource-Adaptive Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27031)
*Karthik Charan Raghunathan,Christian Metzner,Laura Kriener,Melika Payvand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NORACL addresses the stability-plasticity dilemma in continual learning through adaptive neurogenesis-inspired neuronal growth. By dynamically expanding a compact network only when saturation signals indicate need, it achieves oracle-level performance with fewer parameters and interpretable growth patterns.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The stability-plasticity dilemma in continual learning has an architectural root. Fixed-capacity networks cannot handle unknown future task streams — they run out of capacity for dissimilar tasks or are over-provisioned for overlapping ones, requiring an oracle to know the optimal architecture size.

Method: NORACL monitors two complementary signals (representational and plasticity saturation) to grow neurons only when needed, enabling adaptive capacity expansion.

Result: NORACL matches or exceeds oracle-provisioned static baselines across varying task counts and geometries while using fewer parameters. Growth patterns are interpretable: dissimilar tasks expand feature-extraction layers, while overlapping tasks shift growth toward feature-combination layers.

Conclusion: NORACL pushes the stability-plasticity Pareto frontier in continual learning.

Abstract: In a continual learning setting, we require a model to be plastic enough to learn a new task and stable enough to not disturb previously learned capabilities. We argue that this dilemma has an architectural root. A finite network has limited representational and plastic resources, yet the required capacity depends on properties of the future task stream that are unknown: how many tasks will be encountered, and how much they overlap in feature space. Regularization-based methods preserve past knowledge within fixed-capacity architectures and therefore implicitly rely on an oracle architecture sized for this unknown future. When tasks are only weakly related, fixed architectures progressively run out of plastic resources; when tasks are few or strongly overlapping, models are often over-provisioned. Inspired by neurogenesis in biology, we propose NORACL to address the stability-plasticity dilemma by tackling the oracle architecture problem through neuronal growth. Starting from a compact network, NORACL grows only when needed by monitoring two complementary signals for representational and plasticity saturation. We evaluate NORACL against oracle-sized static baselines across varying task counts and geometries. Across all settings, NORACL achieves final average accuracies that are better than or on par with oracle-provisioned static baselines while using fewer parameters. Additionally, NORACL yields architectures with interpretable growth, i.e. dissimilar tasks predominantly expand feature-extraction layers, whereas tasks which rely on common features shift growth toward later feature-combination layers. Our analysis further explains why fixed-capacity networks lose plasticity as tasks accumulate, whereas NORACL creates fresh capacity for new tasks through growth. Together, these results show that adaptive neurogenesis pushes the stability-plasticity Pareto frontier of continual learning.

</details>


### [106] [Cross-Subject Generalization for EEG Decoding: A Survey of Deep Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27033)
*Taida Li,Yujun Yan,Fei Dou,Wenzhan Song,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于跨被试EEG解码深度学习方法的综合综述，将该问题形式化为多源域问题，建立了主题无关评估协议，系统分类了feature alignment、adversarial learning、feature disentanglement、contrastive learning等方法，并探讨了理论局限性、被试身份价值和EEG基础模型三个前沿方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在跨被试EEG解码任务中面临严重挑战，高被试间变异性导致训练集与测试集之间存在显著的域偏移问题。

Method: 采用形式化方法将跨被试设置建模为多源域问题，建立严格的主题无关评估协议，并系统地将现有文献归类为不同的方法论家族。

Result: 提出了一套系统化的方法论分类体系，涵盖特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习等关键技术路径。

Conclusion: 本文为跨被试EEG解码的深度学习方法提供了系统化分类框架，明确了feature alignment、adversarial learning、feature disentanglement、contrastive learning等方法家族，并指出未来研究方向聚焦于理论局限性突破、被试身份的结构化利用及EEG基础模型的兴起。

Abstract: Deep learning for cross-subject EEG decoding is hindered by high inter-subject variability, which introduces a severe domain shift between training and unseen test subjects. This survey presents a comprehensive review of deep learning methodologies specifically engineered to address this cross-subject generalization challenge. To ground this analysis, we formalize the cross-subject setting as a multi-source domain problem and delineate the rigorous, subject-independent evaluation protocols required for valid assessment. Central to this survey is a systematic taxonomy of the current literature into discrete methodological families, including feature alignment, adversarial learning, feature disentanglement, and contrastive learning. We conclude by examining three critical elements for advancing robust, real-world decoding: the theoretical limitations of current methodologies, the structural value of subject identity, and the emergence of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [107] [Detecting Clinical Discrepancies in Health Coaching Agents: A Dual-Stream Memory and Reconciliation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27045)
*Samuel L Pugh,Eric Yang,Alexander Muir Sutherland,Alessandra Breschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出双流记忆架构解决LLM医疗智能体中的记忆冲突问题，通过分离患者叙述与FHIR临床记录并使用专门的协调引擎评估差异。实验表明该架构可检测84.4%的临床差异，13.6%的错误源于记忆提取阶段丢失的临床细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体从单次会话工具演变为管理纵向医疗旅程的持久系统，传统记忆架构在处理两种不完美信息源时存在安全风险。患者的自我报告虽新颖但易产生回忆偏差，而EHR虽经医学验证但常存在时效性问题。现有通用智能体记忆系统通过用最新陈述覆盖旧事实来优化一致性，但这种模式应用于临床数据时存在安全隐患。

Method: 双流记忆架构（Dual-Stream Memory Architecture）：严格分离患者叙述流与结构化临床记录（FHIR）；由专门的协调引擎（Reconciliation Engine）驱动，对每条提取记忆进行评估，按类型、严重程度及涉及的具体FHIR资源进行差异分类。

Result: 独立测试中，引擎检测到84.4%的设计临床差异，安全关键召回率达86.7%。通过在同一数据上耦合提取与协调评估，直接量化了13.6%的错误级联效应，并将性能退化归因于从非结构化对话中提取记忆时丢失的临床细节，而非下游分类错误。

Conclusion: 验证患者自报告记忆与临床记录的匹配对于纵向健康智能体的安全部署既可行又必要。该研究确立了双流记忆架构的实用价值，为医疗AI系统的安全部署提供了重要参考。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents transition from single-session tools to persistent systems managing longitudinal healthcare journeys, their memory architectures face a critical challenge: reconciling two imperfect sources of truth. The patient's evolving self-report is current but prone to recall bias, while the Electronic Health Record (EHR) is medically validated but frequently stale. General-purpose agent memory systems optimize for coherence by overwriting older facts with the user's latest statement, a pattern that risks safety failures when applied to clinical data. We introduce a Dual-Stream Memory Architecture that strictly separates the patient narrative from the structured clinical record (FHIR), governed by a dedicated Reconciliation Engine that evaluates every extracted memory against the patient's FHIR profile and classifies discrepancies by type, severity, and the specific FHIR resources involved. We evaluate this architecture on 26 patients across 675 longitudinal wellness coaching sessions, using a hybrid dataset that interleaves real provider-patient transcripts with synthetic, FHIR-grounded clinical scenarios. In isolated testing, the engine detects 84.4% of designed clinical discrepancies with 86.7% safety-critical recall. By coupling extraction and reconciliation evaluation on the same data, we directly quantify a 13.6% error cascade, tracing the degradation to clinical details lost during memory extraction from unstructured conversation rather than to downstream classification errors. These findings establish that validating patient-reported memories against clinical records is both feasible and necessary for safe deployment of longitudinal health agents.

</details>


### [108] [Co-Evolving Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27083)
*Naibin Gu,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Chuanyu Qin,Dingyu Yao,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了RLVR和OPD两种后训练范式在整合多专家能力时的各自缺陷，提出CoPD方法——通过并行训练专家并让它们互为教师进行双向知识蒸馏，使各专家行为模式保持一致的同时维持充分的互补知识。实验表明该方法能实现文本、图像、视频推理能力的一体化整合，性能超越强基线甚至领域专家，且其模型并行训练模式为训练规模化开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种后训练范式在整合多专家能力时存在各自缺陷：混合RLVR遭受"能力间差异成本"问题，不同能力间存在行为偏差；先训练专家再进行OPD的流水线方式虽避免差异但因师生行为模式差距过大，无法充分吸收教师能力。

Method: 提出Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD)方法：1) 鼓励专家并行训练而非顺序训练；2) 在每个专家的RLVR训练过程中持续引入OPD，而非训练完成后再进行；3) 实现双向知识蒸馏——各专家互为教师，相互学习，共同进化。

Result: 实验验证CoPD成功实现文本、图像、视频推理能力的"全合一"整合，显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线，并超越领域特定专家的性能表现。

Conclusion: CoPD通过双向知识蒸馏和并行训练范式，不仅实现了文本、图像、视频推理能力的一体化整合，显著超越混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线方法，甚至超过领域特定专家模型。更重要的是，这种模型并行训练模式为训练规模化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: RLVR and OPD have become standard paradigms for post-training. We provide a unified analysis of these two paradigms in consolidating multiple expert capabilities into a single model, identifying capability loss in different ways: mixed RLVR suffers from inter-capability divergence cost, while the pipeline of first training experts and then performing OPD, though avoiding divergence, fails to fully absorb teacher capabilities due to large behavioral pattern gaps between teacher and student. We propose Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD), which encourages parallel training of experts and introduces OPD during each expert's ongoing RLVR training rather than after complete expert training, with experts serving as mutual teachers (making OPD bidirectional) to co-evolve. This enables more consistent behavioral patterns among experts while maintaining sufficient complementary knowledge throughout. Experiments validate that CoPD achieves all-in-one integration of text, image, and video reasoning capabilities, significantly outperforming strong baselines such as mixed RLVR and MOPD, and even surpassing domain-specific experts. The model parallel training pattern offered by CoPD may inspire a novel training scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [109] [AutoSP: Unlocking Long-Context LLM Training Via Compiler-Based Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27089)
*Ahan Gupta,Zhihao Wang,Neel Dani,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSP是首个用于自动优化LLM长上下文训练的解决方案，通过自动序列并行和长上下文感知激活检查点技术，在NVIDIA/AMD平台上实现2.5-2.7倍的上下文扩展，同时保持几乎零性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM训练库缺乏针对长上下文场景的易用抽象，主要关注模型并行（ZeRO-3/FSDP、Tensor/Pipeline parallelism）而非序列级并行优化。用户需手动重写训练代码以集成序列并行等复杂长上下文优化，导致开发效率低下。

Method: AutoSP employs automated sequence parallelism and long-context aware activation checkpointing as its core optimization techniques, compiling models with targeted optimizations to enhance trainability for extended contexts.

Result: 在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上验证，AutoSP分别实现最高2.7倍和2.5倍上下文扩展能力，且对运行时性能影响微乎其微。

Conclusion: AutoSP provides a cross-platform (NVIDIA/AMD) solution that enables LLM training with up to 2.5-2.7x longer contexts while maintaining runtime performance, demonstrating the feasibility of automated optimization for long-context scenarios.

Abstract: Large-language-models (LLMs) demonstrate enormous utility in long-context tasks which require processing prompts that consist of tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. However, existing LLM training libraries do not provide easy to use abstractions to optimize for long-context training, instead focusing on optimizations for models with large parameter counts through ZeRO-3/FSDP, Tensor and Pipeline parallelism. This forces users to rewrite LLM training libraries to incorporate compositions of various complex long-context optimizations, such as sequence-parallelism, to training pipelines; a process that requires in-depth expertise, reducing developer productivity. To tackle these challenges, we introduce AutoSP: the first automated solution to automatically optimize LLM training for longer-contexts. AutoSP compiles models and applies a targeted set of optimizations: automated sequence parallelism, and long-context aware activation-checkpointing, to drastically enhance LLM trainability at negligible cost to throughput. Our evaluation demonstrates AutoSP's capability on both NVIDIA and AMD hardware, increasing training contexts by upto 2.7$\times$ and 2.5$\times$ respectively over competitive hand-written baseline at negligible cost to runtime performance.

</details>


### [110] [Anomaly Detection in Soil Heavy Metal Contamination Using Unsupervised Learning for Environmental Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27102)
*Isaac Tettey Adjokatse,Samuel Senyo Koranteng,George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng,Joseph Bremang Tandoh,Kow Ahor Essel-Yorke,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Soil contamination by heavy metals poses a persistent environmental and public health concern in rapidly urbanising regions of Ghana, particularly at unregulated waste disposal sites. This study applies an unsupervised machine learning framework to detect and characterise anomalous heavy metal contamination patterns in soils from twelve waste sites and residential controls in the Central Region, of Ghana. Concentrations of eight metals (As, Cd, Cr, Cu, Hg, Ni, Pb, Zn) were analysed alongside standard health risk indices, including the Hazard Index (HI) and Incremental Lifetime Cancer Risk (ILCR). Isolation Forest and PCA reconstruction error each identified $12$ anomalous samples ($15.4\%$ of $78$ samples), while DBSCAN detected no density-isolated noise points. A consensus approach isolated six robust anomalies ($7.7\%)$, all spatially concentrated at a single site (S3). Anomalies exhibited approximately $70$--$80\%$ higher mean HI values than normal samples, with all consensus anomalies exceeding the HI$=1$ threshold. PCA reconstruction error showed a strong positive association with HI ($r \approx 0.8$), indicating consistency between multivariate deviation and health risk. Three distinct anomaly types were identified: extreme Cu enrichment at S3, anomalously low Ni at S4/S5, and moderate multi-metal (Pb--Zn) co-elevation at S9--S12. The results demonstrate that unsupervised machine learning provides granular, objective insight beyond aggregate indices, enabling targeted site prioritisation and risk-informed environmental management.

</details>


### [111] [Better Models, Faster Training: Sigmoid Attention for single-cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27124)
*Vijay Sadashivaiah,Georgios Dasoulas,Judith Mueller,Soumya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用sigmoid注意力替换softmax可提升生物基础模型的稳定性、训练速度和表示质量：细胞类型分离度提高25%，训练速度快10%，且在160M参数模型8K序列压力测试中，softmax梯度爆炸而sigmoid保持稳定。理论层面，sigmoid注意力导数有界(≤0.25)且雅可比矩阵为对角结构。该工作开源了TritonSigmoid实现515 TFLOPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training stable biological foundation models requires rethinking attention mechanisms due to inherent instabilities in softmax attention.

Method: Sigmoid attention as drop-in replacement for softmax; theoretical analysis of derivatives (globally bounded ≤0.25) and Jacobian structure (diagonal vs dense); GPU kernel implementation with Triton.

Result: 25% higher cell-type separation, better cohesion metrics, lower validation loss, 10% faster training; stress test: 160M-param models on 8K-token sequences without gradient clipping—softmax diverged (gradients exploded 4 orders of magnitude) while sigmoid remained stable.

Conclusion: Sigmoid attention is both theoretically grounded and empirically superior for biological foundation models; implemented TritonSigmoid achieving 515 TFLOPS on H100, outperforming FlashAttention-2 and FlashSigmoid.

Abstract: Training stable biological foundation models requires rethinking attention mechanisms: we find that using sigmoid attention as a drop in replacement for softmax attention a) produces better learned representations: on six diverse single-cell datasets, sigmoid achieves 25% higher cell-type separation, better cell-type cohesion metrics, and lower validation loss, b) faster training, models with sigmoid attention train up to 10% faster than their softmax counterparts, and c) more stable training by eliminating inherent sources of instability in softmax attention. We establish that sigmoid attention has globally bounded derivatives ($\leq 0.25$) as opposed to softmax, and a diagonal Jacobian structure in contrast with softmax's dense coupling, which together help alleviate training instabilities. In stress tests on 160M-parameter bidirectional attention models trained without gradient clipping on 8K-token sequences, softmax diverges catastrophically, with gradients exploding by four orders of magnitude, while sigmoid remains stable. Finally, we implement and open-source TritonSigmoid, an efficient GPU kernel that achieves 515 TFLOPS on H100 GPUs, outperforming both FlashAttention-2 and FlashSigmoid, with native padding support, which is essential for biological sequences. Our results establish sigmoid attention as both theoretically grounded and empirically superior for biological foundation models. Code is available at https://github.com/MSDLLCpapers/triton-sigmoid

</details>


### [112] [How to Guide Your Flow: Few-Step Alignment via Flow Map Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27147)
*Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper reformulates generative guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem and proposes FMRG, a training-free single-trajectory method that uses flow maps to efficiently integrate and guide generation, achieving comparable results to existing methods with 10x fewer computations.


<details>
