  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing guidance methods in generative modeling either require computationally expensive multi-particle or many-step schemes, or rely on poorly understood approximations, creating a need for more efficient and theoretically grounded approaches.

Method: Reformulates guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem, leveraging the flow map to simultaneously integrate and guide the generative flow. This yields a single-trajectory approach without requiring additional training.

Result: At text-to-image scale, FMRG matches or surpasses baselines across inverse problems, style transfer, human preferences, and VLM rewards with as few as 3 NFEs, achieving at least an order-of-magnitude speedup compared to prior state-of-the-art methods.

Conclusion: FMRG provides an efficient, training-free framework for reward-guided generation, achieving comparable or superior performance to existing methods with significantly fewer neural function evaluations (as few as 3 NFEs).

Abstract: In generative modeling, we often wish to produce samples that maximize a user-specified reward such as aesthetic quality or alignment with human preferences, a problem known as guidance. Despite their widespread use, existing guidance methods either require expensive multi-particle, many-step schemes or rely on poorly understood approximations. We reformulate guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem, yielding a hierarchy of algorithms that subsumes existing approaches at the coarsest level. We show that the flow map, an object of significant recent interest for its role in fast inference, arises naturally in the optimal solution. Based on this observation, we propose Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG): a training-free, single-trajectory framework that uses the flow map to both integrate and guide the flow. At text-to-image scale, FMRG matches or surpasses baselines across inverse problems, style transfer, human preferences, and VLM rewards with as few as 3 NFEs, giving at least an order-of-magnitude speedup in comparison to prior state of the art.

</details>


### [113] [Generalizing the Geometry of Model Merging Through Frechet Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27155)
*Marvin F. da Silva,Mohammed Adnan,Felix Dangel,Sageev Oore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出合并模型时不仅几何结构，连平均过程本身也必须是对称性不变的。引入Fréchet平均框架，在适当流形上最小化测地距离来实现对称性感知的模型合并。该方法可推导出Fisher合并，并为低秩适配器(LoRA)的商流形场景设计了实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并旨在无需额外训练即可整合多个模型，但简单的参数空间平均方法在面对架构对称性时表现脆弱，因为其几何结构未考虑对称性因素。

Method: 提出"合并即Fréchet平均"的方法，选择在适当流形上最小化测地距离之和的参数作为合并结果。关键设计在于整体几何结构的选择（度量、流形和距离近似）。当结合简化假设时，Fréchet平均可退化为Fisher合并。针对LoRA的特殊对称性（诱导商流形几何），设计了具体算法。

Result: 证明了Fréchet平均结合简化假设可包含Fisher合并方法，并针对LoRA场景提出了可行的合并算法，验证了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 分析了当前LoRA合并方法的局限性，提出了针对该场景的实用算法，并展示了与其他常用方法的对比效果。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple models into one without additional training. Naïve parameter-space averaging can be fragile under architectural symmetries, as their geometry does not take them into account. In this work we show that not only the geometry, but also the averaging procedure itself, must be symmetry-invariant to achieve symmetry-aware merges. Consequently, we propose a general solution: merging as Fréchet averaging, i.e., selecting parameters that minimize a sum of geodesic distances on an appropriate manifold. In this view, the key design choice is the overall geometry, i.e., the choice of metric, manifold, and distance approximation, that determines what it means for two models to be "close". We show that Fréchet averaging, combined with simplifying assumptions, contains Fisher merging. Building on this, we examine the particular case of low-rank adapters (LoRA), whose symmetries induce a distinct geometry: that of a quotient manifold. We outline the limitations of current LoRA merging methods, propose a practical algorithm for this setting, and show how they compare with other commonly used approaches.

</details>


### [114] [Distributional Alignment Games for Answer-Level Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27166)
*Mehryar Mohri,Jon Schneider,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出答案级微调(ALFT)问题的新型博弈论框架，通过构建策略与目标分布的分布对齐博弈，将原本不可行的边缘化优化转化为可处理的投影问题。该框架统一了多样性与自改进（一致性）方法，Coherence-GRPO算法在数学推理任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型优化依赖于特定推理轨迹（思维链），但实际只需最终答案正确即可。直接优化答案级目标因需对大量潜在推理路径进行边缘化而计算不可行，因此需要新的理论框架来解决这一难题。

Method: 提出"分布对齐博弈"(Distributional Alignment Game)作为通用博弈论框架，将ALFT问题建模为两个参与者——策略(Policy)和目标分布(Target)——之间的二人博弈。通过证明该博弈的纳什均衡恰好对应原始答案级优化问题的解，将不可行的边缘化问题转化为可处理的投影问题。方法兼容Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，并据此设计Coherence-GRPO算法。

Result: 理论证明了纳什均衡与最优解的等价性；实证表明Coherence-GRPO在数学推理任务中取得显著效率提升。

Conclusion: Coherence-GRPO算法在数学推理任务中展现出显著的复杂度提升，证明了该框架的实用价值。游戏理论视角将不可行的边缘化问题转化为可处理的投影问题，为答案级优化提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of \emph{Answer-Level Fine-Tuning} (ALFT), where the goal is to optimize a language model based on the correctness or properties of its final answers, rather than the specific reasoning traces used to produce them. Directly optimizing answer-level objectives is computationally intractable due to the need to marginalize over the vast space of latent reasoning paths. To overcome this, we propose a general game-theoretical framework that lifts the problem to a \emph{Distributional Alignment Game}. We formulate ALFT as a two-player game between a Policy (the generator) and a Target (an auxiliary distribution). We prove that the Nash Equilibrium of this game corresponds exactly to the solution of the original answer-level optimization problem. This variational perspective transforms the intractable marginalization problem into a tractable projection problem. We demonstrate that this framework unifies recent approaches to diversity and self-improvement (coherence) and provide efficient algorithms compatible with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), such as Coherence-GRPO, yielding significant complexity gains in mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Preserving Temporal Dynamics in Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27182)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于MCMC的模型无关框架，通过强制保持转移统计特性来解决GAN生成时间序列中的分布偏移和时间动态缺失问题，理论分析和实验验证表明显式保留转移规律优于单纯对抗分布匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAN方法在生成多元时间序列时，主要关注匹配边缘数据分布，忽视原始序列中自然存在的时间动态特性，导致分布偏移和时间漂移问题，削弱合成序列的保真度。

Method: 提出模型无关的MCMC框架，强制合成序列与经验转移统计保持一致。具体包括：1) 分析条件生成模型在序列生成中累积偏差的理论机制；2) 利用MCMC算法校正分布偏移；3) 保持相邻时间点间的转移统计特性。在Lorenz、Likor、ETTh和ILI数据集上，使用RCGAN、GCWGAN、TimeGAN、SigCWGAN和AECGAN等5种GAN进行验证。

Result: MCMC框架在自相关对齐、偏度误差、峰度误差、R²、判别分数和预测分数等指标上均实现一致提升。

Conclusion: MCMC框架通过显式保留转移规律而非仅依赖对抗分布匹配，为时序数据生成建模提供了原则性方向。该方法模型无关，可与多种GAN架构集成，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Time-series data augmentation plays a crucial role in regression-oriented forecasting tasks, where limited data restricts the performance of deep learning models. While Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) have shown promise in synthetic time-series generation, existing approaches primarily focus on matching marginal data distributions and often overlook the temporal dynamics that naturally exist in the original multivariate time series. When generating multivariate time series, this mismatch leads to distribution shift and temporal drift, thereby degrading the fidelity of the synthetic sequences.
  In this work, we propose a model-agnostic Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC)-based framework to mitigate distribution shift and preserve temporal dynamics in synthetic time series. We provide a theoretical analysis of how conditional generative models accumulate deviations under sequential generation and demonstrate that the MCMC algorithm can correct these discrepancies by enforcing consistency with empirical transition statistics between neighboring time points.
  Extensive experiments on the Lorenz, Licor, ETTh, and ILI datasets using RCGAN, GCWGAN, TimeGAN, SigCWGAN, and AECGAN demonstrate that the proposed MCMC framework consistently improves autocorrelation alignment, skewness error, kurtosis error, R$^2$, discriminative score, and predictive score. These results suggest that synthetic time series consistent with the original data require explicit preservation of transition laws rather than solely relying on adversarial distribution matching, thereby offering a principled direction for improving generative modeling of time-series data.

</details>


### [116] [Analytical Correction for Subsampling Bias in Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27239)
*Jiaru Zhang,Zeyun Deng,Juanwu Lu,Ziran Wang,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对漂移模型(Drifting Models)中由于minibatch有限样本导致的softmax自归一化偏置问题，提出了解析偏置校正(ABC)方法，将偏置从O(1/n)降低到O(1/n²)，同时不增加方差且保持凸包包含性质，实践中仅需两行代码即可在CIFAR-10上提升FID并加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型使用minibatch估计softmax加权质心，但论文发现minibatch质心是有偏估计器，存在O(1/n)的点态偏置（源于softmax自归一化），且该偏置在小batch时最为显著，影响模型训练效果。

Method: 通过分析softmax自归一化引入的偏置项，论文推导出偏置的解析表达式，然后利用batch内统计量近似主要偏置项，提出闭式插件式校正方法ABC（Analytical Bias Correction）。

Result: 理论证明ABC可将偏置从O(1/n)降至O(1/n²)，不引入一阶方差增加，并保持校正后质心仍在凸包内。实验验证了O(1/n)和O(1/n²)的理论缩放率。在CIFAR-10上，ABC降低了FID并加速训练，小batch时收益最大。

Conclusion: ABC是一个轻量、实用、高效的偏置校正方案，仅需两行代码，编译执行下计算开销可忽略，为解决生成模型中minibatch估计偏置问题提供了理论扎实且易于部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Drifting models are capable one-step generative models trained to follow a drifting field. The field combines attractive and repulsive softmax-weighted centroids over the data and current-generator distributions. In practice, only a minibatch of $n$ samples from each distribution is available, and each centroid is approximated by an empirical estimate. In this paper, we begin by showing that the minibatch centroid is in general a biased estimator of the target centroid, with a pointwise $O(1/n)$ bias arising from softmax self-normalization. Correcting this bias requires the expectation over the full distribution, which is intractable. We instead approximate the leading bias term from in-batch statistics and propose Analytical Bias Correction (ABC), a closed-form plug-in adjustment. We prove that ABC reduces the bias from $O(1/n)$ to $O(1/n^2)$, introduces no first-order increase in total variance, and preserves convex-hull containment of the corrected centroid. In practice, ABC requires only two additional lines of code and has negligible wall-time overhead under compiled execution. Toy experiments confirm the theoretical $O(1/n)$ and $O(1/n^2)$ scaling. On CIFAR-10, ABC reduces FID and trains faster, with the largest gains at small $n$, where the bias is most significant.

</details>


### [117] [Predicting Covariate-Driven Spatial Deformation for Nonstationary Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27280)
*Minghao Gu,Weizhi Lin,Qiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于协变量驱动的空间变形方法，实现非平稳高斯过程的预测建模。核心创新在于利用李代数连接可微变形空间与欧几里得协变量空间，并通过物理假设截断高阶交互项，在保证理论严格性的同时获得简洁的函数形式和高效估计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间变形方法假设变形是静态的，无法捕捉由协变量变化引起的空间相关性动态演变。这一局限性严重制约了非平稳高斯过程在新协变量条件下的预测能力。

Method: 1. 将空间变形建模为协变量的函数；2. 利用李代数（Lie algebra）将可微分变形空间与欧几里得协变量空间建立联系，将变形表示为速度场的生成；3. 理论证明高阶交互项可在适度物理假设下截断；4. 基于此推导简洁的变形函数形式；5. 开发高效的估计-推断算法处理有限的协变量-变形样本对。

Result: 所提方法在模拟研究和两个实际案例（制造工程和地理统计学）中均表现出色，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文成功实现了静态变形方法向协变量自适应变形方法的拓展，为非平稳空间数据的预测建模提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Nonstationary Gaussian processes (GPs) are essential for modeling complex, locally heterogeneous spatial data. A common modeling approach is the spatial deformation method that warps the domain to recover isotropy. However, this static method does not account for changes in spatial correlation induced by covariates, limiting its ability to predict nonstationary GPs under new covariate conditions.
  To enable predictive modeling of the deformation method, we propose to model the spatial deformation as a function of covariates. The spaces of diffeomorphic deformations and Euclidean covariate vectors are connected by characterizing deformations as generated by velocity fields living in a Lie algebra. To overcome the estimation instability caused by high-order interactions between multiple covariates in a general Lie algebra, we prove that those interactions can be truncated with a moderate physical assumption. Based on the theoretical results, a concise functional form of deformations driven by multiple covariates can be established, and an efficient estimation-inference algorithm is developed for out-of-sample nonstationary GP prediction with limited covariate-deformation sample pairs. The effectiveness and generalizability of the method are demonstrated on a simulation study and two case studies, in the fields of manufacturing and geostatistics, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [PINN-Cast: Exploring the Role of Continuous-Depth NODE in Transformers and Physics Informed Loss as Soft Physical Constraints in Short-term Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27313)
*Hira Saleem,Flora Salim,Cormac Purcell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PINN-Cast，一种结合神经常微分方程和物理信息学习的连续深度变换器编码器，用于天气预报。该方法用ODE驱动的连续更新替代传统离散残差连接，通过双分支注意力机制捕捉变化敏感信号，并以物理一致性作为软约束训练，在短期预报任务中取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变换器的天气预报模型缺乏物理感知性，且离散层更新不适合建模平滑的潜在动态，需要将数据驱动效率与物理一致性相结合。

Method: 提出PINN-Cast方法，整合Neural ODE动力学于变换器编码器块中，用自适应数值积分求解ODE更新；引入双分支注意力模块，结合标准分块自注意力和基于导数算子的辅助分支；采用物理信息训练目标作为软约束。

Result: PINN-Cast在短期天气预报任务中优于标准离散变换器基线和现有神经ODE预报变体，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PINN-Cast在短期天气预报中显著优于标准离散变换器和现有神经ODE预报方法，证明了连续深度设计、物理一致性和变化敏感注意力机制的优越性。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction has long relied on physics-based numerical weather prediction (NWP), whose accuracy comes at the cost of substantial compute and complex simulation workflows. Recent transformer-based forecasters offer efficient data-driven alternatives, however transformers are physics-agnostic models. Additionally, standard transformer encoders evolve representations through discrete layer updates that may be less suited to modeling smooth latent dynamics. In this work, we propose a continuous-depth transformer encoder for weather forecasting that integrates Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (Neural ODE) dynamics within each encoder block. Specifically, we replace discrete residual updates with ODE-based updates solved using adaptive numerical integration. We also introduce a two-branch attention module that combines conventional patch-wise self-attention with an auxiliary branch that applies a derivative operator to attention logits, providing an additional change-sensitive interaction signal. To further align forecasts with governing principles, we propose a customized physics-informed training objective that enforces physical consistency as a soft constraint. We evaluate the proposed method against a standard discrete transformer baseline and an existing continuous-time Neural ODE forecasting variant, demonstrating the importance of PINN-Cast in short term weather forecasting.

</details>


### [119] [TypeBandit: Type-Level Context Allocation and Reweighting for Effective Attribute Completion in Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27356)
*Ta-Yang Wang,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TypeBandit addresses missing node attributes in heterogeneous graphs by leveraging type-dependent information asymmetry. It is a lightweight, model-agnostic framework combining bandit sampling at the type level with joint representation learning, compatible with multiple GNN backbones (R-GCN, HetGNN, HGT, SimpleHGN), and includes a hybrid pretraining scheme for better initialization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Missing node attributes are a major bottleneck for downstream learning in heterogeneous graphs. The paper identifies and formalizes type-dependent information asymmetry — the phenomenon that different node types provide substantially different levels of useful signal for attribute completion.

Method: TypeBandit combines topology-aware initialization, type-level bandit sampling, and joint representation learning. It allocates a finite global sampling budget across node types, samples representative nodes within each type, and uses sampled type summaries as shared contextual signals during representation construction. The method also introduces a hybrid pretraining scheme combining structural degree priors with feature propagation.

Result: Under a fixed-split protocol on DBLP, IMDB, and ACM, TypeBandit provides dataset-dependent but practically meaningful gains. Additional experiments on ablation, stability, efficiency, semantic-propagation, and sampled OGBN-MAG support its effectiveness.

Conclusion: TypeBandit is a lightweight, model-agnostic front-end for heterogeneous attribute completion that operates at the type level rather than over local neighborhoods. It is dataset-dependent but provides practically meaningful gains under limited sampling resources, with architectural flexibility across multiple GNN backbones including R-GCN, HetGNN, HGT, and SimpleHGN.

Abstract: Heterogeneous graphs are widely used to model multi-relational systems, but missing node attributes remain a major bottleneck for downstream learning. In this paper, we identify and formalize type-dependent information asymmetry: the phenomenon that different node types provide substantially different levels of useful signal for attribute completion. Motivated by this observation, we propose TypeBandit, a lightweight, model-agnostic methodology for heterogeneous attribute completion. TypeBandit combines topology-aware initialization, type-level bandit sampling, and joint representation learning. It allocates a finite global sampling budget across node types, samples representative nodes within each type, and uses the resulting sampled type summaries as shared contextual signals during representation construction. By operating at the type level rather than over each target node's local neighborhood, TypeBandit keeps the adaptive state compact and practical for large heterogeneous graphs.
  A key advantage of TypeBandit is architectural flexibility. Rather than requiring a new heterogeneous graph neural network architecture, TypeBandit acts as a type-aware front end for representative heterogeneous GNN backbones, including R-GCN, HetGNN, HGT, and SimpleHGN. We further introduce a hybrid pretraining scheme that combines structural degree priors with feature propagation, yielding a more reliable initializer than degree-only pretraining. Under a fixed-split protocol on DBLP, IMDB, and ACM, TypeBandit provides dataset-dependent but practically meaningful gains. Additional ablation, stability, efficiency, semantic-propagation, and sampled OGBN-MAG experiments support TypeBandit as a practical strategy for heterogeneous attribute completion when type-specific information is unevenly distributed and sampling resources are limited.

</details>


### [120] [MIFair: A Mutual-Information Framework for Intersectionality and Multiclass Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28030)
*Jeanne Monnier,Thomas George,Frédéric Guyard,Christèle Tarnec,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIFair是一个基于互信息的统一公平性框架，整合偏差评估与缓解功能，支持交叉性和多分类场景，通过正则化训练简化实践应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性方法在处理交叉性、多分类场景时存在局限，缺乏统一的度量标准和灵活、通用的框架来满足不同情境下的偏差评估与缓解需求。

Method: 提出MIFair框架，基于互信息定义群体公平性（预测变量与敏感属性的统计独立性），建立与独立性/分离性公平概念的等价性，引入正则化训练方法，并支持多类分类和复杂子群结构。

Result: MIFair在真实数据集（表格和图像）上有效减少偏差，覆盖此前未解决的多属性场景，同时保持较强的预测性能。

Conclusion: MIFair通过统一框架简化了公平性实践，提供了灵活且可扩展的解决方案，能够在保持预测性能的同时有效减少偏见，包括复杂的多属性场景。

Abstract: Fairness in machine learning remains challenging due to its ethical complexity, the absence of a universal definition, and the need for context-specific bias metrics. Existing methods still struggle with intersectionality, multiclass settings, and limited flexibility and generality. To address these gaps, we introduce MIFair, a unified framework for bias assessment and mitigation based on mutual information. MIFair provides a flexible metric template and an in-processing mitigation method inspired by the Prejudice Remover, defining group fairness as statistical independence between prediction-derived variables and sensitive attributes. We further strengthen its information-theoretic foundation by establishing equivalences with widely used fairness notions such as independence and separation. MIFair naturally supports intersectionality, complex subgroup structures, and multiclass classification and employs regularization-based training to reduce bias according to the selected metric. Its key advantage is its versatility: it consolidates diverse fairness requirements into a single coherent framework, enabling consistent benchmarking and simplifying practical use. Experiments on real-world tabular and image datasets show that MIFair effectively reduces bias, including previously unaddressed multi-attribute scenarios, while maintaining strong predictive performance across the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [121] [AG-TAL: Anatomically-Guided Topology-Aware Loss for Multiclass Segmentation of the Circle of Willis Using Large-Scale Multi-Center Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27357)
*Jialu Liu,Yue Cui,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对脑动脉CoW多分类分割的挑战，提出AG-TAL损失函数（整合半径感知Dice、 breakage感知clDice及邻接感知共现损失），构建大规模多中心数据集，实现80.85%平均Dice分数，显著提升小血管分割性能，并验证了跨数据集泛化能力和阿尔茨海默病影像生物标志物发现潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Circle of Willis（CoW）多分类分割难题，当前深度学习方法存在血管断裂、类别误分类问题，现有拓扑损失函数在3D多分类场景下计算成本过高。

Method: 提出解剖学引导的拓扑感知损失函数AG-TAL，包含三个核心组件：1）半径感知的Dice损失（应对小血管类不平衡）；2） breakage感知的clDice损失（利用分组卷积高效保留局部连通性）；3）邻接感知的共现损失（利用解剖学先验增强相邻动脉边界）。同时构建大规模多中心CoW数据集（统一标注）。采用5折交叉验证进行评估。

Result: AG-TAL在所有CoW动脉上达到80.85%平均Dice分数，小血管相比最先进方法提升1.05-3.09%。在6个独立数据集上Dice为74.46%-81.17%，小血管提升2.20%-9.98%。

Conclusion: AG-TAL在多中心CoW动脉分割任务中展现出优异的性能与泛化能力，在小血管分割上提升显著，且在阿尔茨海默病队列中的临床应用验证了其鲁棒性和发现影像形态学生物标志物的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate multiclass segmentation of the Circle of Willis (CoW) is essential for neurovascular disease management but remains challenging due to complex vascular topology and variable morphology. Existing deep learning methods often suffer from vascular discontinuities and inter-class misclassification, while current topological loss functions incur prohibitive computational costs in 3D multiclass settings. To address these limitations, we propose an Anatomically-Guided Topology-Aware Loss (AG-TAL) and introduce a large-scale, multi-center CoW dataset with unified annotations to facilitate robust model training. AG-TAL specifically integrates a radius-aware Dice loss to address class imbalance in small vessels, a breakage-aware clDice loss that utilizes group convolutions to efficiently preserve local connectivity, and an adjacency-aware co-occurrence loss that leverages anatomical priors to enforce distinct boundaries between neighboring arteries. Evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation, AG-TAL achieved an average Dice score of 80.85% for all CoW arteries, with small arteries notably higher by 1.05-3.09% compared to state-of-the-art methods. Across six independent datasets, the performance of AG-TAL achieved Dice scores ranging from 74.46% to 81.17% for all CoW arteries, with improvements of 2.20% to 9.98% for small arteries compared to other methods. This study demonstrates the superiority of AG-TAL in identifying multiclass CoW arteries and its ability to generalize well to multiple independent datasets. Furthermore, reliability analyses and clinical applications in an Alzheimer's disease cohort validate the AG-TAL's robustness and its potential for discovering imaging-based morphological biomarkers.

</details>


### [122] [Stable but Wrong: An Inference Limit in Galactic Archaeology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27368)
*Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，银河流星考古学中基于分光巡天数据推算恒星年龄的方法存在系统性偏差。当观测质量（信噪比和视差精度）较高时，推算的形成时间尺度相对于天星震学参考存在0.5-1 Gyr的系统性偏移，但统计误差仍保持很小，从而产生"稳定但错误"的推论。这质疑了利用年龄-金属丰度关系重建银河系演化历史方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河流星考古学中，利用分光巡天数据推算的恒星年龄被广泛用于重建银河系盘的形成历史。年龄-金属丰度关系(AMR)及其衍生的形成时间尺度被视为早期盘演化的关键物理诊断指标。这些应用隐含着一个前提：观测质量不会引入系统性偏差。

Method: 使用大型亚巨星样本，基于信号噪声比(SNR)和视差精度两个观测质量参数进行系统性分析，将推算结果与独立的天星震学参考进行对比。

Result: 识别出观测质量参数空间中存在一个特定区域，在该区域内，推算的形成时间尺度相对于独立的天星震学参考存在0.5-1 Gyr的系统性偏移，尽管此时统计不确定性仍然较小。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在高信噪比(SNR)和高视差精度条件下，利用分光巨星样本推算的银河系盘形成时间尺度存在0.5-1 Gyr的系统性偏差，而此时统计误差仍然很小，导致"稳定但错误"的推论状态。这一发现对利用恒星年龄重建银河系演化历史的方法提出了重要质疑。

Abstract: Statistical inference in observational science typically relies on a fundamental assumption: as sample size increases and uncertainties decrease, the inferred results should converge to the true physical quantities. This assumption underpins the notion that big data lead to more reliable conclusions. In Galactic archaeology, stellar ages inferred from spectroscopic surveys are widely used to reconstruct the formation history of the Milky Way disk. The age metallicity relation (AMR) and its derived formation timescale are often regarded as key physical diagnostics of early disk evolution. This interpretation carries an implicit premise: that observational quality does not introduce systematic bias into age inference. Here we show that this premise may fail. Using a large sample of subgiant stars, we identify a region in the observational quality parameter space (signal-to-noise ratio and parallax precision) where the inferred formation timescale exhibits a systematic offset of 0.5-1 Gyr relative to an independent asteroseismic reference, while the statistical uncertainties remain small, thus producing a stable-but-wrong inference state.

</details>


### [123] [ChipLingo: A Systematic Training Framework for Large Language Models in EDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27415)
*Lei Li,Xingwen Yu,Jianguo Ni,Junxuan Zhu,Jieqiong Zhang,Jian Zhao,Zhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChipLingo是一个针对电子设计自动化(EDA)领域的大型语言模型领域适配训练 pipeline，通过多源语料构建、域适应预训练和RAG场景训练三阶段方案，显著提升LLM在EDA专业任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM直接应用于EDA领域面临三大挑战：领域专业知识不足、跨工具知识混淆、以及领域训练后检索增强生成(RAG)性能下降。

Method: 三阶段训练流程：(1)多源数据语料构建与QA增强；(2)域适应预训练，对比不同参数训练策略；(3)指令对齐与多样化检索条件下的RAG场景训练。

Result: ChipLingo-8B在EDA-Bench上达到59.7%准确率，超越同规模基座模型及部分更大通用模型；ChipLingo-32B达70.02%，接近闭源商业模型。QA增强提升领域性能；Partial FT在适配与通用能力保留间优于LoRA；显式RAG场景训练可缓解领域训练后检索利用率下降。

Conclusion: ChipLingo验证了系统化领域训练对知识密集型EDA任务的实用价值，提出的三阶段训练范式为未来EDA智能体和外部知识驱动系统奠定基础。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of semiconductor technology, Electronic Design Automation (EDA) has become an increasingly knowledge-intensive and document-driven engineering domain. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong general capabilities, applying them directly to EDA remains challenging due to limited domain expertise, cross-tool knowledge confusion, and degraded retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) performance after domain training. To address these issues, this paper presents ChipLingo, a systematic training pipeline for domain-adapted LLMs tailored to EDA scenarios.
  ChipLingo consists of three stages: domain corpus construction with multi-source data curation and QA augmentation, domain-adaptive pretraining with comparisons of different parameter training strategies, and instruction alignment with RAG scenario training under diverse retrieval conditions. We also curate an internal benchmark, EDA-Bench, covering representative EDA tool scenarios, with plans for public release.
  Experiments show that ChipLingo-8B achieves 59.7% accuracy on EDA-Bench, outperforming the same-scale base model and some larger general-purpose models. ChipLingo-32B reaches 70.02%, approaching leading closed-source commercial models. Further analysis shows that QA augmentation improves domain performance, Partial FT offers a better balance between adaptation and general capability retention than LoRA, and explicit RAG scenario training mitigates the decline in retrieval utilization after domain training. These results demonstrate the practical value of systematic domain training for knowledge-intensive EDA tasks and provide a foundation for future EDA agents and external-knowledge-driven systems.

</details>


### [124] [AdaBFL: Multi-Layer Defensive Adaptive Aggregation for Bzantine-Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27434)
*Zehui Tang,Yuchen Liu,Feihu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AdaBFL方法，通过三层防御机制实现自适应权重调整，在非凸设置和非独立同分布数据条件下提供了收敛保证，并在多个数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习的去中心化特性使其容易受到投毒攻击，现有拜占庭鲁棒方法难以在多种攻击类型之间提供平衡防御，或依赖于服务器端拥有数据集。

Method: 提出了基于三层防御机制的有效多层防御自适应聚合方法（AdaBFL），能够自适应调整防御算法的权重以应对复杂攻击。

Result: 通过三层防御机制的自适应调整，有效提升了模型在恶意攻击下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AdaBFL在多个数据集上的综合实验中表现出优于同类算法的性能，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a popular distributed learning paradigm in machine learning, which enables multiple clients to collaboratively train models under the guidance of a server without exposing private client data. However, FL's decentralized nature makes it vulnerable to poisoning attacks, where malicious clients can submit corrupted models to manipulate the system. To counter such attacks, although various Byzantine-robust methods have been proposed, these methods struggle to provide balanced defense against multiple types of attacks or rely on possessing the dataset in the server. To deal with these drawbacks, thus, we propose an effective multi-layer defensive adaptive aggregation for Bzantine-robust federated learning (AdaBFL) based on a novel three-layer defensive mechanism, which can adaptively adjust the weights of defense algorithms to counter complex attacks. Moreover, we provide convergence properties of our AdaBFL method under the non-convex setting on non-iid data. Comprehensive experiments across multiple datasets validate the superiority of our AdaBFL over the comparable algorithms.

</details>


### [125] [ABC: Any-Subset Autoregression via Non-Markovian Diffusion Bridges in Continuous Time and Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27443)
*Gabe Guo,Thanawat Sornwanee,Lutong Hao,Elon Litman,Stefano Ermon,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes ABC, a novel method for generating continuous-time, continuous-space stochastic processes conditioned on partial observations. By modeling with a continual SDE that tracks real time and deriving dynamics via changes-of-measure on path space, ABC addresses limitations of existing diffusion models in capturing temporal proximity, scale-appropriate noise injection, and flexible path-dependent conditioning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing diffusion-based approaches for generating continuous-time, continuous-space stochastic processes (e.g., videos, weather forecasts) conditioned on partial observations suffer from three key limitations: (1) noise-to-data evolution fails to capture structural similarity between temporally close states and has unstable integration in low-step regimes; (2) random noise injection is insensitive to physical time elapsed, resulting in incorrect dynamics; (3) they overlook conditioning on arbitrary subsets of states such as irregularly sampled timesteps or future observations.

Method: The method proposes ABC (Any-Subset Autoregressive Models) based on Non-Markovian Diffusion Bridges in continuous time and space. Key innovations include: (1) Modeling with one continual SDE where time variable and intermediate states track real time and process states; (2) Deriving SDE dynamics via changes-of-measure on path space; (3) Learning dynamics via a path- and time-dependent extension of denoising score matching.

Result: Experiments demonstrate ABC's superiority to competing methods on multiple domains, including video generation and weather forecasting.

Conclusion: The proposed ABC method models the process with one continual SDE whose time variable tracks real time, providing three provable advantages: (1) generating from already-close previous states rather than noise; (2) noise injection scaling with physical time elapsed for plausible dynamics; (3) path-dependent conditioning on arbitrary subsets of state history and/or future. The method demonstrates superiority over competing methods on video generation and weather forecasting tasks.

Abstract: Generating continuous-time, continuous-space stochastic processes (e.g., videos, weather forecasts) conditioned on partial observations (e.g., first and last frames) is a fundamental challenge. Existing approaches, (e.g., diffusion models), suffer from key limitations: (1) noise-to-data evolution fails to capture structural similarity between states close in physical time and has unstable integration in low-step regimes; (2) random noise injected is insensitive to the physical process's time elapsed, resulting in incorrect dynamics; (3) they overlook conditioning on arbitrary subsets of states (e.g., irregularly sampled timesteps, future observations). We propose ABC: Any-Subset Autoregressive Models via Non-Markovian Diffusion Bridges in Continuous Time and Space. Crucially, we model the process with one continual SDE whose time variable and intermediate states track the real time and process states. This has provable advantages: (1) the starting point for generating future states is the already-close previous state, rather than uninformative noise; (2) random noise injection scales with physical time elapsed, encouraging physically plausible dynamics with similar time-adjacent states. We derive SDE dynamics via changes-of-measure on path space, yielding another advantage: (3) path-dependent conditioning on arbitrary subsets of the state history and/or future. To learn these dynamics, we derive a path- and time-dependent extension of denoising score matching. Our experiments show ABC's superiority to competing methods on multiple domains, including video generation and weather forecasting.

</details>


### [126] [Improving Graph Few-shot Learning with Hyperbolic Space and Denoising Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27462)
*Yonghao Liu,Jialu Sun,Wei Pang,Fausto Giunchiglia,Ximing Li,Xiaoyue Feng,Renchu Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPRESS框架，通过双曲空间学习具有层次结构的节点表示，并利用去噪扩散机制从少量支持样本丰富目标分布，同时在理论上证明了更紧的泛化界，在多个基准数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图小样本学习方法存在两个关键缺陷：1) 在元训练阶段，仅在欧几里得空间进行节点表示学习，无法捕捉图数据中固有的层次结构；2) 在元测试阶段，仅用少量支持样本拟合经验目标分布，当该分布与真实分布严重偏离时导致性能下降。

Method: 提出IMPRESS框架，将双曲空间与去噪扩散机制相结合。双曲空间用于学习具有层次结构的节点表示，去噪扩散机制用于从少量支持样本丰富目标分布。

Result: IMPRESS在多个基准数据集上均一致性地优于对比基线方法。

Conclusion: IMPRESS通过双管齐下的策略——双曲空间建模层次结构与去噪扩散增强分布——有效解决了图小样本学习中的核心问题，在理论和实证两方面均取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Graph few-shot learning, which focuses on effectively learning from only a small number of labeled nodes to quickly adapt to new tasks, has garnered significant research attention. Despite recent advances in graph few-shot learning that have demonstrated promising performance, existing methods still suffer from several key limitations. First, during the meta-training phase, these methods typically perform node representation learning in Euclidean space, which often fails to capture the inherently hierarchical structure existing in real-world graph data. Second, during the meta-testing phase, they usually fit an empirical target distribution derived from only a few support samples, even when this distribution significantly deviates from the true underlying distribution. To address these issues, we propose IMPRESS, a novel framework that IMproves graPh few-shot learning with hypeRbolic spacE and denoiSing diffuSion. Specifically, our model learns node representations in a hyperbolic space and enriches the support distribution through denoising diffusion mechanisms. Theoretically, IMPRESS achieves a tighter generalization bound. Empirically, IMPRESS consistently outperforms competitive baselines across multiple benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [127] [Toward Scalable SDN for LEO Mega-Constellations: A Graph Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27478)
*Sivaram Krishnan,Bassel Al Homssi,Zhouyou Gu,Jihong Park,Sung-Min Oh,Jinho Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper presents a scalable hierarchical SDN framework for managing mega-constellation LEO satellite networks by combining Graph Neural Networks with Koopman theory-based linearization. The proposed Graph Koopman Autoencoder (GKAE) enables efficient spatio-temporal forecasting, achieving 42.8% better spatial compression and 10.81% improved temporal prediction accuracy compared to baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Terrestrial network limitations necessitate the integration of non-terrestrial networks (NTNs), particularly mega-constellations with thousands of LEO satellites. These satellites function as interconnected network switches via inter-satellite links (ISLs), but their massive scale creates severe bottlenecks for network management.

Method: The authors propose a hierarchical SDN framework combining Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for compact constellation topology representation and Koopman theory for nonlinear dynamics linearization. Specifically, a Graph Koopman Autoencoder (GKAE) is designed to forecast spatio-temporal behavior within linear subspaces for each orbital shell, which are then aggregated by a central SDN controller for globally coordinated control.

Result: Simulations on the Starlink constellation demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves at least a 42.8% improvement in spatial compression and a 10.81% improvement in temporal forecasting compared to established baselines, while utilizing a significantly smaller model footprint.

Conclusion: The proposed GKAE-based hierarchical SDN framework provides a scalable solution for managing mega-constellation networks, achieving significant improvements in both spatial compression (42.8%) and temporal forecasting accuracy (10.81%) with reduced computational overhead.

Abstract: Terrestrial network limitations drive the integration of non-terrestrial networks (NTNs), notably mega-constellations comprising thousands of low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites. While these satellites act as interconnected network switches via inter-satellite links (ISLs), their massive scale creates severe bottlenecks for network management. To address this, we propose a scalable, hierarchical software-defined networking (SDN) framework. Our architecture leverages graph neural networks (GNNs) to compactly represent the constellation topology, and Koopman theory to linearize nonlinear dynamics. Specifically, a Graph Koopman Autoencoder (GKAE) forecasts spatio-temporal behavior within a linear subspace for each orbital shell. A central SDN controller then aggregates these shell-level predictions for globally coordinated control. Simulations on the Starlink constellation demonstrate that our approach achieves at least a 42.8\% improvement in spatial compression and a 10.81\% improvement in temporal forecasting compared to established baselines, all while utilizing a significantly smaller model footprint.

</details>


### [128] [Low Rank Adaptation for Adversarial Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27487)
*Han Liu,Shanghao Shi,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Chongjie Zhang,Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper reveals that adversarial perturbations exhibit an inherently low-rank structure, analogous to the low-rank updates used in LoRA for LLM training. By projecting gradients into a low-dimensional subspace and confining perturbation search within this subspace, the authors significantly improve the efficiency and effectiveness of black-box adversarial attacks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has improved LLM training efficiency by updating neural network layers using low-rank matrices. Since adversarial example generation is an optimization process analogous to model training, the authors investigate whether adversarial perturbations exhibit a similar low-rank structure, and whether this property can be leveraged to improve black-box adversarial attacks that often suffer from excessive query requirements.

Method: The authors use a two-step approach: first, they leverage a reference model and auxiliary data to project gradients into a low-dimensional subspace; then, they confine the perturbation search in black-box attacks to this low-rank subspace, thereby improving both efficiency and effectiveness of adversarial attacks.

Result: The authors provide both theoretical analysis and extensive empirical evidence across various attack methods, model architectures, and datasets, confirming that adversarial perturbations possess an inherently low-rank structure. This insight enables more efficient and effective black-box adversarial attacks by restricting the perturbation search to a low-dimensional subspace.

Conclusion: The proposed low-rank adversarial attack method demonstrates substantial and consistent improvements in attack efficiency and effectiveness across various attack methods, benchmark models, datasets, and threat models.

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), which leverages the insight that model updates typically reside in a low-dimensional space, has significantly improved the training efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) by updating neural network layers using low-rank matrices. Since the generation of adversarial examples is an optimization process analogous to model training, this naturally raises the question: Do adversarial perturbations exhibit a similar low-rank structure?
  In this paper, we provide both theoretical analysis and extensive empirical investigation across various attack methods, model architectures, and datasets to show that adversarial perturbations indeed possess an inherently low-rank structure. This insight opens up new opportunities for improving both adversarial attacks and defenses. We mainly focus on leveraging this low-rank property to improve the efficiency and effectiveness of black-box adversarial attacks, which often suffer from excessive query requirements. Our method follows a two-step approach. First, we use a reference model and auxiliary data to guide the projection of gradients into a low-dimensional subspace. Next, we confine the perturbation search in black-box attacks to this low-rank subspace, significantly improving the efficiency and effectiveness of the adversarial attacks.
  We evaluated our approach across a range of attack methods, benchmark models, datasets, and threat models. The results demonstrate substantial and consistent improvements in the performance of our low-rank adversarial attacks compared to conventional methods.

</details>


### [129] [FMCL: Class-Aware Client Clustering with Foundation Model Representations for Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27510)
*Mahad Ali,Laura J. Brattain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMCL，一种基于基础模型表征的一次性、类感知客户端聚类框架，通过冻结的基础模型计算每个客户端的类别级嵌入原型，利用余弦距离衡量类感知表征的相似性，在训练前一次性完成聚类，避免了联邦优化过程中的额外通信开销，且对下游模型架构保持 agnostic。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing clustered FL methods rely on raw data statistics, model parameters, or heuristic similarity measures that fail to capture class-level semantic structure across heterogeneous domains and often require iterative coordination, leading to communication overhead.

Method: FMCL leverages a frozen foundation model to compute class-level embedding prototypes for each client, then measures similarity via cosine distance between their class-aware representations. Clustering is performed once prior to training in a one-shot manner.

Result: Experiments across heterogeneous benchmarks demonstrate FMCL improves federated performance and yields more stable clustering behavior compared to existing clustering-based methods under non-identically distributed data partitioning.

Conclusion: FMCL improves federated performance and yields more stable clustering behavior compared to existing clustering-based methods under non-identically distributed data partitioning. It introduces no additional communication overhead during federated optimization and remains agnostic to downstream model architecture.

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without sharing raw data, yet its performance deteriorates under statistical heterogeneity. Clustered Federated Learning addresses this challenge by grouping similar clients and training separate models per cluster. However, existing clustering strategies often rely on raw data statistics, model parameters, or heuristic similarity measures that fail to capture class-level semantic structure across heterogeneous domains and frequently require iterative coordination. We propose FMCL, a one-shot, class-aware client clustering framework that leverages foundation model representations to construct semantic client signatures. Using a frozen foundation model, FMCL computes class-level embedding prototypes for each client and measures similarity via cosine distance between their class-aware representations. Clustering is performed once prior to training, introducing no additional communication during federated optimization and remaining agnostic to the downstream model architecture. Extensive experiments across heterogeneous benchmarks demonstrate that FMCL improves federated performance and yields more stable clustering behavior compared to existing clustering-based methods under non-identically distributed data partitioning.

</details>


### [130] [Diagnosing Capability Gaps in Fine-Tuning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27547)
*Saeid Asgari Taghanaki,Rakshanda Agarwal,Bruce Sun,Rohan Jha,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Sara Malvar,Rui Ying,Yifei Xu,Guilherme Potje,Tusher Chakraborty,Leonardo de Oliveira Nunes,Ranveer Chandra,Emre Kiciman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GoalCover是一个用于检测LLM微调数据集能力缺口的框架，通过交互式目标分解和自动化覆盖评估实现事前诊断，在三个领域验证了有效性，并将过滤数据应用于Qwen-3-14B金融摘要任务实现奖励分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）微调场景中，领域特定任务需要全面覆盖目标能力的训练数据集。然而，在代价高昂的微调运行之前，识别数据集中缺失哪些能力仍然是一个悬而未决的问题。现有方法缺乏系统性的事前诊断能力。

Method: GoalCover框架通过三个核心步骤实现能力缺口检测：(1) 交互式目标分解：将高级目标结构化分解为原子级、可独立评估的子目标；(2) 自动覆盖评估：使用LLM为每个训练样本分配与各子目标的对齐分数；(3) 缺失能力识别：自动化分析低分样本的解释信息。验证采用两个维度：(1) 控制腐败实验：在医疗QA、法律摘要、代码生成三个领域评估目标能力与非目标能力的区分度；(2) 下游效用实验：在金融摘要强化微调任务上测试过滤数据的训练效果。

Result: 控制腐败实验显示GoalCover可靠区分目标与非目标能力影响：目标子目标平均下降25.6%，而非目标子目标仅下降2.1%（Cohen's d=1.24）。下游效用实验中，使用GoalCover过滤数据训练Qwen-3-14B，LLM-judge奖励从3.77提升至4.12（满分5）；结合过滤数据与目标条件合成样本达到最佳效果4.20。

Conclusion: GoalCover is validated as a practical pre-fine-tuning diagnostic tool that effectively detects capability gaps and provides actionable signal for closing them. The filtered data approach combined with goal-conditioned synthetic samples yields the strongest performance improvement.

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific tasks requires training datasets that comprehensively cover the target capabilities a practitioner needs. Yet identifying which capabilities a dataset fails to support, and doing so before an expensive fine-tuning run, remains a largely unsolved problem. We introduce GoalCover, a framework that helps practitioners systematically detect capability gaps in fine-tuning datasets through interactive goal decomposition and automated coverage assessment. GoalCover guides a practitioner through structured decomposition of a high-level goal into atomic, independently evaluable subgoals; assigns each training sample an LLM-based alignment score against every subgoal; and surfaces missing capabilities through automated analysis of low-scoring sample explanations. We validate the framework along two complementary axes. First, through controlled corruption experiments across three domains (medical QA, legal summarization, code generation), we show that GoalCover reliably distinguishes targeted from non-targeted capability impacts: target subgoals degrade by 25.6% on average versus 2.1% for non-target subgoals (Cohen's d=1.24). Second, we demonstrate downstream utility on a financial-summarization Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) task with Qwen-3-14B: training on GoalCover-filtered data improves the LLM-judge reward from 3.77 to 4.12 (out of 5) over the unfiltered baseline, and combining filtered data with goal-conditioned synthetic samples yields the strongest result (4.20). The two results together show that GoalCover works as a practical pre-fine-tuning diagnostic: it detects capability gaps and produces concrete signal for closing them.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond the Training Distribution: Mapping Generalization Boundaries in Neural Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27551)
*Henrik Voigt,Michael Habeck,Joachim Giesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过严格控制的算术语法环境揭示：当前Transformer模型在程序合成中存在严重的句法外推困难（性能下降30%+），真正的泛化需要跨多流形最大化训练多样性，且现有log-linear scaling存在瓶颈，需要搜索型新方法突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练Transformer在程序合成任务上虽表现优异，但真实泛化能力被数据污染和不透明的训练语料所掩盖，难以区分模型是真正泛化还是记忆检索。

Method: 构建基于领域特定算术语法的严格受控程序合成环境；系统枚举数百万唯一程序构建可解释的句法和语义度量空间；创建精确隔离特定分布偏移的训练/测试集。

Result: 多样化采样（覆盖语义和句法空间）能实现稳健的分布外泛化；评估support generalization发现Transformer在生成句法新颖程序时性能下降超过30%；扩展计算资源能改善泛化但呈严格对数线性关系。

Conclusion: 稳健的分布外泛化需要最大化训练多样性；当前模型的log-linear scaling存在瓶颈，需要创新的搜索方法来突破。

Abstract: Large-scale transformers achieve impressive results on program synthesis benchmarks, yet their true generalization capabilities remain obscured by data contamination and opaque training corpora. To rigorously assess whether models are truly generalizing or merely retrieving memorized templates, we introduce a strictly controlled program synthesis environment based on a domain-specific arithmetic grammar. By systematically enumerating and evaluating millions of unique programs, we construct interpretable syntactic and semantic metric spaces. This allows us to precisely map data distributions and sample train and test splits that isolate specific distributional shifts. Our experiments demonstrate that optimizing density generalization -- through diverse sampling over both semantic and syntactic spaces -- induces robust out-of-distribution generalization. Conversely, evaluating support generalization reveals that transformers severely struggle with extrapolation, experiencing a performance drop of over 30% when forced to generate syntactically novel programs. While steadily scaling up compute improves generalization, the gains follow a strictly log-linear relationship. We conclude that robust generalization requires maximizing training diversity across multiple manifolds, and our findings indicate the necessity for novel search-based approaches to break through current log-linear scaling bottlenecks.

</details>


### [132] [Online semi-supervised perception: Real-time learning without explicit feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27562)
*Branislav Kveton,Michal Valko,Matthai Phillipose,Ling Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A real-time learning algorithm that integrates graph-based semi-supervised learning with online learning, using offline labeled data as initial bias and online unlabeled data for continuous updates, achieving strong performance on face recognition tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-time learning scenarios often lack explicit feedback, necessitating algorithms that can learn from unlabeled data in an online manner while maintaining theoretical guarantees.

Method: Iterative graph construction using both offline labeled examples (initial bias) and online unlabeled examples stream; combines semi-supervised learning with online learning for continuous updates.

Result: Superior precision and recall on 3 challenging video datasets compared to baseline methods.

Conclusion: The algorithm successfully achieves real-time face recognition with a running recognizer, demonstrating practical applicability of the theoretical framework.

Abstract: This paper proposes an algorithm for real-time learning without explicit feedback. The algorithm combines the ideas of semi-supervised learning on graphs and online learning. In particular, it iteratively builds a graphical representation of its world and updates it with observed examples. Labeled examples constitute the initial bias of the algorithm and are provided offline, and a stream of unlabeled examples is collected online to update this bias. We motivate the algorithm, discuss how to implement it efficiently, prove a regret bound on the quality of its solutions, and apply it to the problem of real-time face recognition. Our recognizer runs in real time, and achieves superior precision and recall on 3 challenging video datasets.

</details>


### [133] [Bayesian policy gradient and actor-critic algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27563)
*Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Yaakov Engel,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于高斯过程的贝叶斯策略梯度框架来解决传统蒙特卡洛方法的高方差问题。该框架将策略梯度建模为高斯过程，减少样本需求并提供不确定性估计。针对无法利用马尔可夫性质的局限，进一步提出结合高斯过程时间差分学习的actor-critic架构，通过非参数化评论家实现闭式梯度后验计算。实验验证了所提方法相比经典蒙特卡洛策略梯度方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略梯度方法使用蒙特卡洛技术估计梯度，存在高方差问题，导致需要大量样本且收敛缓慢。这是强化学习中策略优化的核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出两个核心方法：(1) 贝叶斯策略梯度框架：将策略梯度建模为高斯过程，直接对轨迹建模无需特定动力学模型形式；(2) 基于高斯过程时间差分学习的贝叶斯actor-critic模型：使用非参数化评论家建模动作值函数，利用贝叶斯规则计算后验分布，通过精心选择策略参数化和先验协方差（核函数）获得梯度后验的闭式表达式。

Result: 该方法显著减少了获得准确梯度估计所需的样本数量，同时能够自然地提供自然梯度估计和梯度协方差（不确定性度量）。此外，通过结合actor-critic架构，能够有效利用马尔可夫性质，解决纯贝叶斯框架的局限性。

Conclusion: 实验验证表明贝叶斯策略梯度方法和基于高斯过程的actor-critic方法相比传统蒙特卡洛方法具有更好的样本效率和收敛性能。该框架为强化学习中的策略优化提供了一种理论上更严谨、实践上更有效的贝叶斯方法论。

Abstract: Policy gradient methods are reinforcement learning algorithms that adapt a parameterized policy by following a performance gradient estimate. Conventional policy gradient methods use Monte-Carlo techniques to estimate the gradient, which tend to have high variance, requiring many samples and resulting in slow convergence. We first propose a Bayesian framework for policy gradient, based on modeling the policy gradient as a Gaussian process. This reduces the number of samples needed to obtain accurate gradient estimates. Moreover, estimates of the natural gradient and a measure of the uncertainty in the gradient estimates, namely, the gradient covariance, are provided at little extra cost. Since the proposed framework considers system trajectories as its basic observable unit, it does not require the dynamics within trajectories to be of any particular form, and can be extended to partially observable problems. On the downside, it cannot exploit the Markov property when the system is Markovian. To address this, we supplement our Bayesian policy gradient framework with a new actor-critic learning model in which a Bayesian class of non-parametric critics, based on Gaussian process temporal difference learning, is used. Such critics model the action-value function as a Gaussian process, allowing Bayes rule to be used to compute the posterior distribution over action-value functions, conditioned on the observed data. Appropriate choices of the policy parameterization and of the prior covariance (kernel) between action-values yield closed-form expressions for the posterior of the gradient of the expected return with respect to the policy parameters. We perform detailed experimental comparisons of the proposed Bayesian policy gradient and actor-critic algorithms with classic Monte-Carlo based policy gradient methods, on a number of reinforcement learning problems.

</details>


### [134] [Learning from a single labeled face and a stream of unlabeled data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27564)
*Branislav Kveton,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper addresses one-class face authentication by learning a non-parametric model from a single labeled image plus abundant unlabeled data. The method achieves 90% recall at near-zero false positives on 43 people, outperforming baselines by over 25%, and provides practical parameter guidelines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Face recognition from a single image per person is inherently challenging due to extremely limited training samples. The paper addresses a specific authentication scenario (personal computers and mobile devices) where only one person needs to be recognized and negative examples are unavailable, making traditional supervised learning approaches inapplicable.

Method: The authors propose a one-class classification framework with a non-parametric model that learns from a single labeled face image combined with a stream of unlabeled data. They also conducted a comprehensive sensitivity analysis to provide practical guidelines for parameter tuning.

Result: Evaluated on a dataset of 43 people, the proposed method achieved 90% recall at nearly zero false positives, representing a 25%+ improvement over the best-performing baseline method.

Conclusion: This paper presents the first investigation into leveraging unlabeled data to improve face recognition under the single-image-per-person constraint, demonstrating that abundant unlabeled data can be effectively utilized to substantially improve model performance compared to baseline methods.

Abstract: Face recognition from a single image per person is a challenging problem because the training sample is extremely small. We consider a variation of this problem. In our problem, we recognize only one person, and there are no labeled data for any other person. This setting naturally arises in authentication on personal computers and mobile devices, and poses additional challenges because it lacks negative examples. We formalize our problem as one-class classification, and propose and analyze an algorithm that learns a non-parametric model of the face from a single labeled image and a stream of unlabeled data. In many domains, for instance when a person interacts with a computer with a camera, unlabeled data are abundant and easy to utilize. This is the first paper that investigates how these data can help in learning better models in the single-image-per-person setting. Our method is evaluated on a dataset of 43 people and we show that these people can be recognized 90% of time at nearly zero false positives. This recall is 25+% higher than the recall of our best performing baseline. Finally, we conduct a comprehensive sensitivity analysis of our algorithm and provide a guideline for setting its parameters in practice.

</details>


### [135] [Statistical Channel Fingerprint Construction for Massive MIMO: A Unified Tensor Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27574)
*Zhenzhou Jin,Li You,Xiang-Gen Xia,Xiqi Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出统计信道指纹(sCF)概念，基于CSCM与CPAS关系构建统一张量表示。采用LPWTNet架构（融合拉普拉斯金字塔分解与小波变换卷积）实现高效张量恢复，在重建精度和计算效率上达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Channel fingerprint (CF) is a key enabler for CSI acquisition in massive MIMO systems. This work focuses on statistical CF (sCF) which stores statistical CSI (sCSI) at each location. Practical constraints (measurement cost, privacy, security) necessitate efficient sCF construction through tensor restoration.

Method: Unified tensor-based learning architecture (LPWTNet) with: (1) Closed-form Laplacian pyramid (LP) decomposition/reconstruction replacing encoder-decoder; (2) Shared mask learning strategy for adaptive high-frequency component refinement; (3) Small-kernel convolution via wavelet transform (WT) for larger receptive field without over-parameterization.

Result: The method captures multi-scale frequency subband characteristics, adaptively refines high-frequency components, and enhances feature extraction efficiency across different frequency components.

Conclusion: LPWTNet achieves competitive reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency across various sCF construction scenarios compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

Abstract: Channel fingerprint (CF) is considered a key enabler for facilitating the acquisition of channel state information (CSI) in massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) communication systems. In this work, we investigate a novel type of CF that stores statistical CSI (sCSI) at each potential location, referred to as statistical CF (sCF). Specifically, we reveal the relationship between sCSI, namely the channel spatial covariance matrix (CSCM), and the channel power angular spectrum (CPAS). Building on this foundation, we construct a unified tensor representation of the sCF and further reduce its dimension by exploiting the eigenvalue decomposition of the CSCM and its correlation with the PAS. Considering the practical constraints imposed by measurement cost, privacy, and security, we focus on three representative scenarios and uniformly formulate them as tensor restoration tasks. To this end, we propose a unified tensor-based learning architecture, termed LPWTNet. The architecture incorporates a closed-form Laplacian pyramid (LP) decomposition and reconstruction framework that replaces the traditional encoder-decoder structure, enabling efficient inference while capturing multi-scale frequency subband characteristics of the sCF. Additionally, a shared mask learning strategy is introduced to adaptively refine high-frequency sCF components through level-wise adjustments. To achieve a larger receptive field without over-parameterization, we further propose a small-kernel convolution mechanism based on the wavelet transform (WT), which decouples convolution across different frequency components of the sCF and enhances feature extraction efficiency. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach delivers competitive reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency across various sCF construction scenarios when compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [136] [ZAYAN: Disentangled Contrastive Transformer for Tabular Remote Sensing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27606)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Tanpia Tasnim,Md. Ekramul Islam,Muntasir Tabasum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZAYAN是一种针对遥感表格数据的自监督特征级对比学习框架，通过零锚点动态编码和Transformer分类器，在多个遥感数据集上实现了优于现有方法的性能，特别是在标签稀缺和分布偏移场景下展现出强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感与环境科学中的表格数据面临三大挑战：异构性、标签稀缺和特征冗余。现有方法难以有效处理这些问题，需要一种能够学习信息丰富且解耦表示的自监督方法。

Method: ZAYAN框架包含两个模块：(1) ZAYAN-CL：基于零锚点对比目标进行特征嵌入预训练，采用动态扰动和掩码策略；(2) ZAYAN-T：基于这些嵌入条件进行下游分类的Transformer模型。该方法在特征级别而非样本级别进行对比学习，无需显式锚点选择和类别标签。

Result: 在8个数据集（包括6个遥感表格基准和2个卫星/GIS驱动的洪水预测表）上的实验表明，ZAYAN在准确率、鲁棒性和泛化能力方面均优于表格深度学习基线方法，在标签稀缺和分布偏移条件下也保持了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: ZAYAN通过特征级对比学习和动态特征编码，为遥感表格数据学习提供了一种有效的解决方案。该框架在标签稀缺和分布偏移条件下表现出色，具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning informative representations from tabular data in remote sensing and environmental science is challenging due to heterogeneity, scarce labels, and redundancy among features. We present ZAYAN (Zero-Anchor dYnamic feAture eNcoding), a self-supervised, feature-centric contrastive framework for tabular data. ZAYAN performs contrastive learning at the feature rather than sample level, removing the need for explicit anchor selection and any reliance on class labels, while encouraging a redundancy-minimized, disentangled embedding space. The framework has two modules: ZAYAN-CL, which pretrains feature embeddings via a zero-anchor contrastive objective with dynamic perturbations and masking, and ZAYAN-T, a Transformer that conditions on these embeddings for downstream classification. Across eight datasets, including six remote-sensing tabular benchmarks and two remote-sensing-driven flood-prediction tables from satellite and GIS products, ZAYAN achieves superior accuracy, robustness, and generalization over tabular deep learning baselines, with consistent gains under label scarcity and distribution shift. These results indicate that feature-level contrastive learning and dynamic feature encoding provide an effective recipe for learning from tabular sensing data.

</details>


### [137] [AMGenC: Generating Charge Balanced Amorphous Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27613)
*Yan Lin,Jilin Hu,N. M. Anoop Krishnan,Morten M. Smedskjaer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMGenC是一种针对非晶材料的生成式逆设计方法，通过创新的元素噪声、软投影和离散投影机制，确保生成样本的电荷平衡性，同时不牺牲设计精度且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶材料需要更大的模拟单元（数百至数千原子），传统的试错法资源消耗大。虽然生成式逆设计方法可以更具资源效率地直接输出具有所需特性的材料，但概率生成模型的固有随机性导致当元素分配不受约束时，生成的材料中有很大一部分可能电荷不平衡，且现有方法无法有效解决这一局限。

Method: AMGenC通过元素噪声为生成过程提供以电荷平衡为中心的起点，结合逐步软投影和最终离散投影，引导元素逐步逼近精确电荷平衡，从而保证生成样本的电荷平衡性，同时将额外计算开销降至最低且不影响逆设计精度。

Result: 在两个非晶材料数据集上的广泛实验结果表明，AMGenC成功实现了其设计目标，即保证生成电荷平衡的样本，同时保持较高的逆设计精度。

Conclusion: AMGenC通过元素噪声、逐步软投影和最终离散投影的组合，成功实现了电荷平衡保证的生成，同时不牺牲逆设计精度，且计算开销极小。在两个非晶材料数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了其设计目标的有效性。

Abstract: Amorphous (disordered) materials are solids that have shown great potential in various domains, including energy storage, thermal management, and advanced materials. Unlike crystalline materials that can be described by unit cells containing a few to hundreds of atoms, amorphous materials require larger simulation cells with at least hundreds to thousands of atoms. To advance the design of amorphous materials with desired properties and facilitate the exploration of their vast design space, generative inverse design has emerged as a promising approach. It aims to directly output materials with properties closely aligned with the desired ones using probabilistic generative models conditioned on desired properties, which can be more resource efficient than the traditional trial-and-error approach. However, due to the inherent stochasticity of probabilistic generative models, when element assignments are unconstrained, a large portion of generated materials may be charge unbalanced, and no existing methods can effectively mitigate this limitation. In this work, we propose AMGenC, a new generative inverse design method for amorphous materials that can guarantee the generation of charge balanced samples, with minimal additional computational overhead and without sacrificing inverse design accuracy. AMGenC achieves this through an element noise that gives the generation process a starting point centered around charge balance, and the combination of a per-step soft projection and a final discrete projection for steering the elements toward exact charge balance throughout the generation. We perform extensive experiments on two amorphous materials datasets. Experimental results provide evidence that AMGenC achieves its design goal.

</details>


### [138] [Green Physics-Informed Machine Learning Models For Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27638)
*Daisy R Bradley,Elizabeth J Cross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了结构健康监测中黑盒和灰盒（物理信息）机器学习模型的环境影响。研究表明，灰盒模型在保持高预测性能的同时，能够减少计算运行时间和碳排放，为开发更环保、更高效的结构监测模型提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动方法存在局限性，特别是在缺乏相关环境和操作条件数据的情况下；机器学习在结构工程和结构健康监测中的应用需要更高效、更环保的解决方案。

Method: 通过"绿色"视角比较黑盒模型和灰盒模型；评估物理约束模型的环境影响和运行时性能差异；利用结构健康监测案例研究进行验证。

Result: 灰盒模型具有高外推性能，能够减少运行时间，从而降低碳排放；物理信息模型可在保持高性能的同时实现较低的能耗。

Conclusion: 开发具有降低计算成本同时保持高性能的物理信息模型，通过结构健康监测案例研究说明；灰盒模型在保持高外推性能的同时可减少运行时间和碳排放。

Abstract: Machine learning continues to emerge as an important tool to be utilised within structural engineering and structural health monitoring, due to its ability to accurately and quickly perform both regression and classification tasks. However, a purely data driven approach has its limitations, particularly where we lack data from relevant environmental and operational conditions, a situation that has led to the development of physics-informed machine learners for structural health monitoring. These "grey-box" models take into account the physical insight that an engineer would have about the structure they are modelling and have shown promising results in the structural engineering field among many others. This work compares black and grey-box models through a "green" lens, comparing them in terms of their environmental impact, and investigating how the high extrapolative performance of grey-box models can reduce their runtimes and therefore carbon emissions. The authors aim to develop physics-informed models with reduced computational costs, while maintaining high performance, illustrated through a structural health monitoring case study.

</details>


### [139] [ANCORA: Learning to Question via Manifold-Anchored Self-Play for Verifiable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27644)
*Chengcao Yang,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从"学习回答"到"学习提问"的范式转变，通过ANCORA框架让语言模型自主生成可验证的问题、求解并利用反馈进行自我改进。框架包含两个角色：Proposer合成新规范，Solver生成验证解。三大稳定机制（两层组相对更新、自蒸馏SFT、UCB引导的课程图）防止稀疏验证信号导致的Proposer崩溃。在Verus实现中，Dafny2Verus通过率从基线26.6%提升至81.5%，超越PSV自博弈基线15.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current approaches learn to answer (generate solutions), but the paper proposes learning to question (generating verifiable problems) as a paradigm shift. The core challenge is that sparse verifier feedback drives Proposer collapse even under RL-aligned rewards, requiring specific stabilizers to prevent this failure mode.

Method: Three load-bearing mechanisms: (1) Two-level group-relative updates coupling Proposer advantages across specifications with Solver advantages across solution attempts; (2) Iterative self-distilled SFT projecting the base model onto its valid-output manifold before RL; (3) UCB-guided Curriculum DAG growing only through strictly filtered, novel, Solver-verified specifications. The framework uses a unified policy with alternating Proposer (synthesizes specifications) and Solver (produces verified solutions) roles.

Result: Test-time-training setting: 81.5% pass@1 on Dafny2Verus (vs 26.6% SFT baseline and 65.7% for PSV self-play). Transfer setting: 36.2% on MBPP and 17.2% on HumanEval when training from Dafny2Verus seeds. Outperforms PSV baseline by 15.8 points despite PSV using 1-shot inference vs ANCORA's 0-shot evaluation.

Conclusion: ANCORA enables a unified policy to alternate between specification synthesis (Proposer) and solution verification (Solver), with three critical stabilizers preventing Proposer collapse under sparse verifier feedback. The approach achieves 81.5% pass@1 on Dafny2Verus (vs 26.6% baseline) and shows transferability to MBPP (36.2%) and HumanEval (17.2%).

Abstract: We propose a paradigm shift from learning to answer to learning to question: can a language model generate verifiable problems, solve them, and turn the resulting feedback into self-improvement without human supervision? We introduce ANCORA, an anchored-curriculum framework in which a unified policy alternates between a Proposer that synthesizes novel specifications and a Solver that produces verified solutions. ANCORA rests on three load-bearing mechanisms: a two-level group-relative update that couples Proposer advantages across specifications with Solver advantages across solution attempts; iterative self-distilled SFT that projects the base model onto its valid-output manifold before RL; and a UCB-guided Curriculum DAG that grows only through strictly filtered, novel, Solver-verified specifications. These stabilizers are necessary because sparse verifier feedback otherwise drives Proposer collapse even under MLRL-aligned rewards. Instantiated in Verus, ANCORA lifts Dafny2Verus pass@1 from a 26.6% SFT baseline to 81.5% in the test-time-training setting under 0-shot evaluation, outperforming the PSV self-play baseline by 15.8 points despite PSV using 1-shot inference; in a separate transfer setting, training from Dafny2Verus seeds yields 36.2% and 17.2% pass@1 on held-out MBPP and HumanEval.

</details>


### [140] [Optimized Deferral for Imbalanced Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27723)
*Corinna Cortes,Anqi Mao,Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对两阶段"学会延迟"设置中的专家不平衡问题，提出将延迟损失优化建模为成本敏感学习问题，推导了边界损失函数并设计了MIL算法，在图像分类和LLM路由任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段"学会延迟"设置中存在专家不平衡问题，导致次优性能，延迟算法倾向于偏好多数专家。

Method: 将延迟损失优化建模为输入-专家域上的新型成本敏感学习问题，推导了针对该场景的边界损失函数及理论保证，并开发了成本敏感学习新算法。

Result: 在图像分类和真实大规模语言模型（LLM）路由任务上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: MIL（Margin-based Imbalanced Learning to Defer）是专为专家不平衡场景设计的调优算法，在图像分类和LLM路由任务上均展现出对现有基线方法的明显改进效果。

Abstract: Learning algorithms can be significantly improved by routing complex or uncertain inputs to specialized experts, balancing accuracy with computational cost. This approach, known as learning to defer, is essential in domains like natural language generation, medical diagnosis, and computer vision, where an effective deferral can reduce errors at low extra resource consumption. However, the two-stage learning to defer setting, which leverages existing predictors such as a collection of LLMs or other classifiers, often faces challenges due to an expert imbalance problem. This imbalance can lead to suboptimal performance, with deferral algorithms favoring the majority expert. We present a comprehensive study of two-stage learning to defer in expert imbalance settings. We cast the deferral loss optimization as a novel cost-sensitive learning problem over the input-expert domain. We derive new margin-based loss functions and guarantees tailored to this setting, and develop novel algorithms for cost-sensitive learning. Leveraging these results, we design principled deferral algorithms, MILD (Margin-based Imbalanced Learning to Defer), specifically suited for expert imbalance settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, showing clear improvements over existing baselines on both image classification and real-world Large Language Model (LLM) routing tasks.

</details>


### [141] [Differential Subgroup Discovery: Characterizing Where Two Populations Differ, and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27741)
*Sascha Xu,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在特征空间中识别两个群体的差异结构，提出差分子群（Differential Subgroup）概念，即在共享相似特征的个体中表现出显著结果差异的子群。提出了可进行因果解释的优化目标，并设计了梯度方法 DiffSub 在表格数据中发现可解释的差分子群，应用于临床分析、模型诊断和治疗效果研究等多个场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人口差异分析难以识别哪些特征组合导致了系统性差异，特别是在不同群体间存在差异的特定特征空间区域缺乏有效发现方法。

Method: 提出 DiffSub 方法，采用基于梯度优化的方式发现差分子群；建立了使子群能够获得因果解释的理论条件。

Result: 在合成基准数据集、医学案例研究、模型误差分析和治疗效果场景中进行实验，DiffSub 能够识别出信息丰富的差分子群，揭示人口差异的来源和原因。

Conclusion: DiffSub 通过梯度方法在表格数据中发现可解释的差分子群，能够有效揭示人口差异的结构性来源，并在临床分析、模型诊断和治疗效果研究中具有应用价值。

Abstract: We study the problem of understanding where two populations differ within a feature space, which we formalize in the concept of a differential subgroup: a subset of individuals from both populations who, despite sharing similar characteristics, exhibit exceptional differences in a target outcome. Differential subgroups reveal the regions of the feature space where population-level gaps are most pronounced and can help practitioners identify the covariate combinations that are structurally responsible for these differences, e.g.~in clinical analysis, model diagnostics, or treatment-effect studies. We introduce a general optimization objective for discovering differential subgroups and establish conditions under which the resulting subgroups admit a causal interpretation of population differences. We propose DiffSub, a gradient-based approach that discovers interpretable differential subgroups in tabular data. Across synthetic benchmarks, medical case studies, model-error analyses, and treatment-effect settings, DiffSub identifies informative subgroups that reveal where population differences arise and why.

</details>


### [142] [Linear-Core Surrogates: Smooth Loss Functions with Linear Rates for Classification and Structured Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27742)
*Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的损失函数族Linear-Core Surrogates，通过将线性核心与平滑尾部结合，同时获得光滑性的优化优势和基于间隔损失的统计效率。该方法在结构化预测中实现23倍加速，并在含噪数据上优于交叉熵损失2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fundamental trade-off in loss function choice: smooth losses (Cross-Entropy) enable fast optimization but have slow consistency bounds; piecewise-linear losses (Hinge) have fast linear consistency but suffer from non-differentiability. No existing method achieves both simultaneously.

Method: Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates: convex loss functions combining a linear core stitched to a smooth tail, enabling unbiased stochastic gradient estimators that bypass quadratic complexity of exact inference (e.g., Viterbi) in structured prediction.

Result: 23× speedup over Structured SVMs on large-vocabulary sequence tagging tasks; 2.6% improvement over Cross-Entropy on corrupted CIFAR-10.

Conclusion: Linear-Core Surrogates unify optimization smoothness with statistical efficiency, achieve 23× speedup on sequence tagging, and outperform Cross-Entropy by 2.6% on noisy CIFAR-10, with theoretical guarantees of differentiability and linear H-consistency bounds.

Abstract: The choice of loss function in classification involves a fundamental trade-off: smooth losses (like Cross-Entropy) enable fast optimization rates but yield slow square-root consistency bounds, while piecewise-linear losses (like Hinge) offer fast linear consistency rates but suffer from non-differentiability. We propose Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates, a new family of convex loss functions that resolve this tension by stitching a linear core to a smooth tail. We prove that these surrogates are differentiable everywhere while retaining strict linear $H$-consistency bounds, effectively combining the optimization benefits of smoothness with the statistical efficiency of margin-based losses. In the structured prediction setting, we show that this smoothness unlocks a massive computational and energy advantage: it allows for an unbiased stochastic gradient estimator that bypasses the quadratic complexity $O(|\mathscr{Y}|^2)$ of exact inference (e.g., Viterbi). Empirically, our method achieves a 23$\times$ speedup over Structured SVMs on large-vocabulary sequence tagging tasks and demonstrates superior robustness to instance-dependent label noise, outperforming Cross-Entropy by 2.6% on corrupted CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [143] [Hyper-Dimensional Fingerprints as Molecular Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27810)
*Jonas Teufel,Luca Torresi,André Eberhard,Pascal Friederich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Computational molecular representations underpin virtual screening, property prediction, and materials discovery. Conventional fingerprints are efficient and deterministic but lose structural information through hash-based compression, particularly at low dimensionalities. Learned representations from graph neural networks recover this expressiveness but require task-specific training and substantial computational resources. Here we introduce hyperdimensional fingerprints (HDF), which replace the learned transformations of message-passing neural networks with algebraic operations on high-dimensional vectors, producing deterministic molecular representations without any training. Across diverse property prediction benchmarks, HDF outperforms conventional fingerprints in the majority of tasks while exhibiting greater consistency across datasets and models. Crucially, HDF embeddings preserve molecular similarity faithfully: at 32 dimensions, distances in HDF space achieve a 0.9 Pearson correlation with graph edit distance, compared to 0.55 for Morgan fingerprints at equivalent size. This structural fidelity persists at low dimensions where hash-based methods degrade, allowing simple nearest-neighbor regression to remain predictive with as few as 64 components. We further demonstrate the practical impact in Bayesian molecular optimization, where HDF-based surrogate models achieve substantially improved sample efficiency in regimes where Morgan fingerprints perform comparably to random search.
