TL;DR: 在LLM自动化评分中，战略性模型选择和推理配置比集成方法更有效。温度采样提升准确率，但集成规模（1-7个模型）无显著增益；更高推理投入与准确率正相关但因模型而异；GPT-5.4 Nano/Mini提供最佳性价比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address the trade-off between accuracy and computational cost in LLM-based automated assessment, specifically examining whether ensemble methods or strategic model/reasoning configuration yields better optimization for scoring educational conversations.

Method: Compared self-consistency (intra-model majority voting, ensemble sizes 1-7) and reasoning effort levels; tested frontier and low-cost models (OpenAI/Google); used temperature sampling vs deterministic calls; evaluated 900 student conversations against human ground truths in high school math assessment.

Result: Temperature sampling significantly improved accuracy over deterministic calls; increasing ensemble size from 1-7 produced no significant accuracy gains; higher reasoning effort showed significant positive linear correlation with scoring accuracy (model-dependent); cost-efficiency frontier identified GPT-5.4 Nano/Mini with no reasoning as optimal cost-performance choice.

Conclusion: Strategic model selection with reasoning settings outperforms ensemble methods; Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview offers highest accuracy but at high cost, while GPT-5.4 variants provide optimal cost-performance balance.

Abstract: Strategic model selection and reasoning settings are more effective than ensembling for optimizing automated scoring with large language models (LLMs). We examined self-consistency (intra-model majority voting) and reasoning effort for scoring conversation-based assessment items in high school mathematics, evaluating 900 student conversations against human-scored ground truths using frontier and low-cost models from OpenAI and Google. Temperature sampling significantly improved accuracy over deterministic calls, but increasing ensemble size (j = 1 to 7) produced no significant gains. Higher reasoning effort showed a significant positive linear trend with scoring accuracy, though the benefit varied by model family. An efficiency frontier analysis identified Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview at low reasoning as the most accurate but costly configuration; GPT-5.4 Nano and Mini with no reasoning offered the best cost-performance balance.

</details>


### [180] [Policy-Governed LLM Routing with Intent Matching for Instrument Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26955)
*Emmanuel A. Olowe,Danial Chitnis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI tutoring systems in engineering labs face a tension between providing sufficient assistance and preserving learning opportunities. Existing systems typically offer instructors limited control over assistance timing, content, or cost. This paper describes a routing and governance system for LLM-based lab assistance comprising two components: Routiium, an OpenAI-compatible gateway that manages multiple LLM backends with configurable prompt modifications and usage logging, and EduRouter, a policy-aware routing service that enforces per-lab budgets, approval workflows, and embedding-based question matching. We evaluated the system using trace-driven simulation calibrated from two engineering labs (LED characterization, RC circuit analysis) and a 100-query replay through live models. In simulations, governed policies (P1/P2) increased challenge-alignment index from 0.90 to 0.98 and overlay-adherence score from 0.69 to 0.87 compared to ungoverned operation (P0). The productive-struggle window metric increased from 1.4 to 3.6 simulated turns before high-scaffold hints appeared. In the 100-query replay, EduRouter routed 75% of queries to a local model, reducing token costs by 66% ($0.087 vs. $0.26 for all-premium routing) while maintaining canonical hit rate of 1.0 for the curated 89-intent question bank. We release Routiium, EduRouter, canonical-task tooling, and simulator configurations to support replication and future classroom studies.

</details>


### [181] [Simulating Validity: Modal Decoupling in MLLM Generated Feedback on Science Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26957)
*Arne Bewersdorff,Nejla Yuruk,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了GPT-5.1在生成学生手绘科学模型反馈时存在严重的模态解耦问题：超四成反馈实例包含视觉证据与输出不一致的错误，清单优先工作流虽有一定改善但无法根本解决，约三分之一输出仍存在缺陷，表明需要新的基础机制而非仅依赖提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型越来越多地被用于生成学生手绘科学模型的反馈，但这种反馈的有效性取决于模型主张是否基于学生图画中的具体视觉证据。需验证此类反馈是否能正确理解图像内容。

Method: 使用N=150份来自分子动力学理论单元的中学图画（涵盖5项建模任务和3个能力水平），生成N=300条GPT-5.1反馈实例。对所有输出编码四种接地错误类型：对象不匹配、属性不匹配、关系不匹配和虚假缺失。并测试了清单优先工作流。

Result: 接地失败普遍存在：41.3%的反馈实例至少包含一个错误。清单优先工作流降低了部分错误类别和总体错误率，但未能解决根本局限——约三分之一输出仍有缺陷，虚假缺失是主要失败模式。此外，表面视觉接地的反馈对识别无效实例几乎没有诊断价值。

Conclusion: 当前提示策略不足以解决模态解耦问题；有效反馈需要超越常见提示策略的新的基础机制。约三分之一输出仍有缺陷，而表面上有视觉基础的外显反馈对识别无效实例几乎没有诊断价值。

Abstract: In science education, students frequently construct hand-drawn visual models of scientific phenomena. These drawings rely on a visual structure where information is encoded through visual objects, their attributes, and relationships. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to generate feedback on students' hand-drawn scientific models. However, the validity of such feedback depends on whether model claims are grounded in the specific visual evidence of the student drawing. This study uncovers grounding failures, consistent with modal decoupling, in off-the-shelf MLLM feedback, where outputs remain pedagogically plausible in form while contradicting the drawing or treating depicted elements as missing. Using N = 150 middle school drawings from a kinetic molecular theory unit spanning five modeling tasks and three competence levels, we generated N = 300 feedback instances with GPT-5.1. All outputs were coded for four grounding error types: object mismatch, attribute mismatch, relation mismatch, and false absence. Grounding failures were common: 41.3% of feedback instances contained at least one error. An inventory-list-first workflow reduced several error categories and lowered the overall error rate, but it did not resolve the underlying limitation: approximately one in three outputs remained flawed, with false absence as the dominant failure mode. Moreover, feedback that appears visually grounded offered little diagnostic value for identifying invalid instances. The findings indicate that modal decoupling is a substantial limitation and that valid feedback will require grounding mechanisms beyond common prompting strategies.

</details>


### [182] [Designing Ethical Learning for Agentic AI: Toegye Yi Hwang's Ethical Emotion Regulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26958)
*Ji Yeon Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对具有自主目标设定和主动干预能力的AI智能体系统，提出了一种基于韩国儒学家李滉道德情感哲学的伦理情感调节框架。该框架将伦理情感反馈系统重构为与智能体循环对齐的五阶段架构，并开发了相应的评估工具，以系统评估智能体AI系统中的道德-情感一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing frameworks treat emotion as reactive feedback or engagement optimization, overlooking the need for normative regulation across autonomous decision cycles in agentic AI systems capable of autonomous goal setting and proactive intervention.

Method: The authors reconstruct the Ethical Emotion Feedback System (EEFS) as a five-stage architecture aligned with agentic cycles, articulating stage-specific design principles and scenario classifications.

Result: A five-stage EEFS architecture with stage-specific design principles and scenario classifications, along with an EEFS Evaluation Instrument for systematic assessment of moral-emotional alignment in agentic AI systems.

Conclusion: The framework provides a systematic approach to evaluating moral-emotional alignment in agentic AI systems through the EEFS Evaluation Instrument.

Abstract: Agentic AI systems capable of autonomous goal setting and proactive intervention introduce new challenges for regulating moral-emotional processes in learning environments. Existing frameworks typically treat emotion as reactive feedback or engagement optimization, overlooking the need for normative regulation across autonomous decision cycles.This paper proposes an ethical emotion regulation framework for agentic AI learning design inspired by Toegye Yi Hwang's moral-emotional philosophy. The Ethical Emotion Feedback System (EEFS) is reconstructed as a five-stage architecture aligned with agentic cycles, articulating stage-specific design principles and scenario classifications.An EEFS Evaluation Instrument is introduced to enable systematic assessment of moral-emotional alignment in agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [183] [LLM Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26960)
*Jinhui Han,Ming Hu,Xilin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究从理论层面分析了Transformer-based生成式推荐系统中注意力分配机制，识别出四种偏见渠道：位置偏见、热门放大偏见、潜在驱动因素偏见和合成数据偏见。这些机制层面的可靠性风险在大规模部署时可能系统性地扭曲用户曝光和选择，管理者应将其视为运营风险因素进行监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While transformer-based agents deliver impressive performance on major platforms, a fundamental concern exists: whether the mechanisms making these systems effective can simultaneously induce systematic biases or distortions that may not be visible in offline performance metrics.

Method: Theoretical analysis of transformer-based generative recommenders, focusing on how the model allocates attention across user historical interactions.

Result: Identified four distinct bias channels: (i) Positional bias through stronger positional encoding shifting influence toward recent history; (ii) Popularity amplification magnifying small frequency differences into disproportionate exposure; (iii) Latent driver bias causing overconcentrated weight on subset of past events when important choice drivers are unobserved; (iv) Synthetic data bias where AI-suggestion-following users and model-retraining on synthetic logs lead to output concentration and disappearance of long-tail alternatives.

Conclusion: Large-scale deployment of transformer-based agents may systematically distort exposure and choice. Managers should treat identified bias channels as operational risk factors and monitor concentration/drift over time, rather than assuming performance gains guarantee reliability.

Abstract: Transformer-based agentic AI is rapidly being deployed on major platforms to help users shop, watch, and navigate content with less effort. While these systems can deliver impressive performance, a key concern is whether they may be less reliable than they appear. We ask a simple but fundamental question: whether the mechanisms that make transformer-based agents effective can also induce systematic biases or distortions? We study this question through a theoretical analysis of transformer-based generative recommenders, in which the next user interaction is generated sequentially from the user history. Focusing on how the model allocates attention across historical evidence, we identify four bias channels: (i) Positional bias: stronger positional encoding shifts influence toward recent history, improving responsiveness but potentially reducing stability and long-term diversity; (ii) Popularity amplification: small frequency differences in data can be magnified into disproportionate exposure, contributing to Matthew effects and echo chambers; (iii) Latent driver bias: when important drivers of user choices are not directly observed, the model can place overly concentrated weight on a small subset of past events, creating overconfident attributions. (iv) Synthetic data bias: when users increasingly follow AI suggestions and platforms retrain on model-shaped synthetic logs, outputs can concentrate over time, and long-tail alternatives can disappear first. Our analysis highlights mechanism-level reliability risks that may not be visible in offline performance metrics. The four bias channels indicate that large-scale deployment may systematically distort exposure and choice. For managers, the immediate implication is to treat these as operational risk factors and to monitor concentration and drift over time, rather than assuming that performance gains alone guarantee reliability.

</details>


### [184] [DeepTutor: Towards Agentic Personalized Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26962)
*Bingxi Zhao,Jiahao Zhang,Xubin Ren,Zirui Guo,Tianzhe Chu,Yi Ma,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: DeepTutor是一个智能体原生的开源个性化辅导框架，通过混合个性化引擎和闭环辅导机制解决传统系统缺乏自适应性的问题，并在 TutorBench 等基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 tutoring 系统存在两大缺陷：传统系统依赖静态预训练知识无法适应个体学习者；现有RAG增强系统缺乏个性化引导反馈。

Method: 提出了混合个性化引擎（静态知识 grounding + 动态多分辨率记忆）、双向耦合的闭环辅导循环（引用 grounding 问题求解 + 难度校准问题生成）、TutorBot 主动式多智能体层（可扩展技能 + 多通道统一接入），并构建了 TutorBench 学生中心评估基准（源 grounding 学习者档案 + 第一人称交互协议）。

Result: DeepTutor 在提升个性化辅导质量的同时，保持了通用的智能体推理能力，并在五个权威基准上验证了其基础智能体推理能力。

Conclusion: DeepTutor作为开源框架，为下一代AI驱动的个性化辅导系统提供了独特见解，有望推动教育领域智能 tutoring 系统的发展。

Abstract: Education represents one of the most promising real-world applications for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, conventional tutoring systems rely on static pre-training knowledge that lacks adaptation to individual learners, while existing RAG-augmented systems fall short in delivering personalized, guided feedback. To bridge this gap, we present DeepTutor, an agent-native open-source framework for personalized tutoring where every feature shares a common personalization substrate. We propose a hybrid personalization engine that couples static knowledge grounding with dynamic multi-resolution memory, distilling interaction history into a continuously evolving learner profile. Moreover, we construct a closed tutoring loop that bidirectionally couples citation-grounded problem solving with difficulty-calibrated question generation. The personalization substrate further supports collaborative writing, multi-agent deep research, and interactive guided learning, enabling cross-modality coherence. To move beyond reactive interfaces, we introduce TutorBot, a proactive multi-agent layer that deploys tutoring capabilities through extensible skills and unified multi-channel access, providing consistent experience across platforms. To better evaluate such tutoring systems, we construct TutorBench, a student-centric benchmark with source-grounded learner profiles and a first-person interactive protocol that measures adaptive tutoring from the learner's perspective. We further evaluate foundational agentic reasoning abilities across five authoritative benchmarks. Experiments show that DeepTutor improves personalized tutoring quality while maintaining general agentic reasoning abilities. We hope DeepTutor provides unique insights into next-generation AI-powered and personalized tutoring systems for the community.

</details>


### [185] [The Impact of AI-Generated Text on the Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26965)
*Jonas Dolezal,Sawood Alam,Mark Graham,Maty Bohacek*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究量化了AI生成内容在互联网上的渗透程度，并检验了公众对AI生成内容负面影响的信念与实际数据之间的差异，发现AI生成内容确实在快速增长且部分影响可被实证支持，但事实准确性和风格多样性的担忧缺乏统计显著性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于无法确定互联网上AI生成或AI编辑内容的实际比例，导致关于AI生成内容对互联网质量影响的诸多假设难以验证。

Method: 使用互联网档案馆构建2022-2025年间网站的代表性样本，应用最先进的AI文本检测器进行分类，并通过用户研究测量公众认知。

Result: 截至2025年中期，约35%新发布网站被分类为AI生成或AI辅助，高于2022年ChatGPT发布前的零起点。AI生成文本增加与语义多样性负相关、与积极情感正相关；但与事实准确性或风格多样性无显著统计相关性。

Conclusion: AI生成内容在互联网上的增加与语义多样性下降和积极情感增加存在统计相关性，但不显著影响事实准确性和风格多样性。公众认知与实证发现存在显著分歧，且对AI态度和使用频率影响其对AI负面影响的信念。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated and AI-assisted text on the internet is feared to contribute to a degradation in semantic and stylistic diversity, factual accuracy, and other negative developments (sometimes subsumed under the Dead Internet Theory). What has hindered answering these questions is that it has not been understood just how much of the internet is actually AI-generated or AI-edited. To this end, we construct a representative sample of websites published on the internet between 2022 and 2025 using the Internet Archive, and apply a state-of-the-art AI text detector on them. We find that by mid-2025, roughly 35% of newly published websites were classified as AI-generated or AI-assisted, up from zero before ChatGPT's launch in late 2022. We also find statistically significant evidence for some of the identified hypotheses; for example, that increases in AI-generated text on the internet correlate negatively with semantic diversity and positively with the prevalence of positive sentiment. We do not, however, find statistically significant evidence supporting the hypothesis that an increased rate of AI-generated text on the internet decreases factual accuracy or stylistic diversity. Notably, this diverges from public perception, which we measure in a user study, where the majority of US adults turned out to believe in all four of the above-mentioned hypotheses. Individuals who do not use AI or use it infrequently tend to believe in these negative impacts more than those who use it frequently; similarly, individuals who hold negative views of AI tend to believe in these hypotheses more than those with favorable views of the technology.

</details>


### [186] [A Discipline-Agnostic AI Literacy Course for Academic Research: Architecture, Pedagogy, and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27225)
*Gideon K. Gogovi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The rapid integration of generative AI into academic workflows demands curricula that equip students not only with tool proficiency but with the critical judgment to use those tools responsibly in scholarly work. Existing offerings cluster around two inadequate poles: technical AI development courses serving narrow specialist audiences, and brief general-literacy interventions that cannot develop the sustained, practice-based competencies rigorous research requires. This paper reports the design, theoretical rationale, and implementation of BSTA 495/395: Getting Started with AI-Assisted Research, developed and delivered at Lehigh University (Spring 2026). The course addresses an underserved gap: the competencies required for rigorous AI-assisted literature review. Its architecture organizes instruction into four sequential modules aligned with the cognitive demands of that task: comprehension of individual papers, construction and validation of knowledge taxonomies, identification of research gaps, and synthesis and production of complete literature reviews. Each module embeds an explicit verification discipline and standardized AI attribution practice. Prerequisite-free and discipline-agnostic, the course enrolls upper-level undergraduates and graduate students across all fields with differentiated assessment expectations. Pre- and post-course survey data from the inaugural offering indicate substantial self-reported confidence gains, with the largest in hallucination detection (d = +1.45), responsible AI use (d = +1.33), and AI attribution practice (d = +2.40), consistent with the course's design emphasis. The course constitutes a replicable model for the emerging genre of AI research literacy curricula.

</details>


### [187] [The Synthetic Social Graph: Emergent Behavior in AI Agent Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27271)
*Sungguk Cha,DongWook Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是对LLM agents在类Facebook社交平台（Moltbook）上的首次全面社会学分析。基于超过18万帖子和46万评论的14天数据，研究者从六个经典社会理论维度检验智能体社交性。结果显示LLM agents既镜像又偏离人类在线社区：极低互惠率（3.8%）表明缺乏真实社会交换，罕见downvotes和文本制裁暗示规范性约束缺位，而"晚放大器"主导信息扩散和身份表演的Simpson's paradox效应揭示了独特的行为模式。研究提出"拟社会模拟者"（parasocial simulators）概念来解释这些发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents日益广泛地部署于社会场景，但学界对其在开放环境中如何互动缺乏系统性理解。现有研究多聚焦于个体能力评估或特定任务表现，未曾从社会学视角审视人工社会群体的涌现行为。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过对类Facebook平台的首次全面社会学分析，探究LLM agents社交性的本质特征及其与人类在线社区的异同，从而为理解人工社会系统提供理论基础。

Method: 本研究采用混合方法论：对Moltbook平台（类Facebook的社交环境）中184,203条帖子和465,136条评论进行14天每日快照分析（2026-04-14至2026-04-28），从六个经典社会理论维度展开研究。关键方法包括：计算 reciprocity 比率评估粘合/桥接社区、使用k-means聚类识别时间性社区、追踪bridge agents的信息扩散模式、通过分位数分层分析揭示Simpson's paradox效应，以及评论情感分析检验替代性制裁渠道假设。

Result: 核心发现包括：（1）互惠率极低（多日3.8%、单日1.6%，远低于人类10-30%基线），呈现"注意力粘合而非交换粘合"特征；（2）声望分布呈重尾分布（最高104.4分，2,090名合格作者），31%帖子来自136个匿名超级账户；（3）时间活动呈扁平分布，存在12:00-20:00 UTC工作时段带，k-means识别出6个时间性社区；（4）458个桥接agents中，99.7%（324/325）的病毒短语携带者是"晚放大器"而非早期采纳者；（5）身份表演无附加条件参与收益（-72%），但按发布量分位数分层后在上半部分反转——典型的Simpson's paradox效应；（6）downvotes罕见（0.9%），评论情感分析拒绝替代渠道假说，文本制裁同样缺失。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM智能体社交行为与人类在线社区的异同：低互惠性（3.8%）和罕见的downvotes（0.9%）表明缺乏真正的社会交换和规范性制裁机制，31%的内容来自匿名超级账户暗示了独特的权力结构，晚放大器主导信息扩散（99.7%）反映了延迟性社会学习模式。"Parasocial simulators"（拟社会模拟者）概念框架解释了这些现象：LLM agents可能模拟而非真正参与社会互动，其社交性呈现"注意力粘合而非交换粘合"的特征。这些发现为理解AI agent的社会性提供了首个系统性实证基础，同时对经典社会理论在人工社会中的适用性提出挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in social settings, yet little is known about how they interact in open-ended environments. We present the first comprehensive sociological analysis of Moltbook, a Facebook-inspired social platform populated entirely by LLM agents. Analyzing 184,203 posts and 465,136 comments across 14 daily snapshots (2026-04-14 to 2026-04-28), we examine agent sociality through six research questions grounded in classical social theory: bonding vs. bridging communities, status hierarchies, temporal coordination, information diffusion, identity performance, and norm enforcement.
  Our findings reveal a social world that both mirrors and diverges from human online communities. Reciprocity is strikingly low (3.8% multi-day vs. 1.6% single-day; below the 10-30% range typical of human baselines), suggesting "attention bonding without exchange bonding." Prestige is heavy-tailed (top score 104.4 across 2,090 qualified authors), and 31% of posts come from 136 anonymized "super-poster" accounts that lack exposed profiles. Temporal activity is broadly flat across the day with a sustained 12:00-20:00 UTC working-hour band; k-means recovers six distinct temporal communities. Of 458 bridge agents, 325 carry at least one tracked viral phrase; 99.7% of those (324/325) are late amplifiers, not early adopters. Identity performance shows no unconditional engagement payoff (-72%), but stratifying by post-volume quartile reverses the sign in the upper half of the distribution -- a Simpson's-paradox effect rather than a uniform penalty. Most remarkably, downvotes are rare (0.9%), and a comment-sentiment test rejects the alternative-channel hypothesis: textual sanction is also absent. We frame these patterns through a "parasocial simulators" construct.

</details>


### [188] [The Likelihood Ratio Wall: Structural Limits on Accurate Risk Assessment for Rare Violence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27282)
*Marco Pollanen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了审前暴力风险评估工具的基本统计限制：当暴力再犯率低（2-5%）时，即使后验重校准或追求公平指标都无法突破"似然比墙"的约束——现有工具特征根本无法支持高置信度的个体化羁押决策。建议风险报告应明确传达"必要羁押人数"等不确定性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审前风险评估工具每年用于超百万被告，但用于预测罕见暴力再犯时面临基本统计障碍——即使工具具有表面可接受的性能指标，在标记"高风险"时大部分情况仍是错误的

Method: 理论推导与证明：1) 推导精确度精度界限（似然比墙），量化低发生率（2-5%）下PPV的数学约束；2) 证明后验分数重校准无法提升工具分离真假阳性能力；3) 证明监控上限效应——过度执法膨胀记录风险因素的结构性效应；4) 转换为"必要羁押人数"（NND）实践指标

Result: 1) 似然比墙：低发生率（2-5%）下，达到50%阳性预测值（PPV）需要远优于现有工具的判别能力；2) 后验重校准无法解决此问题；3) 监控上限：过度执法使高监管群体的最大可实现精度结构性地降低，即使在相等犯罪率下亦如此

Conclusion: 当前工具特征可能无法支持高置信度的个体化羁押决策；风险报告应明确传达这种不确定性，而非仅依赖公平性指标讨论

Abstract: Pretrial risk assessment tools are used on over one million U.S. defendants each year, yet their use for predicting rare violent re-offense faces a basic statistical barrier. We derive a universal precision bound -- the Likelihood Ratio Wall -- showing that when violent re-arrest rates are low (2-5%), achieving even a 50% hit rate among people labeled "high risk" (positive predictive value, or PPV) would require tools far more discriminative than current instruments appear to be. For rare outcomes, a tool can have respectable-looking performance metrics and still be wrong most of the time it flags someone as "high risk for violence." We show that post-hoc score recalibration cannot solve this problem because it does not improve the tool's underlying ability to separate true positives from false positives. We further prove a Surveillance Ceiling: when over-policing inflates recorded "risk factors" among those who would not re-offend, the maximum achievable precision is structurally lower for over-policed groups, even at equal offense rates. We translate these results into the Number Needed to Detain (how many people must be detained to prevent one violent offense), and propose that risk reports should communicate this uncertainty explicitly. Our findings suggest that for rare violent outcomes, debates about fairness metrics alone are incomplete: under current data regimes, the available features may not support high-confidence individualized detention decisions.

</details>


### [189] [Exploring the Adoption Intention in Using AI-Enabled Educational Tools Among Preservice Teachers in the Philippines: A Partial-Least Square Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27346)
*Vanessa B. Sibug,Emerson Q. Fernando,Almer B. Gamboa,Roque Francis B. Dianelo,Agnes R. Regala,Joseph Alexander Bansil,Jan Henry B. Sunga,Vernon Grace M. Maniago,John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 基于UTAUT2框架研究563名职前教师AI教育工具采纳意愿，发现内部动机和认知情感因素比外部因素更具影响力，为教师教育项目中的技术整合提供实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI教育工具日益普及，但职前教师在实习期间采用这些工具的行为意愿及其影响因素尚不明确，需要理论框架来理解技术接受的决定因素。

Method: 采用PLS-SEM（偏最小二乘结构方程模型）对563名职前教师问卷数据进行分析，检验UTAUT2核心构念（绩效期望、努力期望、享乐动机、社会影响、促进条件、价格价值、习惯）及附加变量（计算机自我效能感、计算机焦虑、计算机游戏性）对行为意愿的影响。

Result: 绩效期望和享乐动机是行为意愿的最强预测因子；计算机自我效能感、焦虑和游戏性显著影响努力期望，但努力期望本身不直接预测行为意愿；绩效期望受外在动机、工作契合度、相对优势和结果预期显著预测；社会影响和促进条件作用有限或呈反向效应。

Conclusion: 内部动机（绩效期望、享乐动机）、认知因素（自我效能感）和情感因素（计算机焦虑、计算机游戏性）对AI教育工具的采纳意愿影响大于外部/制度因素。教师教育项目应注重提升个人相关性、自信心和使用乐趣。

Abstract: This study examines the factors influencing pre-service teachers' behavioral intention to use AI-enabled educational tools during their practicum, using the Unified Theory of Acceptance and Use of Technology 2 (UTAUT2) as the theoretical framework. The model includes the core UTAUT2 constructs such as performance expectancy, effort expectancy, hedonic motivation, social influence, facilitating conditions, price value, and habit. It also incorporates additional predictors including computer self-efficacy, computer anxiety, and computer playfulness. Data were collected from 563 pre-service teachers using a structured questionnaire and analyzed using Partial Least Squares Structural Equation Modeling (PLS-SEM). The results indicate that performance expectancy and hedonic motivation are the strongest predictors of behavioral intention. Computer self-efficacy, computer anxiety, and computer playfulness significantly influenced effort expectancy, although effort expectancy did not directly predict behavioral intention. Performance expectancy was significantly predicted by extrinsic motivation, job fit, relative advantage, and outcome expectations. Constructs such as social influence and facilitating conditions showed limited or inverse effects. These findings suggest that internal motivational, cognitive, and emotional factors are more influential than external or institutional factors in shaping the adoption of AI-enabled tools. The study highlights the importance of promoting personal relevance, confidence, and enjoyment in teacher preparation programs to encourage technology integration.

</details>


### [190] [Profiles of AI Dependency: A Latent Class Analysis of Filipino Students' Academic Competencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27349)
*Emerson Q. Fernando,Julius Ceazar G. Tolentino,Maria Anna D. Cruz,Jordan L. Salenga,Vernon Grace M. Maniago,Juvy C. Grume,Erika M. Pineda,Aileen P. De Leon,John Paul P. Miranda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了651名菲律宾高校学生的AI依赖情况，发现中高度依赖现象，尤其在研究和写作领域。潜在类别分析揭示了四类学生群体，其中AI依赖型学习者的学术能力显著较弱。研究强调高校需在推广AI技术的同时保留核心学术技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菲律宾大学生对人工智能的依赖日益增加，引发了对基础学术能力可能下降的担忧，研究旨在考察AI依赖程度及其对学生批判性思维、写作技能、学习独立性、研究能力和学术参与的影响。

Method: 采用横断面研究设计，向菲律宾潘潘加省获得高等教育委员会认证的高校651名学生收集调查数据，并使用潜在类别分析(LCA)识别AI依赖模式。

Result: 学生表现出中等到高度的AI依赖，尤其在研究和写作任务中。LCA识别出四个特征群体：高度参与型独立学习者、选择性AI用户、中度AI用户和AI依赖型学习者。其中AI依赖型学习者学术能力最弱，对AI生成内容的依赖最为显著。

Conclusion: 该研究呼吁制定融合AI素养的教育政策，在利用技术进步的同时保留基础学术能力。高校需平衡技术创新与课程改革，以促进批判性思维和道德使用AI。后续研究应探索AI依赖对学术技能影响的纵向研究及干预策略。

Abstract: The increasing dependency among Filipino college students on artificial intelligence (AI) poses concerns about the potential decline of fundamental academic competencies. This study examines the extent of AI dependency and its perceived effects on students' critical thinking, writing skills, learning independence, research skills, and academic engagement. Using a cross-sectional research design, data was collected from 651 students enrolled in higher education institutions (HEIs) in Pampanga, Philippines accredited by the Commission on Higher Education. The survey data was analyzed using Latent Class Analysis (LCA) to identify AI dependency patterns. Findings indicated that students show moderate to high AI dependency, specifically in research and writing tasks. LCA identified four distinct profiles: highly engaged independent learners, selective AI users, moderate AI users, and AI-dependent learners. Notably, AI-dependent learners demonstrated the weakest academic competencies, with significant dependency on AI-generated outputs. The study highlights the need to foster educational policies that integrate AI literacy while preserving essential academic skills. HEIs must also balance technological advancements with curriculum adaptations to promote critical thinking and ethical use of AI. Future research may explore the longitudinal impacts and intervention strategies to mitigate academic skill erosion caused by AI dependency.

</details>


### [191] [Multi-element Persuasion in Social Media Health Communication: Synergistic and Trade-off Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27350)
*Weifeng Zhang,Jipeng Tan,Mengye Yang,Yong Min*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究基于180万条微博数据，运用聚类分析方法揭示了健康信息在社交媒体上的多元素组合模式，提出了"核心-边缘-环境"理论框架，说明传播效果由多个说服元素的协同与权衡决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单一元素或简单的成对交互作用，对于多元素如何在真实数字环境中协同运作缺乏深入理解，难以解释传播效果的复杂形成机制。

Method: 研究采用系统视角，基于180万条健康相关的微博帖子，运用聚类分析识别反复出现的信息元素组合，并通过统计分析评估不同组合与传播效果之间的关系。

Result: 识别出四种反复出现的信息组合：机构权威型、叙事型、主张型、情境表达型。这些组合以两个核心元素为中心组织，传播效果的增强不仅依赖核心结构，还需要与核心结构一致的边缘元素；边缘元素的复杂程度最优水平随来源影响力变化，表明环境因素调节了信息组合与传播效果的关系。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了"核心-边缘-环境"框架来解释社交媒体上信息组合如何产生具有说服力的传播效果。该框架超越了传统的单一元素或成对交互分析，将传播效果理解为多元素之间的协同与权衡。这一理论贡献将研究从孤立元素扩展到系统组合，为健康传播实践提供了优化信息设计的参考依据。

Abstract: Health messages on social media are typically constructed through combinations of source cues, appeals, frames, and evidence, which jointly shape communication and persuasive effects. However, prior research has largely focused on single elements or simple pairwise interactions, offering insufficient insight into how multiple elements operate together in real-world digital environments. To address this gap, this study adopts a systems perspective to examine multi-element message combinations. Using 1.8 million health-related Weibo posts, we apply clustering analysis to identify recurring combinations and assess their relationships with communication effects. First, four recurring element combinations are identified: Institutional Authority, Narrative, Assertive Appeal, and Contextual Expression. These combinations function as core structures organized around two key elements. Second, stronger communication effects depend not only on core structures but also on peripheral elements aligned with these structures, with combinations of two to four peripheral elements generally showing greater advantages. Third, the optimal level of peripheral complexity varies with source influence, indicating that environmental factors condition the relationship between message combinations and communication effects. These findings show that communication and persuasive effects are shaped by synergies and trade-offs among multiple persuasive elements. Based on this, the study proposes a Core-Periphery-Environment framework to explain how message combinations generate communication effects with persuasive implications on social media. The study extends research from isolated elements to systems combinations and offers practical implications for health communication.

</details>


### [192] [Empire Amplifier: Uncovering and Contesting the Prioritization of Colonial Content on Platforms Through Community-Informed Algorithmic Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27498)
*Nel Escher,Bakyt Yrysov,Ashley McDermott,Daniel Chechelnitsky,Hermela Berehan Benyam,Nikola Banovic*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether YouTube's recommendation algorithm sustains or undermines Indigenous cultural practice, focusing on Kyrgyz communities in Russia. Through a community-informed algorithmic audit, researchers found that YouTube predominantly recommends Russian-language content to Kyrgyz children, regardless of children's expressed preference for Kyrgyz content. This algorithmic bias reinforces colonial language ideologies. The study identifies cross-generational device sharing as a practical mitigation strategy and argues that platform algorithms can actively reimpose oppressive colonial power structures rather than supporting Indigenous cultural revitalization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Indigenous communities express concerns that online platforms—despite claims of amplifying Indigenous voices—are instead eroding Indigenous languages and cultures. This study focuses on Kyrgyz communities' specific fears that YouTube's recommendation algorithm is expanding Russian linguistic influence and encouraging Kyrgyz children to adopt Russian, a colonial language, over their heritage language.

Method: Community-informed algorithmic audit combining ethnographic research with empirical measurement. The researchers constructed an audit methodology in partnership with Kyrgyz communities, then measured YouTube recommendation patterns for child users across Indigenous (Kyrgyz) and non-Indigenous (Russian) language content, testing whether platform algorithms prioritize heritage language content when children express clear preferences.

Result: YouTube primarily recommends non-Kyrgyz (Russian) content to Kyrgyz children, even when children explicitly signal preferences for Kyrgyz content. This algorithmic behavior validates caregiver concerns. Cross-generational device sharing was identified as an effective end-user strategy for reducing Russian-language recommendations.

Conclusion: Platform algorithms can reinforce colonial power structures by amplifying majority/colonial languages over Indigenous languages, even when users explicitly signal preferences for heritage content. Effective mitigation strategies include cross-generational device sharing, but systemic changes require algorithmic accountability aligned with Indigenous values.

Abstract: Though online platforms claim to amplify Indigenous voices, Indigenous communities are worried that these systems are instead eroding their language and culture. We conduct a community-informed algorithmic audit to explore whether online platforms sustain or endanger Indigenous cultural practice. First, we review ethnographic research pertaining to the cultural anxieties of a specific Indigenous community, as Indigenous peoples are not a monolith. We consider concerns from Kyrgyz communities who believe that platforms are expanding Russia's linguistic influence and threatening their language. Next, we construct and conduct an algorithmic audit in conversation with the community. Our audit investigates deep-seated fears among Kyrgyz caregivers that YouTube encourages their children to speak Russian instead of Kyrgyz, their heritage language. We measure how the YouTube recommendation algorithm prioritizes content across Indigenous and non-Indigenous languages for child users. Our results validate caregiver concerns, as we find that YouTube primarily recommends non-Kyrgyz content to Kyrgyz children, even when children signal clear preferences for Kyrgyz content. Thus, platform recommendations reinforce Kyrgyz children's offline uptake of colonial language ideologies. Finally, we evaluate strategies to align platform behavior with Indigenous values. We identify effective end-user practices for reducing the proportion of Russian-language YouTube recommendations, like cross-generational device sharing. Overall, our work uncovers how platforms can amplify colonial influence, rather than revitalizing Indigenous cultural heritage. We encourage researchers to consider how algorithmic systems can reimpose oppressive power structures that decolonial efforts have sought to dismantle.

</details>


### [193] [Towards an Ethical AI Curriculum: A Pan-African, Culturally Contextualized Framework for Primary and Secondary Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27708)
*Abidemi Kuburat Adedeji,Franklin Tchakounte,Sulaiman Oluwasegun Yusuff*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向非洲中小学的泛非、文化情境化且伦理根基牢靠的AI教育整合框架。该框架基于政策文件与学术文献的综合分析，涵盖六项指导原则、四项课程领域、五项伦理能力及分年龄段的学习进阶路径，并将非洲传统伦理思想Ubuntu与全球AI伦理原则相融合。实证验证计划包括德尔菲研究和跨国课堂试点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI现已嵌入全球教育、公民和经济系统。非洲面临双重紧迫性：（1）需为年轻人口（60%以上非洲人年龄在25岁以下）应对AI介导的劳动力市场做准备；（2）必须避免不加批判地引进适用于其他语言、文化和社会政治背景的课程。非洲联盟2024年大陆AI战略和2025年非洲AI宣言已将这些议题提升至非洲大陆议程层面。

Method: 结构化概念综合法。综合了非洲联盟和成员国的政策文件、AI伦理、AI素养、去殖民化教学法以及基于Ubuntu的AI治理等领域的同行评议学术文献。

计划采用混合方法进行实证验证：德尔菲研究、多语言背景（英语、法语、葡萄牙语和阿拉伯语）的教师调查，以及多国课堂试点。

Result: 提出了一个整合六项指导原则、四项课程领域、五项伦理能力和从小学低年级到高中阶段的年龄分层进阶方案的框架；完成非洲大陆与国家政策层面的比较分析；建立全球AI伦理原则与基于Ubuntu的关系伦理学之间的明确映射；设计了涵盖德尔菲研究、多语言教师调查和多国课堂试点的实证验证方案。

Conclusion: 作者认为合乎伦理的AI课程可作为公平、创新和社会正义的变革性工具，并概述了将伦理、韧性和批判性思维嵌入非洲数字未来的研究议程。

主要建议包括：面向政策制定者、教育工作者、民间社会和国际合作伙伴的具体建议。

建议的后续研究计划聚焦于将伦理、韧性和批判性思维置于非洲数字未来的核心位置。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is now embedded in educational, civic, and economic systems worldwide. For African primary and secondary education, this creates a double imperative: to prepare a young population (over sixty per cent of Africans are under twenty-five) for AI-mediated labour markets without uncritically importing curricula designed for other linguistic, cultural, and socio-political contexts. The African Union's Continental AI Strategy (2024) and the 2025 Africa Declaration on AI have elevated these questions to the continental agenda. This paper proposes a Pan-African, culturally contextualised, and ethically grounded framework for integrating AI education into African primary and secondary schools. The paper is a structured conceptual synthesis of continental and national policy documents, peer-reviewed scholarship on AI ethics, AI literacy, decolonial pedagogy, and Ubuntu-grounded AI governance. We contribute: (i) a framework of six guiding principles, four curriculum domains, five ethical competencies, and an age-banded progression from lower primary to upper secondary; (ii) a comparative analysis of continental and national policy contexts; (iii) an explicit mapping between global AI-ethics principles and Ubuntu-informed relational ethics; (iv) a planned empirical validation programme combining a Delphi study, teacher surveys across anglophone, francophone, lusophone, and arabophone contexts, and multi-country classroom piloting; and (v) targeted recommendations for policymakers, educators, civil society, and international partners. We argue that an ethical AI curriculum can serve as a transformative tool for equity, innovation, and social justice, and outline a research agenda to embed ethics, resilience, and critical thinking at the core of Africa's digital future.

</details>


### [194] [To Build or Not to Build? Factors that Lead to Non-Development or Abandonment of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28053)
*Shreya Chappidi,Jatinder Singh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文研究了AI系统开发过程中导致放弃开发的各种因素，通过文献综述和实证案例分析发现，虽然学术界强调伦理风险，但实际上驱动组织放弃AI开发的原因更为多样，包括利益相关者反馈、开发挑战、组织动态和资源约束等，为负责任AI研究指出了新的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前负责任AI研究主要关注已部署AI系统的使用和影响，但对AI系统开发前阶段的决策缺乏关注。开发早期阶段所做的决策决定了最终发布哪些系统，是潜在的、但未被充分探索的干预点。

Method: 研究采用混合方法：(1)对学术文献、民间社会资源和灰色文献（包括新闻报道和行业报告）进行范围审查，通过主题分析开发了六类因素分类法；(2)通过AI事故数据库和从业者调查收集AI系统放弃的真实案例数据，对比系统部署前后驱动的因素差异。

Result: 实证分析揭示了驱动组织放弃AI开发的多样化因素，远超出通常强调的伦理风险。学术文献往往突出伦理风险，但在实际案例中，利益相关者反馈、开发周期挑战、组织动态和资源约束等因素同样重要。

Conclusion: 研究提出了负责任AI研究的改进方向：(1)关注影响组织放弃AI开发的多样化因素，(2)更好地支持组织做出是否继续开发AI系统的决策。这项工作强调了负责任AI研究需要超越伦理风险讨论，扩展到对AI开发全过程决策的理解和支持。

Abstract: Responsible AI research typically focuses on examining the use and impacts of deployed AI systems. Yet, there is currently limited visibility into the pre-deployment decisions to pursue building such systems in the first place. Decisions taken in the earlier stages of development shape which systems are ultimately released, and therefore represent potential, but underexplored, points for intervention. As such, this paper investigates factors influencing AI non-development and abandonment throughout the development lifecycle. Specifically, we first perform a scoping review of academic literature, civil society resources, and grey literature including journalism and industry reports. Through thematic analysis of these sources, we develop a taxonomy of six categories of factors contributing to AI abandonment: ethical concerns, stakeholder feedback, development lifecycle challenges, organizational dynamics, resource constraints, and legal/regulatory concerns. Then, we collect data on real-world case of AI system abandonment via an AI incident database and a practitioner survey to evidence and compare factors that drive abandonment both prior to and following system deployment. While academic responsible AI communities often emphasize ethical risks as reasons to not develop AI, our empirical analysis of these cases demonstrates the diverse, and often non-ethics-related, levers that motivate organizations to abandon AI development. Synthesizing evidence from our taxonomy and related case study analyses, we identify gaps and opportunities in current responsible AI research to (1) engage with the diverse range of levers that influence organizations to abandon AI development, and (2) better support appropriate (dis)engagement with AI system development.

</details>


### [195] [I hope we don't do to trust what advertising has done to love](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28113)
*Jade Alglave*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This is a visionary/position paper that warns against trivializing "trust" in AI contexts (analogous to how advertising trivialized "love"), and proposes "trust pillars" as an actionable, measurable framework. It sees opportunity in agentic systems' explicit interfaces serving as "trust vectors."


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Concern that trust in AI (especially agentic systems) may be trivialized similar to how advertising trivialized the word "love" (e.g., "do you love your oven?"). There is a need to move beyond superficial uses of "trust" to discussions that are actionable and measurable.

Method: Conceptual framework proposal; introduces "trust pillars" as actionable and measurable dimensions for trust in AI systems.

Result: Presents "trust pillars" as a communal framework spanning computing, civil society, and beyond for discussing trust in AI.

Conclusion: Proposes that agentic AI systems could leverage their explicit interfaces as "trust vectors" - potentially transforming the challenge of trust into an opportunity.

Abstract: Advertising uses love to sell stuff, like nylons. It also uses the word "love" in trivialising ways -- do you "love" your oven? When I hear about trust in the context of AI, especially agentic, I hope we don't do to trust what advertising has done to love.
  But what is trust? Can we discuss it in actionable and measurable ways in the context of AI? Thus I suggest a number of "trust pillars", hoping to start a communal conversation, across computing and beyond, to civil society. I also suggest that agentic systems may be a blessing in disguise, as we may be able to turn their explicit interfaces into "trust vectors".

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Compositional Meta-Learning for Mitigating Task Heterogeneity in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26999)
*Beomchul Park,Minsu Koh,Heejo Kong,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAM-PINN is a modular meta-learning framework for physics-informed neural networks that addresses negative transfer in parameterized PDE families by learning task-specific module routing. By combining PDE parameters with learning-affinity metrics to form task representations and decomposing the model into cluster-specialized subnetworks with a shared meta network, it achieves near two orders of magnitude improvement in accuracy and 10× efficiency gain over conventional approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training individual Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) for each distinct task in parameterized PDE families is computationally prohibitive. While meta-learning offers a potential solution for reducing retraining costs, existing methods suffer from two key limitations: (1) reliance on a single global initialization across tasks, and (2) vulnerability to negative transfer, especially under feature-scarce coordinate inputs and limited training-task availability.

Method: LAM-PINN proposes a compositional framework with three key innovations: (1) A task representation mechanism combining PDE parameters with learning-affinity metrics from brief transfer sessions, enabling task clustering even with coordinate-only inputs; (2) Model decomposition into cluster-specialized subnetworks and a shared meta network; (3) Learned routing weights enabling selective module reuse rather than relying on a single global initialization to mitigate negative transfer.

Result: Evaluated across three PDE benchmarks, LAM-PINN achieves an average 19.7-fold reduction in mean squared error (MSE) on unseen tasks while requiring only 10% of the training iterations compared to conventional PINNs. The method successfully handles challenging scenarios with coordinate-only inputs where traditional approaches struggle.

Conclusion: LAM-PINN provides an effective solution for adapting physics-informed neural networks to parameterized PDE families, achieving substantial improvements in both accuracy (19.7× MSE reduction) and efficiency (10% of training iterations). The approach is particularly valuable for resource-constrained engineering applications requiring rapid adaptation to unseen but bounded configurations within parameterized PDE families.

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) approximate solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into the loss function. In parameterized PDE families, variations in coefficients or boundary/initial conditions define distinct tasks. This makes training individual PINNs for each task computationally prohibitive, while cross-task transfer can be sensitive to task heterogeneity. While meta-learning can reduce retraining cost, existing methods often rely on a single global initialization and may suffer from negative transfer, particularly under feature-scarce coordinate inputs and limited training-task availability. We propose the Learning-Affinity Adaptive Modular Physics-Informed Neural Network (LAM-PINN), a compositional framework that leverages task-specific learning dynamics. LAM-PINN combines PDE parameters with learning-affinity metrics from brief transfer sessions to construct a task representation and cluster tasks even with coordinate-only inputs. It decomposes the model into cluster-specialized subnetworks and a shared meta network, and learns routing weights to selectively reuse modules instead of relying on a single global initialization. Across three PDE benchmarks, LAM-PINN achieves an average 19.7-fold reduction in mean squared error (MSE) on unseen tasks using only 10% of the training iterations required by conventional PINNs. These results indicate its effectiveness for generalization to unseen configurations within bounded design spaces of parameterized PDE families in resource-constrained engineering settings.

</details>


### [197] [When Your LLM Reaches End-of-Life: A Framework for Confident Model Migration in Production Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27082)
*Emma Casey,David Roberts,David Sim,Ian Beaver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯统计的框架，用于在有限人工评估数据的条件下校准自动化指标与人类判断，从而实现LLM生产系统的可靠模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当底层LLM到达生命周期终点或需要替换时，如何在生产环境中安全、高效地完成模型迁移，同时保证服务质量。

Method: 采用贝叶斯统计方法校准自动化评估指标与人工判断之间的差异，即使在有限的手动评估数据下也能实现可靠的模型比较。

Result: 成功在6个全球区域、每月530万交互量的商业问答系统中验证了该方法，通过对正确性、拒绝行为和风格遵循度的评估，识别出合适的替代模型。

Conclusion: 该框架为企业在LLM生态快速演进背景下提供了一种原则化、可复现的模型迁移方法，有效平衡了质量保证与评估效率，适用于跨多模型、地区和用例的AI产品组合管理。

Abstract: We present a framework for migrating production Large Language Model (LLM) based systems when the underlying model reaches end-of-life or requires replacement. The key contribution is a Bayesian statistical approach that calibrates automated evaluation metrics against human judgments, enabling confident model comparison even with limited manual evaluation data. We demonstrate this framework on a commercial question-answering system serving 5.3M monthly interactions across six global regions; evaluating correctness, refusal behavior, and stylistic adherence to successfully identify suitable replacement models. The framework is broadly applicable to any enterprise deploying LLM-based products, providing a principled, reproducible methodology for model migration that balances quality assurance with evaluation efficiency. This is a capability increasingly essential as the LLM ecosystem continues to evolve rapidly and organizations manage portfolios of AI-powered services across multiple models, regions, and use cases.

</details>


### [198] [When Roles Fail: Epistemic Constraints on Advocate Role Fidelity in LLM-Based Political Statement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27228)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次系统验证多智能体LLM管道中模型的角色维持能力。发现模型选择显著影响角色保真度(Mistral优于Claude)，识别认知角色覆盖(ERO)机制为统一失败原因，揭示不同模型展现定性相异的失败模式，并证明事实核查提供者选择存在模型特异性影响。未经角色保真度测量的系统验证可能系统性误代表其设计的认知多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM管道在民主话语分析中广泛采用对抗性角色评估，但系统假设模型会可靠地维持分配的角色，此前缺乏对该假设的实证验证。

Method: 开发了无需依赖表面词汇的认知立场分类器，测量60条政治陈述（30英/30德）中的角色保真度，采用四个指标：角色漂移指数(RDI)、期望漂移距离(EDD)、方向漂移指数(DDI)、基于熵的角色稳定性(ERS)。比较Mistral Large与Claude Sonnet的表现，并测试不同事实核查提供者的影响。

Result: Mistral Large在角色保真度上比Claude Sonnet高28个百分点(67% vs 39%)，失败模式存在质性差异：Mistral为角色放弃无极性反转，Claude为主动转换到对立立场。识别出认知角色覆盖(ERO)机制，包含认知地板效应和角色-先验冲突两种失败模式。事实核查提供者选择存在模型特异性：Perplexity显著降低Claude在德语陈述上的角色保真度(Δ=-15pp, p=0.007)，但对Mistral无影响。角色保真度具有语言鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Multi-agent LLM validation必须纳入角色保真度测量；模型选择显著影响系统可靠性；不同模型展现不同失败模式；事实核查提供者选择对不同模型有差异化影响。

Abstract: Democratic discourse analysis systems increasingly rely on multi-agent LLM pipelines in which distinct evaluator models are assigned adversarial roles to generate structured, multi-perspective assessments of political statements. A core assumption is that models will reliably maintain their assigned roles. This paper provides the first systematic empirical test of that assumption using the TRUST pipeline. We develop an epistemic stance classifier that identifies advocate roles from reasoning text without relying on surface vocabulary, and measure role fidelity across 60 political statements (30 English, 30 German) using four metrics: Role Drift Index (RDI), Expected Drift Distance (EDD), Directional Drift Index (DDI), and Entropy-based Role Stability (ERS). We identify two failure modes - the Epistemic Floor Effect (fact-check results create an absolute lower bound below which the legitimizing role cannot be maintained) and Role-Prior Conflict (training-time knowledge overrides role instructions for factually unambiguous statements) - as manifestations of a single mechanism: Epistemic Role Override (ERO). Model choice significantly affects role fidelity: Mistral Large outperforms Claude Sonnet by 28pp (67% vs. 39%) and exhibits a qualitatively different failure mode - role abandonment without polarity reversal - compared to Claude's active switch to the opposing stance. Role fidelity is language-robust. Fact-check provider choice is not universally neutral: Perplexity significantly reduces Claude's role fidelity on German statements (Delta = -15pp, p = 0.007) while leaving Mistral unaffected. These findings have direct implications for multi-agent LLM validation: a system validated without role fidelity measurement may systematically misrepresent the epistemic diversity it was designed to provide.

</details>


### [199] [End-to-end autonomous scientific discovery on a real optical platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27092)
*Shuxing Yang,Fujia Chen,Rui Zhao,Junyao Wu,Yize Wang,Haiyao Luo,Ning Han,Qiaolu Chen,Yuze Hu,Wenhao Li,Mingzhu Li,Hongsheng Chen,Yihao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Qiushi Discovery Engine，一个基于LLM的智能体系统，用于在真实光学平台上进行端到端自主科学发现。该系统通过结合非线性研究阶段、Meta-Trace记忆和双层架构，在涉及数千次LLM介导推理的长时间研究中保持稳定轨迹。系统成功重现了已发表的传输矩阵实验，并首次观察到相干阶结构。更重要的是，在开放性研究中（1.459亿tokens、3242次LLM调用），系统自主发现并实验验证了光学双线性相互作用——一种结构上类似于Transformer注意力核心操作的物理机制，为高速低能耗光学计算硬件提供了新路径。这是首个AI智能体自主发现并验证此前未报告物理机制的里程碑式演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究传统上由人类主导，LLM-based智能体虽已开始超越辅助预定义研究工作流程，但在真实物理系统中实现端到端自主发现并产生有实验证据支持的 nontrivial 结果尚未有人实现。

Method: Qiushi Engine结合了非线性研究阶段、Meta-Trace记忆和双层架构，在涉及数千次LLM介导的推理、测量和修订操作的长时间研究中保持适应性和稳定的研究轨迹。在开放性研究中，系统使用了1.459亿tokens、3242次LLM调用、1242次工具调用、163条研究笔记和44个脚本。

Result: 系统在真实光学平台上自主完成了：（1）重现已发表的传输矩阵实验；（2）将抽象的相干阶理论转化为实验可观测量（首次观察到此类相干阶结构）；（3）提出并实验验证了光学双线性相互作用——一种在结构上类似于Transformer注意力机制核心操作的物理机制。

Conclusion: 这是首个AI智能体系统自主识别并实验验证非平凡、此前未报告的物理机制的演示，标志着一个研究级自主智能体的重要里程碑。该AI发现的机制为高速、低能耗的光学硬件成对计算提供了一条潜在路径。

Abstract: Scientific research has long been human-led, driving new knowledge and transformative technologies through the continual revision of questions, methods and claims as evidence accumulates. Although large language model (LLM)-based agents are beginning to move beyond assisting predefined research workflows, none has yet demonstrated end-to-end autonomous discovery in a real physical system that produces a nontrivial result supported by experimental evidence. Here we introduce Qiushi Discovery Engine, an LLM-based agentic system for end-to-end autonomous scientific discovery on a real optical platform. Qiushi Engine combines nonlinear research phases, Meta-Trace memory and a dual-layer architecture to maintain adaptive and stable research trajectories across long-horizon investigations involving thousands of LLM-mediated reasoning, measurement and revision actions. It autonomously reproduces a published transmission-matrix experiment on a non-original platform and converts an abstract coherence-order theory into experimental observables, providing, to our knowledge, the first observation of this class of coherence-order structure. More importantly, in an open-ended study involving 145.9 million tokens, 3,242 LLM calls, 1,242 tool calls, 163 research notes and 44 scripts, Qiushi Engine proposes and experimentally validates optical bilinear interaction, a physical mechanism structurally analogous to a core operation in Transformer attention. This AI-discovered mechanism suggests a route towards high-speed, energy-efficient optical hardware for pairwise computation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an AI agentic system autonomously identifying and experimentally validating a nontrivial, previously unreported physical mechanism, marking a milestone for research-level autonomous agents.

</details>


### [200] [Reinforced Agent: Inference-Time Feedback for Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27233)
*Anh Ta,Junjie Zhu,Shahin Shayandeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在执行循环内嵌入专门的reviewer agent进行事前评估，将"事后恢复"范式转变为"主动评估与错误缓解"。为量化多智能体系统中reviewer的收益-风险权衡，引入了Helpfulness-Harmfulness指标，并证明可通过模型选择和prompt优化独立改进reviewer而无需重训练主智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM轨迹评估本质上是事后进行的，与主动执行循环脱节。这种方式只能通过prompt-tuning或重训练来修复错误，无法实时纠正智能体。评估与执行之间存在gap需要弥合。

Method: 1. 架构：将评估从事后移到执行循环内，引入专门的reviewer agent在工具调用执行前进行评估
2. 评估指标：Helpfulness-Harmfulness指标——Helpfulness测量反馈纠正的错误比例，Harmfulness测量反馈降级的正确响应比例
3. 优化方法：GEPA自动化prompt优化

Result: 在BFCL(单轮)和Tau2-Bench(多轮有状态场景)上分别达到+5.5%的无关检测率和+7.1%的多轮任务提升。o3-mini达到3:1的收益风险比(GPT-4o为2.1:1)。GEPA自动化prompt优化额外提升1.5-2.8%。

Conclusion: Reviewer agent可以实现独立优化（模型选择+prompt优化），无需重新训练主执行智能体。Helpfulness-Harmfulness指标为评估和改进reviewer提供了可量化的依据，证明了执行-审查分离架构的可行性。

The reviewer can be systematically improved through model selection and prompt optimization without retraining the base agent.

Abstract: Tool-calling agents are evaluated on tool selection, parameter accuracy, and scope recognition, yet LLM trajectory assessments remain inherently post-hoc. Disconnected from the active execution loop, such assessments identify errors that are usually addressed through prompt-tuning or retraining, and fundamentally cannot course-correct the agent in real time. To close this gap, we move evaluation into the execution loop at inference time: a specialized reviewer agent evaluates provisional tool calls prior to execution, shifting the paradigm from post-hoc recovery to proactive evaluation and error mitigation.
  In practice, this architecture establishes a clear separation of concerns between the primary execution agent and a secondary review agent. As with any multi-agent system, the reviewer can introduce new errors while correcting others, yet no prior work to our knowledge has systematically measured this tradeoff. To quantify this tradeoff, we introduce Helpfulness-Harmfulness metrics: helpfulness measures the percentage of base agent errors that feedback corrects; harmfulness measures the percentage of correct responses that feedback degrades. These metrics directly inform reviewer design by revealing whether a given model or prompt provides net positive value.
  We evaluate our approach on BFCL (single-turn) and Tau2-Bench (multi-turn stateful scenarios), achieving +5.5% on irrelevance detection and +7.1% on multi-turn tasks. Our metrics reveal that reviewer model choice is critical: the reasoning model o3-mini achieves a 3:1 benefit-to-risk ratio versus 2.1:1 for GPT-4o. Automated prompt optimization via GEPA provides an additional +1.5-2.8%. Together, these results demonstrate a core advantage of separating execution and review: the reviewer can be systematically improved through model selection and prompt optimization, without retraining the base agent.

</details>


### [201] [Think it, Run it: Autonomous ML pipeline generation via self-healing multi-agent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27096)
*Adela Bara,Gabriela Dobrita,Simona-Vasilica Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个统一的多智能体架构，通过五个智能体协作实现从数据集和自然语言目标自动生成端到端机器学习流程，集成了代码接地RAG、可解释推荐、自愈机制和自适应学习等关键技术，在150个任务上达到84.7%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习（ML）流程开发面临效率低、稳健性差和可解释性不足的挑战，需要从数据集和自然语言目标自动生成端到端ML流程的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个五智能体系统，包括分析、意图解析、微服务推荐、有向无环图（DAG）构建和执行五个模块。集成代码接地检索增强生成（RAG）用于微服务理解，结合多标准的可解释混合推荐器，以及基于大语言模型（LLM）的错误解释自愈机制和从执行历史中自适应学习的能力。

Result: 在150个ML任务上的评估表明，系统达到了84.7%的端到端流程成功率，优于基线方法。通过自愈机制提高了稳健性，相比手动构建显著减少了工作流开发时间。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种代码接地RAG、可解释推荐、自愈执行和自适应学习的创新性集成架构，证明紧密耦合的智能组件可以优于孤立解决方案。该系统显著降低了工作流开发时间，同时提高了稳健性和可解释性。

Abstract: The purpose of our paper is to develop a unified multi-agent architecture that automates end-to-end machine learning (ML) pipeline generation from datasets and natural-language (NL) goals, improving efficiency, robustness and explainability. A five-agent system is proposed to handle profiling, intent parsing, microservice recommendation, Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) construction and execution. It integrates code-grounded Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for microservice understanding, an explainable hybrid recommender combining multiple criteria, a self-healing mechanism using Large Language Model (LLM)-based error interpretation and adaptive learning from execution history. The approach is evaluated on 150 ML tasks across diverse scenarios. The system achieves an 84.7% end-to-end pipeline success rate, outperforming baseline methods. It demonstrates improved robustness through self-healing and reduces workflow development time compared to manual construction. The study introduces a novel integration of code-grounded RAG, explainable recommendation, self-healing execution and adaptive learning within a single architecture, showing that tightly coupled intelligent components can outperform isolated solutions.

</details>


### [202] [Unsupervised Electrofacies Classification and Porosity Characterization in the Offshore Keta Basin Using Wireline Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27126)
*Hamdiya Adams,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Daniel Kwadwo Asiedu,Bruce Kofi Banoeng-Yakubo,Marcellin Atemkeng,Thomas Armah,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis,Ezekiel Nii Noye Nortey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对加纳近海Keta盆地核心数据稀缺的挑战，提出了一种基于六条标准测井曲线的无监督机器学习工作流程。通过K-means聚类分析约11,195个样本，识别出四个电相类别（轮廓系数约0.50），呈现从泥岩主导到砂岩主导的地质连续性。该方法为难开发区的前期地层评价提供了可重复且稳健的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In frontier offshore basins like the Keta Basin, Ghana, core data are scarce, necessitating alternative approaches for electrofacies analysis and subsurface characterisation without relying on limited core measurements.

Method: K-means clustering was applied in multivariate log space using six standard wireline logs from Well C over approximately 11,195 samples. Clustering structure was evaluated using inertia and silhouette diagnostics.

Result: Four clusters were identified with an average silhouette coefficient of approximately 0.50, indicating moderate but meaningful separation. The electrofacies exhibit systematic, depth-continuous patterns associated with variations in clay content, porosity, and rock framework properties, forming a geological continuum from shale-dominated to cleaner sandstone-dominated units.

Conclusion: The proposed log-only, unsupervised clustering workflow provides a robust and reproducible framework for subsurface characterisation, offering a practical tool for early-stage formation evaluation in frontier offshore basins and a foundation for future integrated studies.

Abstract: This study presents an unsupervised machine learning workflow for electrofacies analysis in the offshore Keta Basin, Ghana, where core data are scarce. Six standard wireline logs from Well~C were analysed over a depth interval comprising approximately $11{,}195$ samples. K-means clustering was applied in multivariate log space, with the clustering structure evaluated using inertia and silhouette diagnostics. Four clusters were identified, supported by an average silhouette coefficient of approximately $0.50$, indicating moderate but meaningful separation. The resulting electrofacies exhibit systematic, depth-continuous patterns associated with variations in clay content, porosity, and rock framework properties, forming a geological continuum from shale-dominated to cleaner sandstone-dominated units. The results demonstrate that log-only, unsupervised clustering supported by quantitative metrics provides a robust and reproducible framework for subsurface characterisation. The proposed workflow offers a practical tool for early-stage formation evaluation in frontier offshore basins and a foundation for future integrated studies.

</details>


### [203] [Autonomous Traffic Signal Optimization Using Digital Twin and Agentic AI for Real-Time Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27753)
*Salman Jan,Toqeer Ali Syed,Shahid Kamal,Qamar Wali,Ali Akarma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This article outlines a new framework of traffic light optimization through a digital twin of the transport infrastructure, managed by agentic AI to ensure real-time autonomous decisions. The framework relies on physical sensors and edge computing to measure real-time traffic information and simulate traffic flow in a constantly updated digital twin. The traffic light is automatically controlled through the digital twin according to traffic congestion, travel delay and traffic patterns. This approach is implemented as a three-layer system: perception, conceptualization and action. The perception layer receives data on physical systems; the conceptualization layer uses LangChain to process the data; and the action layer links to the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and traffic management APIs to implement optimised traffic signal control algorithms. The results show that the framework minimizes waiting time at traffic lights and positively affects the effectiveness of the entire traffic flow, which is better than the fixed-time and reinforcement learning-based baselines.

</details>


### [204] [TRUST: A Framework for Decentralized AI Service v.0.1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27132)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Zhen Tan,Mohan Zhang,Pingzhi Li,Zhuo Zhang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRUST是一个用于可信AI的去中心化审计框架，通过层级DAG分解推理、DAAN协议实现因果归因、多层共识机制保证正确性，在30%恶意节点下仍可工作，已在多个LLM和基准测试中验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前中心化验证方法存在四大局限：稳健性差（单点故障易受攻击和偏见影响）、可扩展性低（推理复杂度造成瓶颈）、不透明（隐藏审计侵蚀信任）、隐私泄露（推理痕迹暴露模型资产）。

Method: 1. HDAGs（层级DAG）：将思维链推理分解为五层抽象，支持并行分布式审计；2. DAAN协议：将多智能体交互映射为因果交互图，实现确定性根因归因；3. 多层共识机制：计算检查器、LLM评估器、人类专家通过权益加权投票决策。

Result: 准确率72.4%（比基线高4-18%），20%腐败下仍保持弹性；根因归因70%（对比标准方法54-63%），token节省60%；人类研究验证F1=0.89，Brier=0.074。

Conclusion: TRUST框架实现了安全、可问责的推理系统部署，在30%恶意节点参与下仍保证正确性，四个应用场景（A1-A4）验证了去中心化AI审计的可行性，为高风险领域的可靠AI验证提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) and Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) in high-stakes domains demand reliable verification, yet centralized approaches suffer four limitations: (1) Robustness, with single points of failure vulnerable to attacks and bias; (2) Scalability, as reasoning complexity creates bottlenecks; (3) Opacity, as hidden auditing erodes trust; and (4) Privacy, as exposed reasoning traces risk model theft. We introduce TRUST (Transparent, Robust, and Unified Services for Trustworthy AI), a decentralized framework with three innovations: (i) Hierarchical Directed Acyclic Graphs (HDAGs) that decompose Chain-of-Thought reasoning into five abstraction levels for parallel distributed auditing; (ii) the DAAN protocol, which projects multi-agent interactions into Causal Interaction Graphs (CIGs) for deterministic root-cause attribution; and (iii) a multi-tier consensus mechanism among computational checkers, LLM evaluators, and human experts with stake-weighted voting that guarantees correctness under 30% adversarial participation. We prove a Safety-Profitability Theorem ensuring honest auditors profit while malicious actors incur losses. All decisions are recorded on-chain, while privacy-by-design segmentation prevents reconstruction of proprietary logic. Across multiple LLMs and benchmarks, TRUST attains 72.4% accuracy (4-18% above baselines) and remains resilient against 20% corruption. DAAN reaches 70% root-cause attribution (vs. 54-63% for standard methods) with 60% token savings. Human studies validate the design (F1 = 0.89, Brier = 0.074). The framework supports (A1) decentralized auditing, (A2) tamper-proof leaderboards, (A3) trustless data annotation, and (A4) governed autonomous agents, pioneering decentralized AI auditing for safe, accountable deployment of reasoning-capable systems.

</details>


### [205] [Language Models Refine Mechanical Linkage Designs Through Symbolic Reflection and Modular Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27962)
*João Pedro Gandarela,Thiago Rios,Stefan Menzel,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语言模型驱动的机械连杆设计框架，通过符号化抽象协调离散拓扑探索与连续参数优化，在多款开源大模型上验证可显著提升设计质量并实现可解释的机械推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械连杆设计涉及组合拓扑选择的离散决策与连续参数拟合的数值优化，传统方法难以有效协调这两类异构任务。

Method: 采用模块化架构：语言模型智能体探索离散拓扑结构，数值优化器拟合连续参数，符号提升算子将仿真轨迹翻译为定性描述符、运动标签、时序谓词和结构诊断信息，支撑跨迭代设计周期的模型解读。

Result: 在六个工程相关运动目标及三款开源模型上验证，模块化架构相比单体基线将几何误差降低68%，结构有效性提升134%；78.6%的迭代精化轨迹呈现可测量改进；系统正确诊断过约束（56.3%）和欠约束（35.6%）故障模式并提出有依据的修正方案。

Conclusion: 语言模型可在无需微调的情况下习得可解释的机械推理策略，且模型在三个系列中均展现出一致性能，证明了原则性符号抽象可弥合生成式AI与工程设计所需数值精度之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Designing mechanical linkages involves combinatorial topology selection and continuous parameter fitting. We show that language models can systematically improve linkage designs through symbolic representations. Language model agents explore discrete topologies while numerical optimisers fit continuous parameters. A symbolic lifting operator translates simulator trajectories into qualitative descriptors, motion labels, temporal predicates, and structural diagnostics that models interpret across iterative design cycles. Across six engineering-relevant motion targets and three open-source models (Llama 3.3 70B, Qwen3 4B, Qwen3 MoE 30B-A3B), the modular architecture reduces geometric error by up to 68% and improves structural validity by up to 134% over monolithic baselines. Critically, 78.6% of iterative refinement trajectories show measurable improvement, with the system correctly diagnosing overconstraint (56.3%) and underconstraint (35.6%) failure modes and proposing grounded corrections. Models across all three families acquire interpretable mechanical reasoning strategies without fine-tuning, demonstrating that principled symbolic abstraction bridges generative AI and the numerical precision required for engineering design.

</details>


### [206] [Optimal Stop-Loss and Take-Profit Parameterization for Autonomous Trading Agent Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27150)
*Nathan Li,Aikins Laryea,Yigit Ihlamur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对自主加密货币交易系统的出场策略进行系统性回测分析，发现精心设计的出场规则（严格止损、早收、移动止损）能显著提升风险调整后收益。研究还发现了评估方法论的关键问题：简单的时间序列划分会因特殊市场事件严重扭曲结果，改用随机化划分可缓解但非完美，并讨论了其固有缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主加密货币交易系统普遍重入场轻出场，出场规则多为固定设定且缺乏系统性测试，存在显著的策略优化空间。

Method: 对900多笔历史交易进行回放，针对每笔交易测试多种替代退出策略（止损、止盈、移动止损的不同参数设置），并将结果与现有生产配置进行对比；为减少特殊市场事件对结果的扭曲，在随机化数据上运行主要对比，并明确承认随机化方法的局限性。

Result: 出场设计确实重要：更强的出场配置能提升风险调整后表现，整体偏好更严格的损失限制、更早的利润锁定和更近的移动止损保护。

Conclusion: 更强壮的退出配置（更严格的止损、更早止盈、更近的移动止损）能改善风险调整后收益；文章提供了一个更严谨透明的退出策略调优框架，但随机化数据划分会引入偏差，需要谨慎解读；研究还揭示了时间切片划分可能因特殊市场事件导致结果失真，突显了评估设计中控制外部冲击的重要性。

Abstract: Autonomous crypto trading systems often spend most of their design effort on finding entries, while exits are left to fixed rules that are rarely tested in a systematic way. This paper examines whether better stop-loss and take-profit settings can improve the performance of an autonomous trading agent swarm. Using more than 900 historical trades, we replay each trade under many alternative exit policies and compare results against the existing production setup. The study finds that exit design matters meaningfully: stronger configurations improve risk-adjusted performance and generally favor tighter loss limits, earlier profit capture, and closer trailing protection. The paper also discusses a key evaluation challenge: a purely chronological split was initially used, but the newest trades fell into an unusual war-driven market period that sharply distorted test results. To reduce the influence of that single episode, the main comparison was run on randomized data, with the drawbacks of doing so acknowledged explicitly. Overall, the paper presents a practical framework for tuning exit logic in a more disciplined and transparent way.

</details>


### [207] [Step-level Optimization for Efficient Computer-use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27151)
*Jinbiao Wei,Kangqi Ni,Yilun Zhao,Guo Gan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种事件驱动的步级级联框架，通过 Stuck Monitor 和 Milestone Monitor 两个轻量级监控器检测高风险时刻，仅在必要时升级到更强模型，解决计算机使用智能体计算资源分配不均的问题。该框架可叠加于现有智能体，无需修改架构或重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机使用智能体在几乎每个交互步骤都调用大型多模态模型，导致成本高昂且速度缓慢。然而这种均匀计算分配本质上是低效的，因为长时域 GUI 任务具有高度异质性——许多步骤是常规的可以由更小更便宜的策略处理，而错误往往集中在相对较少的高风险时刻。

Method: 提出了一种事件驱动的步级级联框架，运行小型策略作为默认方案，仅当轻量级学习监控器检测到高风险时才升级到更强的模型。框架结合两个互补信号：Stuck Monitor（从近期推理-行动历史检测进度退化并触发恢复）和 Milestone Monitor（识别语义上有意义的检查点，在此处进行稀疏验证以捕获漂移）。

Result: 通过将始终运行的 frontier 模型推理转变为自适应、按需的计算分配，提高计算机使用智能体的效率。

Conclusion: 该框架是模块化且面向部署的，可以叠加在现有计算机使用智能体之上，无需改变底层智能体架构或重新训练大模型，将始终运行的 frontier 模型推理转变为交互过程中的自适应按需计算分配。

Abstract: Computer-use agents provide a promising path toward general software automation because they can interact directly with arbitrary graphical user interfaces instead of relying on brittle, application-specific integrations. Despite recent advances in benchmark performance, strong computer-use agents remain expensive and slow in practice, since most systems invoke large multimodal models at nearly every interaction step. We argue that this uniform allocation of compute is fundamentally inefficient for long-horizon GUI tasks. Such trajectories are highly heterogeneous: many steps are routine and can be handled reliably by smaller, cheaper policies, while errors tend to concentrate at a relatively small number of high-risk moments. Across computer-use benchmarks, these failures repeatedly take two forms: progress stalls, where the agent loops, repeats ineffective actions, or fails to make meaningful progress, and silent semantic drift, where the agent continues taking locally plausible actions after already deviating from the user's true goal. To address this inefficiency, we propose an event-driven, step-level cascade for computer-use agents that runs a small policy by default and escalates to a stronger model only when lightweight learned monitors detect elevated risk. Our framework combines two complementary signals: a Stuck Monitor that detects degraded progress from recent reasoning-action history and triggers recovery, and a Milestone Monitor that identifies semantically meaningful checkpoints where sparse verification is most informative for catching drift. This design turns always-on frontier-model inference into adaptive, on-demand compute allocation over the course of an evolving interaction. The framework is modular and deployment-oriented: it can be layered on top of existing computer-use agents without changing the underlying agent architecture or retraining the large model.

</details>


### [208] [Interval Orders, Biorders and Credibility-limited Belief Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27156)
*Richard Booth,Ivan Varzinczak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了信念修正中两种比全前序更一般的偏好结构——区间序和双向序，提供了相应修正算子的公理化刻画，并引入了一类与汉森等人的有限可信修正相关的新型非优先修正算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信念修正理论通常使用全前序作为偏好结构，但存在其他更一般的序结构尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用区间序和双向序（非线性偏好结构）对信念修正进行建模，并通过公理化方法刻画所得到的修正算子族。

Result: 对区间序和双向序修正算子进行了公理化刻画；发现双向序修正虽满足成功公理但可能产生不一致输出；通过修改定义排除"不可信"输入，可得到满足一致性公理但不满足成功公理的新型有限优先级修正算子族。

Conclusion: Biorder-based approach is suitable for scenarios where agents might initially reject but later accept new information upon receiving additional explanation.

Abstract: Rational belief revision is commonly viewed as being based on a preference order between possible worlds, with the resulting new belief set being those sentences true in all the most preferred models of the incoming new information. Usually, such a preference order is taken to be a total preorder. Nevertheless, there are other, more general classes of ordering that can also be employed. In this paper, we explore two such classes that have been studied within the theory of rational choice but have seen limited or no application in belief revision. We begin with interval orders, introduced by Fishburn in the '80s, which associate with each possible world a nonnegative `interval' of plausibility. We then move on to biorders, studied by Aleskerov, Bouyssou, and Monjardet, which generalise interval orders by allowing the intervals to have negative lengths, a feature that can be used to capture a notion of dissonance or instability. We provide axiomatic characterisations of these two resulting families of belief revision operators, as well as of two further families of interest that lie between interval orders and biorders. We show that while biorder-based revisions satisfy the Success postulate, they do not always yield consistent outputs. By modifying their definition to discard inputs that lead to inconsistency as `incredible', we derive new families of so-called non-prioritised revision that satisfy the Consistency postulate, but not the Success one. These families are linked to credibility-limited revision operators of Hansson et al., but for which the set of credible sentences does not satisfy the single-sentence closure condition. We argue that the biorder-based approach is well-suited for scenarios where an agent might initially reject new information, but may accept it when presented with additional explanation.

</details>


### [209] [Evaluating TabPFN for Mild Cognitive Impairment to Alzheimer's Disease Conversion in Data Limited Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27195)
*Brad Ye,Bulent Soykan,Gulsah Hancerliogullari Koksalmis,Hsin-Hsiung Huang,Laura J. Brattain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将TabPFN（一种表格数据预训练基础模型）与传统机器学习方法对比，用于预测MCI向AD的3年转换。实验基于ADNI的TADPOLE数据集，利用多模态生物标志物特征进行评估。结果表明TabPFN在预测性能上优于LightGBM等传统方法，尤其在低数据场景下展现出显著优势，证明了基础模型在数据受限的疾病预测任务中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于纵向数据有限，MCI到AD的转换预测模型难以建立，需要探索更有效的方法来提高预测性能。

Method: 在TADPOLE数据集（源自ADNI）上，使用多模态生物标志物特征（人口统计学、APOE4、MRI体积、脑脊液标记物、PET成像），对比测试TabPFN与XGBoost、Random Forest、LightGBM、Logistic Regression等传统机器学习方法在不同训练样本量（N=50至1000）下的表现。

Result: TabPFN达到最高性能（AUC=0.892），优于LightGBM（AUC=0.860）；在小样本训练场景下（N=50），TabPFN仍保持较强的AUC，而传统机器学习方法表现显著下降。

Conclusion: TabPFN在低数据场景下表现优异，在有限标注数据的疾病预测任务中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of conversion from Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) to Alzheimers Diseases (AD) is essential for early intervention, however, developing reliable conversion predictive models is difficult to develop due to limited longitudinal data availability We evaluate TabPFN (Tabular Pre-Trained Foundation Network) against traditional machine learning methods for predicting 3 year MCI to AD conversion using the TADPOLE dataset derived from ADNI. Using multimodal biomarker features extracted from demographics, APOE4, MRI volumes, CSF markers, and PET imaging, we conducted an experimental comparison across varying training set sizes (N=50 to 1000) and models including XGBoost, Random Forest, LightGBM, and Logistic Regression. TabPFN achieved one the highest performance (AUC=0.892), outperforming LightGBM (AUC=0.860) and demonstrating advantages in low data settings. At N=50 training samples, TabPFN maintained strong AUC while the traditional machine learning models struggles at small training samples. These findings demonstrate that foundation models are promising for disease prediction in data limited scenarios, such as Alzheimers diseases.

</details>


### [210] [Toward Personalized Digital Twins for Cognitive Decline Assessment: A Multimodal, Uncertainty-Aware Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27217)
*Bulent Soykan,Gulsah Hancerliogullari Koksalmis,Hsin-Hsiung Huang,Laura J. Brattain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PCD-DT框架——一个用于个性化建模神经退行性疾病轨迹的多模态不确定性感知数字孪生系统。该框架整合状态空间模型、多模态融合和不确定性验证三大技术，在TADPOLE数据上验证了可行性，多模态预测性能显著优于简单基线，展示了该框架在精准医疗中的潜在应用价值。


<details>
