Our work offers a structured approach to bridging intrinsic values and grounded behavior for autonomous agents.

</details>


### [242] [Knowledge Graph Representations for LLM-Based Policy Compliance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27713)
*Wilder Baldwin,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于知识图谱的智能体框架，用于从AI政策文档中构建知识图谱并检索政策相关信息以回答问题。研究表明，知识图谱增强能提升LLM在政策问答任务中的表现，且LLM自发现的模式在性能上可与形式本体相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The integration of AI features into software applications raises increasing risks, necessitating regulatory responses and standards for safe and secure AI. There is a need for systems that can effectively retrieve and reason over AI policy documents.

Method: The authors constructed knowledge graphs from three AI risk-related policies using two ontology schemas, then evaluated five LLMs on 42 policy QA tasks across six reasoning types. Evaluation used both heuristic scoring and LLM-as-judge approaches.

Result: Knowledge graph augmentation improved scores for all five tested models. Notably, an open, LLM-discovered schema matched or exceeded the performance of formal ontology schemas.

Conclusion: The proposed agentic KG framework effectively improves LLM performance on policy QA tasks, with an LLM-discovered schema being competitive with formal ontologies. This suggests that KG augmentation is a viable approach for AI policy information retrieval.

Abstract: The risks posed by AI features are increasing as they are rapidly integrated into software applications. In response, regulations and standards for safe and secure AI have been proposed. In this paper, we present an agentic framework that constructs knowledge graphs (KGs) from AI policy documents and retrieves policy-relevant information to answer questions. We build KGs from three AI risk-related polices under two ontology schemas, and then evaluate five LLMs on 42 policy QA tasks spanning six reasoning types, from entity lookup to cross-policy inference, using both heuristic scoring and an LLM-as-judge. KG augmentation improves scores for all five models, and an open, LLM-discovered schema matches or exceeds the formal ontology.

</details>


### [243] [Auditing Frontier Vision-Language Models for Trustworthy Medical VQA: Grounding Failures, Format Collapse, and Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27720)
*Xupeng Chen,Binbin Shi,Chenqian Le,Qifu Yin,Lang Lin,Haowei Ni,Ran Gong,Panfeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究审计了5个前沿VLM在医学VQA场景下的失效模式，发现感知定位能力普遍不足（最高仅0.23 mIoU），自定位流程会显著降低VQA性能，且存在临床危险的左右侧混淆问题。微调可提升VQA准确率，但感知瓶颈的可解决性仍待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿VLM在专业医学输入上的失败模式尚未被充分表征，无法满足临床部署对可审计性的需求。

Method: 对5个前沿VLM在Medical VQA任务上进行审计，评估两个信任轴：感知能力（IoU、Acc@0.5定位性能）和流程整合（自定位再回答的pipeline）；并对Qwen 2.5 VL进行监督微调（SFT）。

Result: ①感知能力差：最佳模型仅达0.23 mean IoU和19.1% Acc@0.5，存在危险的左右侧混淆；②Pipeline整合导致VQA准确率下降（解析失败率达70%-99%）；③真值标注替换预测框可恢复性能，证实失败源于感知模块；④微调后Qwen 2.5 VL在SLAKE开放式recall达85.5%，为同类方法最高。

Conclusion: grounding quality是可信度的核心瓶颈；结合微调可在VQA任务上达到SOTA（85.5% recall），但感知瓶颈的解决仍需进一步研究。模型在临床部署中存在安全隐患，尤其是左右侧混淆问题。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language models (VLMs) in clinical settings demands auditable behavior under realistic failure conditions, yet the failure landscape of frontier VLMs on specialized medical inputs is poorly characterized. We audit five recent frontier and grounding-aware VLMs (Gemini~2.5~Pro, GPT-5, o3, GLM-4.5V, Qwen~2.5~VL) on Medical VQA along two trust-relevant axes. Perception: all models localize anatomical and pathological targets poorly -- the best model reaches only 0.23 mean IoU and 19.1% Acc@0.5 -- and exhibit clinically dangerous laterality confusion. Pipeline integration: a self-grounding pipeline, where the same model localizes then answers, degrades VQA accuracy for every model -- driven by both inaccurate localization and format-compliance failures under the two-step prompt (parse failure rises to 70%--99% for Gemini and GPT-5 on VQA-RAD). Replacing predicted boxes with ground-truth annotations recovers and improves VQA accuracy, consistent with the failure residing in the perception module rather than in the decomposition itself. These observational findings identify grounding quality as a primary trustworthiness bottleneck in our SLAKE bounding-box setting. As a complementary fine-tuning follow-up, supervised fine-tuning of Qwen~2.5~VL on combined Med-VQA training data attains the highest reported SLAKE open-ended recall (85.5%) among comparable methods, suggesting that the VQA-level gap is tractable with domain adaptation; whether this also closes the perception/trustworthiness bottleneck is left to future work.

</details>


### [244] [Iterative Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27724)
*Xupeng Chen,Binbin Shi,Chenqian Le,Jiaqi Zhang,Kewen Wang,Ran Gong,Jinhan Zhang,Chihang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MED-VRAG是一种创新性的迭代式多模态医学检索增强生成框架，通过直接处理PMC文档页面图像而非OCR文本，结合粗到细索引加速和VLM迭代推理机制，在医学问答任务上显著优于传统文本检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学RAG系统依赖从生物医学文献提取的文本块，丢弃了原始文档页面中丰富的视觉内容（表格、图表、结构化布局），限制了检索和推理能力。

Method: MED-VRAG采用迭代式多模态RAG框架，使用ColQwen2.5进行补丁级页面嵌入，通过离线粗到细索引实现快速检索（~350K页面，30ms内完成Stage-1），VLM在最多3轮推理中迭代精炼查询并在记忆库中累积证据。

Result: MedQA、MedMCQA、PubMedQA、MMLU-Med四项基准平均准确率达78.6%；相比无检索基线提升+5.8点；相比MedRAG+GPT-4提升+1.8点。消融实验显示：页面图像vs文本块检索贡献+1.0，迭代推理贡献+1.5，记忆库贡献+1.0。

Conclusion: MED-VRAG通过多模态检索和迭代推理有效利用医学文档中的视觉内容，在四项医学QA基准上达到78.6%平均准确率，显著优于基线方法。

Abstract: Medical retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically operate on text chunks extracted from biomedical literature, discarding the rich visual content (tables, figures, structured layouts) of original document pages. We propose MED-VRAG, an iterative multimodal RAG framework that retrieves and reasons over PMC document page images instead of OCR'd text. The system pairs ColQwen2.5 patch-level page embeddings with a sharded MapReduce LLM filter, scaling to ~350K pages while keeping Stage-1 retrieval under 30 ms via an offline coarse-to-fine index (C=8 centroids per page, ANN over centroids, exact two-way scoring on the top-R shortlist). A vision-language model (VLM) then iteratively refines its query and accumulates evidence in a memory bank across up to 3 reasoning rounds, with a single iteration costing ~15.9 s and the full three-round pipeline ~47.8 s on 4xA100. Across four medical QA benchmarks (MedQA, MedMCQA, PubMedQA, MMLU-Med), MEDVRAG reaches 78.6% average accuracy. Under controlled comparison with the same Qwen2.5-VL-32B backbone, retrieval contributes a +5.8 point gain over the no-retrieval baseline; we also note a +1.8 point edge over MedRAG + GPT-4 (76.8%), with the caveat that this is a cross-paper rather than head-to-head comparison. Ablations isolate +1.0 from page-image vs text-chunk retrieval, +1.5 from iteration, and +1.0 from the memory bank.

</details>


### [245] [Consumer Attitudes Towards AI in Digital Health: A Mixed-Methods Survey in Australia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27744)
*Wei Zhou,Rashina Hoda,Joycelyn Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI applications are increasingly being introduced into digital health. While technical performance has advanced rapidly, successful deployment mainly depends on consumer attitudes, especially to patient-facing applications. However, most existing research examines consumer attitudes towards healthcare AI at an abstract level rather than in response to concrete artefacts. We report a mixed-methods survey study in Australia (N=275) examining consumer readiness, acceptance, trust, and risk perceptions of healthcare AI, combined with a scenario-based evaluation of an AI-generated versus clinician-written consultation summary. Participants expressed moderate optimism and strong perceived usefulness and ease of use, but also substantial concerns about accuracy, safety, and data use. In the scenario task, the AI-generated summary was strongly preferred for quality, empathy, and overall usefulness, yet identification of the AI summary was near chance. Findings show that consumers judge AI through concrete communication quality and visible human governance, underscoring the need for clinically supervised deployment frameworks beyond technical performance alone.

</details>


### [246] [Intent2Tx: Benchmarking LLMs for Translating Natural Language Intents into Ethereum Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27763)
*Zhuoran Pan,Yue Li,Zhi Guan,Jianbin Hu,Zhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Intent2Tx是一个高保真基准测试，用于评估LLM将自然语言意图转换为Web3链上交易的能力，基于真实以太坊主网数据构建，采用差分状态分析进行执行感知评估，揭示了当前LLM在多步规划和执行准确性方面的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法捕获将高层用户意图转换为功能性正确、状态依赖的链上交易这一复杂过程的全部复杂性，现有工作依赖合成指令，缺乏真实世界协议交互的支撑。

Method: 提出Intent2Tx基准测试，包含29,921个单步实例和1,575个多步实例，源自300天的真实以太坊主网轨迹，涵盖11类协议交互（包括多样化长尾DeFi原语）。采用执行感知评估框架，通过在分叉主网环境上进行差分状态分析来超越表面文本匹配，实现严格评估。对16个前沿LLM进行了广泛测试。

Result: 扩展和检索增强可提升逻辑一致性和参数精度，但当前模型在分布外泛化和多步规划方面存在困难。关键发现：语法有效的输出往往无法实现预期的状态转换，揭示了当前"推理到执行"能力的显著差距。

Conclusion: Intent2Tx作为关键基础，支持在意图驱动的Web3生态中开发自主、可靠的智能体。该基准测试填补了当前LLM在Web3领域推理到执行能力的重要空白，为未来研究提供了严格评估的框架。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) offers a transformative interface for Web3, yet existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of translating high-level user intents into functionally correct, state-dependent on-chain transactions. We present \textsc{Intent2Tx}, a high-fidelity benchmark featuring 29,921 single-step and 1,575 multi-step instances meticulously derived from 300 days of real-world Ethereum mainnet traces. Unlike prior works that rely on synthetic instructions, \textsc{Intent2Tx} grounds natural language intents in real-world protocol interactions across 11 categories, including diverse long-tail Decentralized Finance (DeFi) primitives. To enable rigorous evaluation, we propose an execution-aware framework that transcends surface-level text matching by employing differential state analysis on forked mainnet environments. Our extensive evaluation of 16 state-of-the-art LLMs reveals that while scaling and retrieval-augmentation enhance logical consistency and parameter precision, current models struggle with out-of-distribution generalization and multi-step planning. Crucially, our execution-based analysis demonstrates that syntactically valid outputs often fail to achieve intended state transitions, highlighting a significant gap in current "reasoning-to-execution" capabilities. \textsc{Intent2Tx} serves as a critical foundation for developing autonomous, reliable agents in intent-centric Web3 ecosystems. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Intent2Tx_Bench-97FF .

</details>


### [247] [WindowsWorld: A Process-Centric Benchmark of Autonomous GUI Agents in Professional Cross-Application Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27776)
*Jinchao Li,Yunxin Li,Chenrui Zhao,Zhenran Xu,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WindowsWorld是一个跨应用计算机使用基准测试，通过多智能体框架生成181个任务（78%多应用），用于评估GUI智能体在复杂专业工作流程中的表现。研究表明，当前最先进的智能体在多应用任务上表现不佳（<21%成功率），难以处理跨≥3应用的条件推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current GUI agent benchmarks (e.g., OSWorld) focus on isolated single-application tasks, overlooking the critical real-world requirement of coordinating across multiple applications for complex profession-specific workflows.

Method: Multi-agent framework steered by 16 occupations generates four difficulty-level tasks with intermediate inspection, refined by human review and executed in a simulated environment.

Result: 181 tasks with an average of 5.0 sub-goals across 17 common desktop applications, 78% inherently multi-application. Leading large models achieve <21% success rate on multi-app tasks, far below single-app task performance.

Conclusion: All computer-use agents perform poorly on multi-application tasks (<21% success rate), particularly those requiring conditional judgment and reasoning across ≥3 applications. Current agents largely stall at early sub-goals with low execution efficiency despite exceeding human step limits.

Abstract: While GUI agents have shown impressive capabilities in common computer-use tasks such as OSWorld, current benchmarks mainly focus on isolated and single-application tasks. This overlooks a critical real-world requirement of coordinating across multiple applications to accomplish complex profession-specific workflows. To bridge this gap, we present a computer-use benchmark in cross-application workflows, named WindowsWorld, designed to systematically assess GUI Agents on complex multi-step tasks that mirror real-world professional activities. Our methodology uses a multi-agent framework steered by 16 occupations to generate four difficulty-level tasks with intermediate inspection, which are then refined by human review and executed in a simulated environment. The resulting benchmark contains 181 tasks with an average of 5.0 sub-goals across 17 common desktop applications, of which 78% are inherently multi-application. Experimental results of leading large models and agents show that: 1) All computer-use agents perform poorly on multi-application tasks (< 21% success rate), far below the performance of simple single-app tasks; 2) They largely fail at tasks requiring conditional judgment and reasoning across $\geq$ 3 applications, stalling at early sub-goals; 3) Low execution efficiency, where tasks often fail despite far exceeding human step limits. Code, benchmark data, and evaluation resources are available at github.com/HITsz-TMG/WindowsWorld.

</details>


### [248] [Post-Optimization Adaptive Rank Allocation for LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27796)
*Vishnuprasadh Kumaravelu,Sunil Gupta,P. K. Srijith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARA is a post-hoc, data-free compression method for LoRA that uses SVD-based singular value thresholding to achieve non-uniform, adaptive rank allocation across layers, reducing parameters by 75-90% without training modifications or performance loss.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard LoRA implementations assign uniform ranks to all layers, ignoring the varying intrinsic dimensionality of different model layers. This one-size-fits-all approach leads to parameter redundancy where some layers are significantly over-parameterized while others may need more capacity.

Method: PARA uses Singular Value Decomposition to analyze singular values across all LoRA layers and applies a global threshold for rank pruning, allocating ranks non-uniformly based on layer-wise spectral importance. This is done post-hoc without modifying the training process.

Result: 75-90% parameter reduction while preserving predictive performance across multiple vision and language benchmarks, with the method being validated on both domains.

Conclusion: PARA provides a practical, training-free solution for reducing LoRA redundancy by achieving 75-90% parameter reduction while maintaining performance, making it particularly valuable for deploying large foundation models in resource-constrained environments.

Abstract: Exponential growth in the scale of modern foundation models has led to the widespread adoption of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) as a parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique. However, standard LoRA implementations disregard the varying intrinsic dimensionality of model layers and enforce a uniform rank, leading to parameter redundancy. We propose Post-Optimization Adaptive Rank Allocation (PARA), a data-free compression method for LoRA that integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. PARA leverages Singular Value Decomposition to prune LoRA ranks using a global threshold over singular values across all layers. This results in non-uniform rank allocation based on layer-wise spectral importance. As a post-hoc method, PARA circumvents the training modifications and resulting instabilities that dynamic architectures typically incur. We empirically demonstrate that PARA reduces parameter count by 75-90\% while preserving the predictive performance of the original, uncompressed LoRA across multiple vision and language benchmarks. Code will be published upon acceptance.

</details>


### [249] [MCPHunt: An Evaluation Framework for Cross-Boundary Data Propagation in Multi-Server MCP Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27819)
*Haonan Li,Tianjun Sun,Yongqing Wang,Qisheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCPHunt is the first controlled benchmark for detecting non-adversarial credential propagation across multi-server MCP agent trust boundaries, revealing significant policy violations (11.5-41.3%) that are pathway-specific and browser-concentrated. Prompt mitigations are partially effective but insufficient as standalone defenses.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-server MCP agents create a structural information-flow control problem where faithful tool composition can inadvertently propagate credentials across trust boundaries -- a side effect of workflow topology rather than malicious behavior. No existing benchmark isolates this non-adversarial, verbatim credential propagation.

Method: (1) Canary-based taint tracking via objective string matching; (2) environment-controlled coverage design with risky/benign/hard-negative conditions controlling for credential-format confounds; (3) CRS stratification separating task-mandated from policy-violating propagation. Evaluated across 3,615 traces from 5 models on 147 tasks spanning 9 mechanism families, followed by a prompt-mitigation study.

Result: Policy-violating propagation rates of 11.5-41.3% across all tested models; propagation is pathway-specific with 25x cross-mechanism variance and concentrated in browser-mediated data flows. Prompt mitigations achieve up to 97% reduction while preserving 80.5% utility, but effectiveness correlates with instruction-following capability.

Conclusion: Policy-violating credential propagation occurs in 11.5-41.3% of cases across models, is pathway-specific (25x range), and concentrated in browser-mediated flows. Prompt-level mitigations reduce violations by up to 97% but may be insufficient alone due to varying model instruction-following capabilities.

Abstract: Multi-server MCP agents create an information-flow control problem: faithful tool composition can turn individually benign read/write permissions into cross-boundary credential propagation -- a structural side effect of workflow topology, not necessarily malicious model behavior. We present MCPHunt, to our knowledge the first controlled benchmark that isolates non-adversarial, verbatim credential propagation across multi-server MCP trust boundaries, with three methodological contributions: (1) canary-based taint tracking that reduces propagation detection to objective string matching; (2) an environment-controlled coverage design with risky, benign, and hard-negative conditions that validates pipeline soundness and controls for credential-format confounds; (3) CRS stratification that disentangles task-mandated propagation (faithful execution of verbatim-transfer instructions) from policy-violating propagation (credentials included despite the option to redact). Across 3,615 main-benchmark traces from 5 models spanning 147 tasks and 9 mechanism families, policy-violating propagation rates reach 11.5--41.3% across all models. This propagation is pathway-specific (25x cross-mechanism range) and concentrated in browser-mediated data flows; hard-negative controls provide evidence that production-format credentials are not necessary -- prompt-directed cross-boundary data flow is sufficient. A prompt-mitigation study across 3 models reduces policy-violating propagation by up to 97% while preserving 80.5% utility, but effectiveness varies with instruction-following capability -- suggesting that prompt-level defenses alone may not suffice. Code, traces, and labeling pipeline are released under MIT and CC BY 4.0.

</details>


### [250] [A Grid-Aware Agent-Based Model for Analyzing Electric Vehicle Charging Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27849)
*Khalil Al-Rahman Youssefi,Marija Gojkovic,Walter Stefanutti,Mika Auer,Melanie Schranz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Python SimPy框架的可配置电网感知ABM，用于系统分析电动汽车充电系统。通过整合异构EV行为、充电桩约束和能源沙盒的功率调节机制，该模型支持可扩展的事件驱动仿真。研究以工作场所充电场景为例，验证了基础设施适用性的情境依赖性，并展示了充电策略和充电器类型对服务水平和电网行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统性地分析电动汽车充电系统在可配置的基础设施和运营条件下的行为特征。

Method: 基于Python SimPy离散事件框架开发了一种可配置、电网感知的ABM。该模型整合了异构电动汽车行为、充电桩约束和共享的"能源沙盒"（用于调节总功率分配），支持跨不同系统规模、充电器组合和调度策略的可扩展事件驱动仿真。

Result: 结果表明基础设施适用性具有情境依赖性，充电策略和充电器类型会重塑服务水平和电网侧行为。

Conclusion: 该ABM为探索电动汽车充电生态系统的技术、运营和电网感知方面提供了灵活且可扩展的仿真环境，可作为后续高级协调策略研究的方法论基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a configurable, grid-aware Agent-Based Model (ABM) for the systematic analysis of electric vehicle (EV) charging systems under configurable infrastructure and operational conditions. The model integrates heterogeneous EV behavior, charging column constraints, and a shared Energy Sandbox that regulates aggregate power allocation, enabling the joint study of user-centric charging dynamics and facility-level power behavior. Implemented in Python using the SimPy discrete-event framework, the approach supports scalable, event-driven simulations across varying system sizes, charger compositions, and scheduling strategies. A representative workplace charging scenario is investigated to illustrate how infrastructure configuration and coordination mechanisms influence energy delivery performance, infrastructure utilization, and aggregate load characteristics. The results highlight the context-dependence of infrastructure suitability and demonstrate how charging strategies and charger types reshape both service-level outcomes and grid-facing behavior. The proposed ABM provides a flexible and extensible simulation environment for exploring technical, operational, and grid-aware aspects of EV charging ecosystems, and for serving as a methodological basis for subsequent studies on advanced coordination strategies beyond the specific scenario analyzed in this study.

</details>


### [251] [Rethinking Agentic Reinforcement Learning In Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27859)
*Fangming Cui,Ruixiao Zhu,Cheng Fang,Sunan Li,Jiahong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于LLM-based Agentic RL的综述性文章，探讨了传统强化学习向自主智能体范式的转变，分析了将大型语言模型与强化学习结合以实现目标设定、长期规划、策略自适应和交互式推理能力的方法，并展望了该领域的关键挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习局限于优化预定义奖励函数的专门化智能体，无法适应开放性任务和不确定的的真实世界环境。随着大型语言模型（LLM）的兴起和任务复杂性的增加，需要一种新的范式转变，使智能体能够自主设定目标、进行长期规划、动态调整策略，并在真实环境中进行交互式推理。

Method: 本文通过深入分析概念基础、方法论创新和有效设计，系统性地梳理了这一新兴范式的核心要素，并将元推理、自我反思和多步决策等认知能力整合到学习循环中。

Result: 本文系统性地分析了LLM-based Agentic RL的概念基础、方法论创新和有效设计，并识别了该领域面临的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 本文指出了构建LLM-based Agentic RL的关键挑战，并勾勒出有前景的未来研究方向，为该领域的后续研究提供了指导框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has traditionally focused on training specialized agents to optimize predefined reward functions within narrowly defined environments. However, the advent of powerful Large Language Models (LLMs) and increasingly complex, open-ended tasks has catalyzed a paradigm shift towards agentic paradigms within RL. This emerging framework extends beyond traditional RL by emphasizing the development of autonomous agents capable of goal-setting, long-term planning, dynamic strategy adaptation, and interactive reasoning in uncertain, real-world environments. Unlike conventional approaches that rely heavily on static objectives and episodic interactions, LLM-based Agentic RL incorporates cognitive-like capabilities such as meta-reasoning, self-reflection, and multi-step decision-making directly into the learning loop. In this paper, we provide a deep insight for looking the conceptual foundations, methodological innovations, and effective designs underlying this trend. Furthermore, we identify critical challenges and outline promising future directions for building LLM-based Agentic RL.

</details>


### [252] [KellyBench: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27865)
*Thomas Grady,Kip Parker,Iliyan Zarov,Henry Course,Chengxi Taylor,Ross Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KellyBench is a sports betting market simulation benchmark for evaluating LLM sequential decision-making in non-stationary environments. All frontier models tested lose money (best: -8% return), and strategy sophistication scores are low (best: 26.5%), highlighting significant room for improvement in open-ended decision tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing language model benchmarks saturate on narrow procedural tasks, but real-world deployment involves long-horizon, non-stationary environments with open-ended goals. There is a need for evaluation frameworks that assess sequential decision-making capabilities beyond simple benchmarks.

Method: KellyBench simulates the 2023-24 English Premier League season where agents maximize long-term bankroll growth. Agents receive detailed historical data including advanced statistics, lineups, and public odds. Success requires building ML models, identifying market edge, and adapting to environmental changes over time. Strategy sophistication is evaluated using a human expert rubric.

Result: All evaluated frontier models lose money on average over five seeds. The best performing model achieves only -8% average return, with many models experiencing ruin across seeds. Claude Opus 4.6 achieves a rubric score of 26.5%, indicating unsophisticated strategies compared to human baselines.

Conclusion: KellyBench presents a challenging sequential decision-making benchmark where all frontier LLMs fail to achieve positive returns. The best model averages -8% return, with many experiencing financial ruin. Strategy evaluation via expert rubric reveals significant sophistication gaps (26.5% for best model). This benchmark successfully identifies critical deficiencies in current LLM capabilities for long-horizon, non-stationary decision tasks with open-ended goals.

Abstract: Language models are saturating benchmarks for procedural tasks with narrow objectives. But they are increasingly being deployed in long-horizon, non-stationary environments with open-ended goals. In this paper we introduce KellyBench, an environment for evaluating sequential decision-making in sports betting markets. Agents are placed in a sequential simulation of the 2023-24 English Premier League season and tasked with maximising their long-term bankroll growth. They are given detailed historical data, including advanced statistics, lineups, and public odds. To succeed they must build machine learning models, identify edge in public markets, and adapt as the environment changes over time. We find that all frontier models evaluated lose money on average over the course of the season for five seeds. The best performing model achieves an average return of -8%, and many models experiencing ruin across seeds. To judge strategy sophistication, we use a human expert rubric to grade each model and find their approaches to be unsophisticated compared to human baselines; Claude Opus 4.6 achieves a rubric score of 26.5%, which means there is significant room for improvement. KellyBench is available as an open-access API endpoint at https://openreward.ai/GeneralReasoning/KellyBench.

</details>


### [253] [Building Persona-Based Agents On Demand: Tailoring Multi-Agent Workflows to User Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27882)
*Giuseppe Arbore,Andrea Sillano,Luigi De Russis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI are shifting automation from discrete tools to proactive multi-agent systems that coordinate multi-specialized capabilities behind unified interfaces. However, today's agent systems typically rely on hard-coded agent architectures with fixed roles, coordination patterns, and interaction flows that limit end-user personalization and make adaptation to individual needs and contexts difficult. Given this limitation, we argue that on-demand persona-based agent generation offers a promising path towards more efficient and contextually appropriate interaction within agentic workflows. By dynamically crafting agents and personas at run-time to match user characteristics, task demands, and workflow context, agentic platforms can move beyond one-size-fits-all configurations. We present a pipeline for on-demand persona generation in agentic platforms, detailing how real-time crafting of AI personas can be systematically integrated within agent systems, aiming to open new possibilities in agentic platform design paradigms.

</details>


### [254] [In-Context Prompting Obsoletes Agent Orchestration for Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27891)
*Simon Dennis,Michael Diamond,Rivaan Patil,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，对于程序化任务，将整个流程放入系统提示词让模型自我编排的方法，在质量和失败率上均优于外部编排框架（如LangGraph）。这意味着随着前沿模型能力提升，复杂的外部编排架构可能已不必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体编排框架（LangGraph、CrewAI、Google ADK、OpenAI Agents SDK等）依赖外部编排器跟踪状态并注入路由指令，架构复杂。需要验证这种复杂性是否仍然必要。

Method: 在三个领域（旅行预订14节点、Zoom技术支持14节点、保险理赔55节点）各进行200次对话评估，采用LLM-as-judge在5个质量标准上进行评分对比。

Result: 上下文方法的评分为4.53-5.00（5分制），而使用相同模型的LangGraph编排器评分为4.17-4.84。编排系统在旅行预订中失败率24%、Zoom中9%、保险理赔中17%，而上下文基线分别为11.5%、0.5%、5%。

Conclusion: 外部编排框架对于遵循定义流程的多轮对话已非必要，前沿模型的能力进步使得简单的上下文方法就足够有效。

Abstract: Agent orchestration frameworks -- LangGraph, CrewAI, Google ADK, OpenAI Agents SDK, and others -- place an external orchestrator above the LLM, tracking state and injecting routing instructions at every turn. We present a controlled comparison showing that for procedural tasks, this architecture is dominated by a simpler alternative: putting the entire procedure in the system prompt and letting the model self-orchestrate. Across three domains -- travel booking (14 nodes), Zoom technical support (14 nodes), and insurance claims processing (55 nodes) -- we evaluate 200 conversations per condition using LLM-as-judge scoring on five quality criteria. The in-context approach scores 4.53--5.00 on a 5-point scale while a LangGraph orchestrator using the same model scores 4.17--4.84. The orchestrated system fails on 24% of travel, 9% of Zoom, and 17% of insurance conversations, compared to 11.5%, 0.5%, and 5% for the in-context baseline. While external orchestration may have been necessary for earlier models, advances in frontier model capabilities have made it unnecessary for multi-turn conversations following a defined procedure.

</details>


### [255] [Graph World Models: Concepts, Taxonomy, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27895)
*Jiawei Liu,Senqiao Yang,Mingjun Wang,Yu Wang,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文首次系统性地将基于图结构的世界模型(GWMs)统一为独立研究范式，针对传统扁平张量世界模型的噪声敏感性、误差累积和弱推理等问题，提出基于关系归纳偏置(RIB)的三分类法（空间RIB、物理RIB、逻辑RIB），并展望了动态图适应、概率关系动态、多粒度归纳偏置和专用基准等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扁平张量的世界模型存在噪声敏感性、误差累积和弱推理能力等问题，难以有效处理复杂环境表示。为解决这些局限性，研究者开始利用图结构将环境分解为实体节点和交互边，在结构化空间中建模虚拟环境。

Method: 提出基于关系归纳偏置（RIB）的分类法，将现有GWMs研究分为三大类别：空间RIB（拓扑抽象）、物理RIB（动态模拟）和逻辑RIB（因果语义推理）。

Result: 系统地将图世界模型正式定义并统一为独立研究范畴，建立了基于RIB的分类体系，对各类模型的设计原则、代表模型和性能特点进行了比较分析。

Conclusion: 该综述提出了GWMs作为统一研究范式的地位，并指出了四个未来研究方向：动态图适应、概率关系动态、多粒度归纳偏置、以及专用基准测试与评估指标，为该领域的后续研究提供了清晰的发展路径。

Abstract: As one of the mainstream models of artificial intelligence, world models allow agents to learn the representation of the environment for efficient prediction and planning. However, classical world models based on flat tensors face several key problems, including noise sensitivity, error accumulation and weak reasoning. To address these limitations, many recent studies use graph structure to decompose the environment into entity nodes and interactive edges, and model virtual environments in a structured space. This paper systematically formalizes and unifies these emerging graph-based works under the concept of graph world models (GWMs). To the best of our knowledge, GWMs have not yet been explicitly defined and surveyed as a unified research paradigm. Furthermore, we propose a taxonomy based on relational inductive biases (RIB), categorizing GWMs by the specific structural priors they inject: (1) spatial RIB for topological abstraction; (2) physical RIB for dynamic simulation; and (3) logical RIB for causal and semantic reasoning. For each model category, we outline the key design principles, summarize representative models, and conduct comparative analyses. We further discuss open challenges and future directions, including dynamic graph adaptation, probabilistic relational dynamics, multi-granularity inductive biases, and the need for dedicated benchmarks and evaluation metrics for GWMs.

</details>


### [256] [Simulating clinical interventions with a generative multimodal model of human physiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27899)
*Guy Lutsker,Gal Sapir,Jordi Merino,Smadar Shilo,Anastasia Godneva,Eli Meirom,Shie Mannor,Hagai Rossman,Gal Chechik,Eran Segal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HealthFormer是一个基于Transformer的生成式生理轨迹模型，通过学习15,000+个体的多领域健康数据，实现无需任务特定训练即可完成疾病预测、风险分层和干预模拟，在4个独立验证队列中展现优异的跨人群泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人体健康随时间的演变规律，以及为何干预反应在不同个体间存在差异，是医学领域的核心挑战。

Method: 使用解码器专用Transformer架构，在Human Phenotype Project数据（15,000+深度表型个体、多访次）上训练。将667个测量指标跨7个领域进行标记化，通过单一生成目标训练模型进行生理轨迹预测，无需任务特定训练即可迁移至下游临床任务。

Result: 模型成功迁移至4个独立队列，对27/30种事件性疾病和死亡率终点预测优于对照；超越所有临床风险评分。在个性化营养试验中，条件预测恢复6个月生物标志物变化（如舒张压Pearson r=0.78）。41项随机干预比较中，预测效应方向全部一致，30例预测均值落在95%置信区间内。

Conclusion: HealthFormer定位为"初始健康世界模型"，预测、风险分层和干预条件模拟均作为查询功能，为临床数字孪生提供基础。

Abstract: Understanding how human health changes over time, and why responses to interventions vary between individuals, remains a central challenge in medicine. Here we present HealthFormer, a decoder-only transformer that models the human physiological trajectory generatively, by training on data from the Human Phenotype Project, a multi-visit cohort of over 15,000 deeply phenotyped individuals. We tokenise each participant's health trajectory across 667 measurements spanning seven domains: blood biomarkers, body composition, sleep physiology, continuous glucose monitoring, gut microbiome, wearable-derived physiology, and behaviour and medication exposure. We train HealthFormer to forecast individual physiological trajectories across these domains, and from this single generative objective a range of clinically relevant tasks can be expressed as queries on the model. We show that, without task-specific training, HealthFormer transfers to four independent cohorts and improves prediction for 27 of 30 incident-disease and mortality endpoints, exceeding established clinical risk scores in every comparison. We further show that the model can simulate interventions in silico: in a held-out personalised-nutrition trial, intervention-conditioned predictions recover individual six-month biomarker changes (e.g., Pearson r = 0.78 for diastolic blood pressure). Across 41 randomised intervention-outcome comparisons drawn from published trials, our results show that the predicted direction of effect agrees in every case, and the predicted mean falls within the reported 95% confidence interval in 30 cases. We position HealthFormer as an initial health world model, from which forecasting, risk stratification, and intervention-conditioned simulation arise as queries, providing a basis for clinical digital twins.

</details>


### [257] [From Unstructured Recall to Schema-Grounded Memory: Reliable AI Memory via Iterative, Schema-Aware Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27906)
*Alex Petrov,Alexander Gusak,Denis Mukha,Dima Korolev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: xmemory proposes a schema-grounded memory architecture that shifts memory processing from retrieval-based reads to validated writes, achieving state-of-the-art performance on structured extraction, memory benchmarks, and application tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing AI memory systems treat memory as retrieval problem (store/embed/retrieve), which works for thematic recall but fails for production agent needs: exact facts, current state, updates/deletions, aggregation, relations, negative queries, and explicit unknowns. Current approach is mismatched to "system of record" requirements.

Method: Iterative, schema-aware write path decomposing memory ingestion into: (1) object detection, (2) field detection, (3) field-value extraction, with validation gates, local retries, and stateful prompt control. Shifts interpretation from read path to write path—reads become constrained queries over verified records instead of repeated inference over retrieved prose.

Result: xmemory achieves 90.42% object-level accuracy and 62.67% output accuracy on extraction benchmark (surpassing frontier structured-output baselines); 97.10% F1 on end-to-end memory benchmark (vs 80.16%-87.24% baselines); 95.2% accuracy on application-level task (outperforming specialized memory systems, Markdown harnesses, and frontier-model application harnesses).

Conclusion: Schema-grounded write path shifts interpretation burden from read to write; xmemory achieves 90.42% object-level accuracy on extraction benchmark, 97.10% F1 on memory benchmark, and 95.2% accuracy on application-level task, demonstrating architecture > retrieval scale or model strength for stateful memory workloads

Abstract: Persistent AI memory is often reduced to a retrieval problem: store prior interactions as text, embed them, and ask the model to recover relevant context later. This design is useful for thematic recall, but it is mismatched to the kinds of memory that agents need in production: exact facts, current state, updates and deletions, aggregation, relations, negative queries, and explicit unknowns. These operations require memory to behave less like search and more like a system of record.
  This paper argues that reliable external AI memory must be schema-grounded. Schemas define what must be remembered, what may be ignored, and which values must never be inferred. We present an iterative, schema-aware write path that decomposes memory ingestion into object detection, field detection, and field-value extraction, with validation gates, local retries, and stateful prompt control. The result shifts interpretation from the read path to the write path: reads become constrained queries over verified records rather than repeated inference over retrieved prose.
  We evaluate this design on structured extraction and end-to-end memory benchmarks. On the extraction benchmark, the judge-in-the-loop configuration reaches 90.42% object-level accuracy and 62.67% output accuracy, above all tested frontier structured-output baselines. On our end-to-end memory benchmark, xmemory reaches 97.10% F1, compared with 80.16%-87.24% across the third-party baselines. On the application-level task, xmemory reaches 95.2% accuracy, outperforming specialised memory systems, code-generated Markdown harnesses, and customer-facing frontier-model application harnesses. The results show that, for memory workloads requiring stable facts and stateful computation, architecture matters more than retrieval scale or model strength alone.

</details>


### [258] [Taming the Centaur(s) with LAPITHS: a framework for a theoretically grounded interpretation of AI performances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27927)
*Matteo Da Pelo,Alessio Donvito,Claudio Frongia,Pietro Salis,Antonio Lieto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAPITHS框架通过最小认知网格和对比实验，揭示CENTAUR等统一认知模型的主张缺乏认知合理性，证明相似行为输出可在缺乏真正认知解释力的系统中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究存在行为主义倾向，将transformer语言模型的人类水平表现解读为人类-like底层计算及认知能力的证据。CENTAUR等统一认知模型提出了若干重大主张，但这些主张缺乏充分的理论和实证支持。

Method: 通过两个定量评估工具实现：(i) 最小认知网格(Minimal Cognitive Grid)，一种估计人工系统认知合理性的理论驱动方法；(ii) 行为对比实验，证明与CENTAUR等模型相似的结果可被不满足认知 plausibility 结构约束的系统复现。

Result: CENTAUR等模型提出的若干主要主张在理论和实证上均不成立。

Conclusion: LAPITHS enables principled critique of strong claims about AI systems' cognitive plausibility, highlighting that similar behavioral outputs can be achieved by systems lacking genuine cognitive explanatory power.

Abstract: We introduce a framework called LAPITHS (Language model Analysis through Paradigm grounded Interpretations of Theses about Human likenesS) and use it to show that several major claims advanced by models such as CENTAUR, proposed as an artificial Unified Model of Cognition, are not theoretically or empirically justified. LAPITHS provides a principled reference point for counteracting the current behaviouristic tendency in AI research to interpret the human level performances of transformer based language models as evidence of human like underlying computation and, by extension, as signs of cognitive abilities. The novelty of LAPITHS lies in making explicit the arguments grounded in two quantitative assessments: (i) the Minimal Cognitive Grid, a theoretically motivated method for estimating the cognitive plausibility of artificial systems, and (ii) a behavioural comparison showing that results similar to those reported for CENTAUR like models can be reproduced by other systems that do not satisfy the structural constraints typically associated with cognitive plausibility, and whose outputs do not provide independent explanatory insight into human cognition.

</details>


### [259] [MM-StanceDet: Retrieval-Augmented Multi-modal Multi-agent Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27934)
*Weihai Lu,Zhejun Zhao,Yanshu Li,Huan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multimodal Stance Detection (MSD) is crucial for understanding public discourse, yet effectively fusing text and image, especially with conflicting signals, remains challenging. Existing methods often face difficulties with contextual grounding, cross-modal interpretation ambiguity, and single-pass reasoning fragility. To address these, we propose Retrieval-Augmented Multi-modal Multi-agent Stance Detection (MM-StanceDet), a novel multi-agent framework integrating Retrieval Augmentation for contextual grounding, specialized Multimodal Analysis agents for nuanced interpretation, a Reasoning-Enhanced Debate stage for exploring perspectives, and Self-Reflection for robust adjudication. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate MM-StanceDet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the efficacy of its multi-agent architecture and structured reasoning stages in addressing complex multimodal stance challenges.

</details>


### [260] [A Collective Variational Principle Unifying Bayesian Inference, Game Theory, and Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27942)
*Djamel Bouchaffra,Faycal Ykhlef,Mustapha Lebbah,Hanane Azzag*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出博弈论自由能原理，将变分推断与博弈论统一于多智能体框架，证明局部自由能最小化隐式实现随机博弈并收敛于纳什均衡，合作博弈可表示为Gibbs分布，且存在可证伪的感觉精度-影响非单调关系。该工作为集体智能提供了跨尺度的通用变分原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（生物、物理、人工系统）中普遍涌现出集体智能现象，但缺乏统一理论框架整合贝叶斯推理/变分推断与博弈论。自由能原理解释了单智能体的适应行为，但尚未扩展到多智能体交互场景；同时博弈论虽形式化了策略交互，却未与神经科学和统计物理中的变分原理建立联系。

Method: 该研究采用理论推导与实证验证相结合的方法。理论上，证明了多智能体局部自由能最小化隐式实现随机博弈；在有限理性和局部信息约束下，建立了自由能与纳什均衡的对应关系；同时将合作博弈重新表述为联盟上的Gibbs分布。为表征高阶效应，引入了Harsanyi红利的自由能形式化表示。实证上，在神经、生物和人工多智能体系统中验证了理论预测。

Result: 建立了"博弈论自由能原理"统一框架；证明集体自由能稳态点对应近似纳什均衡；合作博弈可表示为Gibbs分布；引入自由能版Harsanyi红利量化多智能体协同；发现感觉精度与智能体影响存在非单调关系的可证伪预测；在三类系统中验证了预测。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个统一框架，揭示了推理、热力学和博弈论均衡共享的变分原理。核心贡献包括：（1）证明了在有限理性和局部信息约束下，集体自由能的稳态点对应于近似纳什均衡；（2）建立了合作博弈的变分表示，其中均衡表现为联盟上的吉布斯分布；（3）引入了自由能版本的Harsanyi红利，用于量化不可约的多智能体协同效应；（4）验证了感觉精度与智能体影响之间存在可证伪的非单调关系。该框架已在神经、生物和人工多智能体系统中得到验证，为理解集体智能提供了一个跨尺度的通用原理。

Abstract: Collective intelligence emerges across biological, physical, and artificial systems without central coordination, yet a unifying principle governing such behaviour remains elusive. The Free Energy Principle explains how individual agents adapt through variational inference, while game theory formalises strategic interactions. Here we introduce the Game-Theoretic Free Energy Principle, a unified framework showing that multi-agent systems performing local free-energy minimisation implicitly implement a stochastic game. We prove that, under bounded rationality and local information constraints, stationary points of collective free energy correspond to approximate Nash equilibria of an induced game. Conversely, a broad class of cooperative games admits a variational representation in which equilibria arise as Gibbs distributions over coalitions, establishing a bridge between Bayesian inference and strategic interaction. To characterise higher-order effects, we introduce a free-energy formulation of the Harsanyi dividend, isolating irreducible multi-agent synergy. This yields a predictive theory of cooperation, including a falsifiable non-monotonic relationship between sensory precision and agent influence. We validate this prediction across neural, biological, and artificial multi-agent systems. These results identify a common variational principle underlying inference, thermodynamics, and game-theoretic equilibrium.

</details>


### [261] [The Effects of Visual Priming on Cooperative Behavior in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27953)
*Kenneth J. K. Ong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了视觉提示对视觉-语言模型(VLM)合作行为的影响，采用迭代囚徒困境(IPD)测试场景，通过行为概念图像和颜色编码奖励矩阵检验多种模型的决策模式。研究发现VLM行为会受到图像内容和颜色线索影响，且不同模型对视觉提示的敏感性和缓解策略有效性存在差异，强调了为视觉丰富和安全关键环境中部署VLM建立稳健评估框架的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉-语言模型(VLM)日益深入融入决策系统，理解视觉输入如何影响其行为变得至关重要。该研究旨在探究视觉提示对VLM合作行为的影响，以评估其在安全关键环境中的可靠性。

Method: 采用迭代囚徒困境(IPD)作为测试场景，检验暴露于行为概念图像（善良/乐于助人 vs. 攻击性/自私）以及颜色编码奖励矩阵对VLM决策模式的影响。实验涵盖多种最先进的VLM，并探索了三种缓解策略：提示修改、思维链(CoT)推理和视觉token减少。

Result: VLM的行为会受到图像内容和颜色线索的双重影响，不同模型表现出不同程度的敏感性和不同的缓解策略有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉提示能够影响VLM的合作行为，模型对图像内容和颜色线索的敏感程度存在差异，且缓解策略效果各异。研究强调了为视觉丰富和安全关键环境中部署VLM建立稳健评估框架的重要性，同时指出模型的架构和训练差异会导致不同的行为响应，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) become increasingly integrated into decision-making systems, it is essential to understand how visual inputs influence their behavior. This paper investigates the effects of visual priming on VLMs' cooperative behavior using the Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD) as a test scenario. We examine whether exposure to images depicting behavioral concepts (kindness/helpfulness vs. aggressiveness/selfishness) and color-coded reward matrices alters VLM decision patterns. Experiments were conducted across multiple state-of-the-art VLMs. We further explore mitigation strategies including prompt modifications, Chain of Thought (CoT) reasoning, and visual token reduction. Results show that VLM behavior can be influenced by both image content and color cues, with varying susceptibility and mitigation effectiveness across models. These findings not only underscore the importance of robust evaluation frameworks for VLM deployment in visually rich and safety-critical environments, but also highlight how architectural and training differences among models may lead to distinct behavioral responses-an area worthy of further investigation.

</details>


### [262] [GUI Agents with Reinforcement Learning: Toward Digital Inhabitants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27955)
*Junan Hu,Jian Liu,Jingxiang Lai,Jiarui Hu,Yiwei Sheng,Shuang Chen,Jian Li,Dazhao Du,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了强化学习与图形用户界面(GUI)智能体的交叉领域，提出了包含离线RL、在线RL和混合策略的原则性分类体系，揭示了复合奖励架构、世界模型训练和System-2推理涌现等关键趋势，并为GUI自动化发展提供了研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调无法处理长时序信用分配、分布偏移和不可逆环境中的安全探索问题，因此强化学习成为推进GUI智能体自动化的核心方法学。

Method: 提出了一个原则性分类体系，将现有方法组织为离线强化学习、在线强化学习和混合策略三大类，并辅以奖励工程、数据效率和关键技术创新的分析。

Result: 揭示三大趋势：(1)可靠性与可扩展性之间的张力推动复合多层级奖励架构的采用；(2)GUI输入输出延迟瓶颈加速了基于世界模型的训练的转变，可带来显著性能提升；(3)System-2风格推理的自发涌现表明在足够丰富的奖励信号下可能无需显式推理监督。

Conclusion: 提出了一个涵盖过程奖励、持续强化学习、认知架构和安全部署的研究路线图，旨在指导下一代鲁棒的GUI自动化及其智能体原生基础设施的发展。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for intelligent systems that perceive and interact with graphical interfaces visually. Yet supervised fine-tuning alone cannot handle long-horizon credit assignment, distribution shifts, and safe exploration in irreversible environments, making Reinforcement Learning (RL) a central methodology for advancing automation. In this work, we present the first comprehensive overview of the intersection between RL and GUI agents, and examine how this research direction may evolve toward digital inhabitants. We propose a principled taxonomy that organizes existing methods into Offline RL, Online RL, and Hybrid Strategies, and complement it with analyses of reward engineering, data efficiency, and key technical innovations. Our analysis reveals several emerging trends: the tension between reliability and scalability is motivating the adoption of composite, multi-tier reward architectures; GUI I/O latency bottlenecks are accelerating the shift toward world-model-based training, which can yield substantial performance gains; and the spontaneous emergence of System-2-style deliberation suggests that explicit reasoning supervision may not be necessary when sufficiently rich reward signals are available. We distill these findings into a roadmap covering process rewards, continual RL, cognitive architectures, and safe deployment, aiming to guide the next generation of robust GUI automation and its agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [263] [Synthetic Computers at Scale for Long-Horizon Productivity Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28181)
*Tao Ge,Baolin Peng,Hao Cheng,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的合成计算机环境生成方法，用于训练AI智能体完成长时域生产力任务。通过创建逼真的文件系统和工作环境，并在其上运行多智能体模拟，产生大量训练信号，实验验证了该方法能显著提升智能体在生产力场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域生产力任务高度依赖用户特定的计算机环境，包含目录结构和内容丰富的工件，现有合成数据生成方法难以规模化创建此类环境。

Method: 提出Synthetic Computers at Scale方法论，生成逼真的文件夹层级结构和内容丰富的人工制品（文档、电子表格、演示文稿等）；基于每个合成计算机运行长时域模拟：一个智能体创建需要约一个月工作量多个专业交付物的生产力目标，另一个智能体扮演用户角色执行文件系统导航、与模拟协作者协调等任务。

Result: 创建1000个合成计算机，单次模拟平均需8小时以上、2000+轮对话，产生丰富的体验式学习信号，在领域内和领域外生产力评估中均取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展至百万甚至数十亿量级的合成用户环境，为长时域生产力场景中的智能体自我改进和强化学习提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Realistic long-horizon productivity work is strongly conditioned on user-specific computer environments, where much of the work context is stored and organized through directory structures and content-rich artifacts. To scale synthetic data creation for such productivity scenarios, we introduce Synthetic Computers at Scale, a scalable methodology for creating such environments with realistic folder hierarchies and content-rich artifacts (e.g., documents, spreadsheets, and presentations). Conditioned on each synthetic computer, we run long-horizon simulations: one agent creates productivity objectives that are specific to the computer's user and require multiple professional deliverables and about a month of human work; another agent then acts as that user and keeps working across the computer -- for example, navigating the filesystem for grounding, coordinating with simulated collaborators, and producing professional artifacts -- until these objectives are completed.
  In preliminary experiments, we create 1,000 synthetic computers and run long-horizon simulations on them; each run requires over 8 hours of agent runtime and spans more than 2,000 turns on average. These simulations produce rich experiential learning signals, whose effectiveness is validated by significant improvements in agent performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain productivity evaluations. Given that personas are abundant at billion scale, this methodology can in principle scale to millions or even billions of synthetic user worlds with sufficient compute, enabling broader coverage of diverse professions, roles, contexts, environments, and productivity needs. We argue that scalable synthetic computer creation, together with at-scale simulations, is highly promising as a foundational substrate for agent self-improvement and agentic reinforcement learning in long-horizon productivity scenarios.

</details>


### [264] [Splitting Assumption-Based Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27964)
*Giovanni Buraglio,Wolfgang Dvorak,Stefan Woltran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对ABAFs实例化到图形式时产生的指数级增长问题，提出在知识库层面而非图实例化层面进行splitting的方法，并将其推广到参数化版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABAFs向图形式（如AFs、SETAFs）的实例化会产生指数级增长，可能削弱splitting在ABAF argument graphs上的实用性。

Method: 将splitting策略从基于图的方法转移到基于知识库的方法；对ABAFs引入参数化版本的splitting技术。

Result: 成功将splitting应用于ABAFs的知识库层面，并建立了参数化版本的splitting框架。

Conclusion: 提出在知识库层面而非图实例化层面进行splitting的方法，并将其推广到ABAFs的参数化版本，为缓解ABAFs实例化带来的指数级增长问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Assumption-Based Argumentation (ABA) is a well-established formalism for modelling and reasoning over debates, with a wide range of applications. However, the high computational complexity of core reasoning tasks in ABA poses a significant challenge for its applicability. This issue is further aggravated when ABA frameworks (ABAFs) are instantiated into graph-based argumentation formalisms, such as Dung's Argumentation Frameworks (AFs) and Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks (SETAFs). In knowledge representation and reasoning, a key strategy to address computational intractability is to optimise reasoning over a given knowledge base through divide-and-conquer algorithms. A paradigmatic example of this approach is splitting, where extensions of a given framework are computed incrementally, by restricting the search space to sub-frameworks only, and then combining the obtained results. This approach has been successfully applied to AFs, for which also a parametrised version has been introduced under stable semantics. However, the exponential growth produced by the instantiation might undermine the usefulness of splitting on the argument graphs induced by ABAFs. To address this issue, our work investigates the concept of splitting on the knowledge base rather than on its graph-based instantiation. Furthermore, we generalise splitting to its parametrised version for ABAFs.

</details>


### [265] [From LLM-Driven Trading Card Generation to Procedural Relatedness: A Pokémon Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27972)
*Johannes Pfau,Panagiotis Vrettis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper explores using Large Language Models and Image Diffusion Models for procedural generation of Trading Card Game cards, proposing a player-centric co-creation pipeline. A user study with 49 participants generating 196 Pokémon cards demonstrates high satisfaction and successful idea realization, suggesting generative AI can address metagame stagnation and enhance player engagement through personalized content creation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trading Card Games face inherent challenges: as metagames stabilize, predictable strategies dominate and viable card options diminish, leading to repetitive and impaired player experiences. The research explores how modern generative AI can address these issues by enabling personalized, infinite card design possibilities while expanding creative range.

Method: The paper presents a pipeline combining player-centric co-creation, fine-tuned embeddings, local Large Language Models, and Image Diffusion Models to generate dynamic, personalized TCG cards. The system was evaluated through a user study where participants created Pokémon card samples.

Result: Participants rated the aesthetics and representativeness of both visuals and mechanics, providing qualitative feedback. Results indicate that the pipeline successfully enabled most participants to realize their own creative ideas through iterative prompt adjustments.

Conclusion: Results from a user study with 49 participants (196 Pokémon card samples) show high satisfaction, with most participants successfully realizing their ideas through prompt adjustments. This work lays groundwork for future PCG systems and offers alternatives to conventional metagame evolution through "procedural relatedness" - fostering unique player-card connections.

Abstract: Since the dawn of Trading Card Games, the genre has grown into a multi-billion-dollar industry engaging millions of analog and digital players worldwide. Popular TCGs rely on regular updates, balance adjustments, and rotating constraints to sustain engagement. Yet, as metagames stabilize, predictable strategies dominate and viable card options diminish, often resulting in repetitive and impaired player experiences.
  This paper investigates the use of Large Language Models and Image Diffusion Models for Procedural Content Generation of TCG cards, addressing these challenges by enabling a personalized infinity of card designs. Modern generative AI not only enables large-scale content creation but could even introduce procedural relatedness, fostering unique connections between players and their cards. We present a pipeline combining player-centric co-creation, fine-tuned embeddings, local LLMs, and Diffusion Models to generate dynamic, personalized cards while potentially expanding creative range.
  We evaluated the pipeline in a user study with 49 participants who generated 196 Pokémon card samples. Participants rated aesthetics and representativeness of visuals and mechanics, and provided qualitative feedback. Results show high satisfaction and indicate that most participants successfully realized their own ideas through prompt adjustments. These findings lay groundwork for future content generation systems and alternatives to conventional metagame evolution through procedural relatedness.

</details>


### [266] [D3-Gym: Constructing Real-World Verifiable Environments for Data-Driven Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27977)
*Hanane Nour Moussa,Yifei Li,Zhuoyang Li,Yankai Yang,Cheng Tang,Tianshu Zhang,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Ali Payani,Ziru Chen,Huan Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D3-Gym is the first automatically constructed dataset with verifiable environments for scientific Data-Driven Discovery, comprising 565 tasks from 239 real scientific repositories. It provides comprehensive task specifications including instructions, environments, datasets, and evaluation scripts. Rigorous evaluation confirms 87.5% agreement with human gold standards. Training on D3-Gym trajectories yields significant improvements across Qwen3 models, boosting Qwen3-32B by 7.8 points on ScienceAgentBench.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite recent progress in language models and agents for scientific data-driven discovery, further advancement is hindered by the absence of verifiable environments representing real-world scientific tasks.

Method: D3-Gym constructs 565 tasks from 239 real scientific repositories across 4 disciplines. Each task includes natural language instruction, executable environment with pre-installed dependencies, input dataset, artifact previews, reference code solution, and automatically synthesized evaluation script.

Result: Evaluation scripts achieve 87.5% agreement with human-annotated gold standards with strong alignment in domain-specific evaluation logic. Training on D3-Gym trajectories yields consistent and substantial gains across Qwen3 models of varying sizes, boosting Qwen3-32B by 7.8 absolute points on ScienceAgentBench.

Conclusion: D3-Gym provides a comprehensive benchmark suite with verified scientific evaluation logic (87.5% human agreement). Training on D3-Gym trajectories yields consistent improvements, boosting Qwen3-32B by 7.8 points on ScienceAgentBench and narrowing the gap with proprietary models. All artifacts are open-sourced.

Abstract: Despite recent progress in language models and agents for scientific data-driven discovery, further advancing their capabilities is held back by the absence of verifiable environments representing real-world scientific tasks.To fill this gap, we introduce D3-Gym, the first automatically constructed dataset with verifiable environments for scientific Data-Driven Discovery. D3-Gym comprises (1) 565 tasks sourced from 239 real scientific repositories across four disciplines where (2) each task is equipped with a natural language instruction, an executable environment with pre-installed dependencies, input dataset and artifact previews, a reference code solution, and an automatically synthesized evaluation script. Rigorous evaluation of the quality of the verification signal in D3-Gym confirms that our evaluation scripts achieve 87.5% agreement with human-annotated gold standards and strong alignment in domain-specific evaluation logic, showing their scientific soundness. Further, training on trajectories sampled from D3-Gym yields consistent and substantial gains across Qwen3 models of varying sizes on ScienceAgentBench, boosting Qwen3-32B by 7.8 absolute points and substantially shrinking the gap with strong proprietary models. All D3-Gym artifacts (environments, creation workflow, trajectories, and models) can be found at https://github.com/OSU-NLP-Group/D3-Gym.

</details>


### [267] [SpecVQA: A Benchmark for Spectral Understanding and Visual Question Answering in Scientific Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28039)
*Jialu Shen,Han Lyu,Suyang Zhong,Hanzheng Li,Haoyi Tao,Nan Wang,Changhong Chen,Xi Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpecVQA是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在科学光谱理解方面能力的专业基准测试,包含7种代表性光谱类型、620幅图像和3100个问答对。该研究还提出了一种光谱数据采样与插值重建方法以有效压缩token长度,并通过消融实验验证了该方法的有效性,同时建立了MLLMs在科学光谱理解上的排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱是一种普遍存在但信息高度密集的科学图像形式,由于其非结构化和领域特定的特点,对多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)构成了重大挑战。现有模型缺乏对科学光谱的专业理解能力。

Method: 1) 构建SpecVQA基准测试,涵盖7种代表性光谱类型,从同行评审文献中收集620幅图像和3100个QA对,针对直接信息提取和领域特定推理两类任务; 2) 提出光谱数据采样与插值重建方法,在保留关键曲线特征的同时有效减少token长度。

Result: 消融实验证实所提方法在SpecVQA基准上实现了显著的性能提升;对主流MLLMs进行了科学光谱理解能力测试并建立了排行榜。

Conclusion: 该工作为提升多模态大模型的光谱理解能力迈出了重要一步,为将视觉-语言模型扩展到更广泛的科学研究和数据分析领域提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Spectra are a prevalent yet highly information-dense form of scientific imagery, presenting substantial challenges to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to their unstructured and domain-specific characteristics. Here we introduce SpecVQA, a professional scientific-image benchmark for evaluating multimodal models on scientific spectral understanding, covering 7 representative spectrum types with expert-annotated question-answer pairs. The aim comprises two aspects: spectra scientific QA evaluation and corresponding underlying task evaluation. SpecVQA contains 620 figures and 3100 QA pairs curated from peer-reviewed literature, targeting both direct information extraction and domain-specific reasoning. To effectively reduce token length while preserving essential curve characteristics, we propose a spectral data sampling and interpolation reconstruction approach. Ablation studies further confirm that the approach achieves substantial performance improvements on the proposed benchmark. We test the capability of prominent MLLMs in scientific spectral understanding on our benchmark and present a leaderboard. This work represents an essential step toward enhancing spectral understanding in multimodal large models and suggests promising directions for extending visual-language models to broader scientific research and data analysis.

</details>


### [268] [Agent-Agnostic Evaluation of SQL Accuracy in Production Text-to-SQL Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28049)
*Taslim Jamal Arif,Kuldeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STEF是一个生产原生的Text-to-SQL评估框架，突破性地无需数据库schema和参考查询即可评估SQL质量。通过语义特征提取、归一化对齐和复合评分机制（含过滤器对齐、语义判断、置信度），实现0-100准确度评分，同时支持问题质量验证、可配置规则注入及生产级规范化处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL评估基准存在根本性局限：基于规则的SQL匹配和依赖模式的语义解析器均需访问真实查询和数据库schema，这在实际生产环境中几乎无法满足。导致生产T2SQL智能体除开发时测试外几乎无法被评估，形成无声质量退化且缺乏持续改进的反馈机制。

Method: STEF通过三阶段流程实现schema无关评估：首先从自然语言问题（含用户原始问题及丰富化重述版本）和生成SQL中提取语义规格；其次执行归一化特征对齐；最后通过复合度量指标生成0-100准确度评分，指标涵盖过滤器对齐、语义判断及评估置信度。该框架还内置GROUP BY容忍度、ORDER BY默认值和LIMIT启发式等生产环境规范化处理机制。

Result: 实验结果表明STEF能够实现无需schema依赖的生产持续监控和智能体改进反馈循环，使结构化查询评估首次可在规模化场景中真正落地应用。

Conclusion: STEF首次实现无需数据库模式和参考查询的大规模结构化查询评估，使生产级T2SQL系统的持续监控和智能体迭代优化成为可能，解决了传统评估方法与真实部署环境之间的根本性脱节问题。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL (T2SQL) evaluation in production environments poses fundamental challenges that existing benchmarks do not address. Current evaluation methodologies whether rule-based SQL matching or schema-dependent semantic parsers assume access to ground-truth queries and structured database schema, constraints that are rarely satisfied in real-world deployments. This disconnect leaves production T2SQL agents largely unevaluated beyond developer-time testing, creating silent quality degradation with no feedback mechanism for continuous improvement. We present STEF (Schema-agnostic Text-to-SQL Evaluation Framework), a production-native evaluation system that operates exclusively on natural language inputs the user question, an enriched reformulation, and the generated SQL without requiring database schema or reference queries. STEF extracts semantic specifications from both natural language and SQL representations, performs normalized feature alignment, and produces an interpretable 0 to 100 accuracy score via a composite metric that encompasses filter alignment, semantic verdict, and confidence of the evaluator. Key contributions include: enriched question quality validation as a first-class evaluation signal, configurable application-specific rule injection via prompt templating, and production-robust normalization handling GROUP BY tolerance, ORDER BY defaults, and LIMIT heuristics. Empirical results demonstrate that STEF enables continuous production monitoring and agent improvement feedback loops without schema dependency, making structured query evaluation viable at scale for the first time.

</details>


### [269] [RHyVE: Competence-Aware Verification and Phase-Aware Deployment for LLM-Generated Reward Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28056)
*Feiyu Wu,Xu Zheng,Zhuocheng Wang,Yi ming Dai,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强化学习中LLM生成奖励的部署时机问题，提出RHyVE协议，通过能力感知验证和阶段感知部署来克服生成的奖励不可靠的问题。实验表明奖励排名在低策略能力时不可靠，需要在任务相关阈值后才有信息量，且奖励生成与部署应作为耦合问题研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使强化学习中的奖励设计更加可扩展，但生成的奖励并非自动可靠的训练目标。现有工作主要关注奖励候选的生成、进化或选择，而较少关注这些候选在策略优化过程中何时可以验证和部署。

Method: 提出了RHyVE，一个能力感知验证和阶段感知部署协议，使用短视野分叉验证从共享策略检查点比较小规模奖励假设集。

Result: 实验表明：1）奖励排名在低能力时不可靠，但会在任务相关阈值后变得有信息量；2）在稀疏操作任务中，阶段感知部署改善了锁定协议下的峰值和保留性能；3）LLM生成的奖励候选表现出阶段依赖的赢家变化，但没有固定的预热计划是普遍最优的；4）RHyVE应被理解为验证知情部署协议而非通用调度器。

Conclusion: 奖励生成与奖励部署应作为耦合问题进行研究：生成的奖励必须在不断变化的策略能力下进行验证和部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) make reward design in reinforcement learning substantially more scalable, but generated rewards are not automatically reliable training objectives. Existing work has focused primarily on generating, evolving, or selecting reward candidates, while paying less attention to when such candidates can be verified and deployed during policy optimization. We study this deployment-time problem by treating generated rewards as reward hypotheses whose utility depends on the competence of the current policy and the phase of training. We propose \textsc{RHyVE}, a competence-aware verification and phase-aware deployment protocol that compares small sets of reward hypotheses from shared policy checkpoints using short-horizon fork verification. Our experiments show that reward rankings are unreliable at low competence but become informative after task-dependent thresholds. On a sparse manipulation task, phase-aware deployment improves peak and retained performance under a locked protocol. Updated LLM-generated reward-candidate experiments show candidate-family-dependent behavior: generated pools can exhibit phase-dependent winner changes, but no fixed warm-up schedule is universally optimal. Held-out schedule selection, conservative selector baselines, compute-matched controls, and scale controls further show that \textsc{RHyVE} is best understood as a verification-informed deployment protocol rather than a universal scheduler. Dense and all-failure boundary experiments delimit the scope of the method. Together, these results suggest that reward generation and reward deployment should be studied as coupled problems: generated rewards must be verified and deployed under changing policy competence.

</details>


### [270] [Characterizing the Consistency of the Emergent Misalignment Persona](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28082)
*Anietta Weckauff,Yuchen Zhang,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过微调 Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct 在六个狭窄不对齐领域，发现 EM 人格存在两种模式：一致人格（有害行为与自我报告不对齐耦合）和反转人格（输出有害但自认对齐），挑战了 EM 人格一致性的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究新兴对齐（EM）现象中，有害行为与自我评估之间的对应关系在不同任务和微调领域中的一致性。

Method: 在六个狭窄不对齐领域（如不安全代码、风险金融建议、不当医疗建议）对 Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct 进行微调，并进行多项实验评估，包括危害性评估、自我评估、在两个 AI 系统描述间选择、输出识别和分数预测。

Result: 揭示了两种不同模式：一致人格模型（有害行为与自我报告不对齐相关联）和反转人格模型（产生有害输出但自认为是对齐的 AI 系统）。

Conclusion: 研究发现新兴对齐（EM）人格比之前认为的更加细粒度，存在两种不同模式：一致人格模型（有害行为与自我报告的不对齐相耦合）和反转人格模型（产生有害输出但自认为是 aligned AI 系统），这挑战了 EM 人格一致性的假设。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly misaligned data generalizes to broadly misaligned behavior, a phenomenon termed emergent misalignment (EM). While prior work has found a correlation between harmful behavior and self-assessment in emergently misaligned models, it remains unclear how consistent this correspondence is across tasks and whether it varies across fine-tuning domains. We characterize the consistency of the EM persona by fine-tuning Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned domains (e.g., insecure code, risky financial advice, bad medical advice) and administering experiments including harmfulness evaluation, self-assessment, choosing between two descriptions of AI systems, output recognition, and score prediction. Our results reveal two distinct patterns: coherent-persona models, in which harmful behavior and self-reported misalignment are coupled, and inverted-persona models, which produce harmful outputs while identifying as aligned AI systems. These findings reveal a more fine-grained picture of the effects of emergent misalignment, calling into question the consistency of the EM persona.

</details>


### [271] [Splitting Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks and Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28112)
*Matti Berthold,Lydia Blümel,Giovanni Buraglio,Anna Rapberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对包含支持关系的论证形式化框架提出了新的分裂技术，以双向集合论证框架(BSAFs)为研究对象，涵盖集体攻击和集体支持等多种分裂形式，并证明其对常见论证语义的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着论证框架表达能力的增强（从SETAFs到BAFs再到BSAFs），需要开发多样化的分裂技术来处理更复杂的论证结构，以建立论证形式化理论与结构化论证之间的桥梁。

Method: 基于BSAFs的统一框架，分别针对集体攻击、集体支持以及两者同时存在的情况提出相应的分裂模式。

Result: 建立了适用于不同分裂场景的分裂模式，并证明了这些模式对常见论证语义的正确性。

Conclusion: BSAFs能够自然地捕捉一般的（非平面的）假设基础论证，分裂技术的提出增强了其在实际应用中的可处理性和可扩展性。

Abstract: This work proposes novel splitting techniques for argumentation formalisms that incorporate supports between defeasible elements. We base our studies on bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs) which generalize argumentation frameworks with collective attacks (SETAFs), as well as bipolar argumentation frameworks (BAFs), by incorporating both collective attacks and supports. Notably, BSAFs establish a crucial link to structured argumentation as they naturally capture general (potentially non-flat) assumption-based argumentation. The increase in expressiveness calls for diverse forms of splitting. We consider splits over collective attacks (thereby generalizing the recently proposed splitting techniques for SETAFs), splits over collective supports, as well as splits over both collective attacks and supports. We establish suitable splitting schemata and prove their correctness for the most common argumentation semantics.

</details>


### [272] [LLM as Clinical Graph Structure Refiner: Enhancing Representation Learning in EEG Seizure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28178)
*Lincan Li,Zheng Chen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索将大语言模型作为图边精炼器用于脑电图癫痫检测。鉴于现有图构建方法易受EEG噪声影响产生冗余边，提出Transformer+MLP构建初始图、LLM精炼边缘的两阶段框架。在TUSZ数据集上验证了该方法能有效移除冗余连接，提升检测精度并生成更可解释的图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图构建方法（基于相关性或学习）因EEG信号噪声大，容易产生冗余或不相关的边，严重损害图表示质量和下游任务性能。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段框架：第一阶段使用Transformer边缘预测器和MLP为潜在边缘分配概率分数，通过阈值确定边缘存在性；第二阶段利用LLM作为边缘集精炼器，综合文本和统计特征验证剩余连接，实现智能边缘筛选。

Result: 实验结果表明LLM精炼的图学习框架不仅提升了任务性能，还产生了更清洁、更可解释的图结构。

Conclusion: 提出的大语言模型图细化框架在TUSZ数据集上显著提升了癫痫检测精度，同时生成了更清晰、更可解释的图结构，证明了LLM在图学习中的边缘细化能力。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) signals are vital for automated seizure detection, but their inherent noise makes robust representation learning challenging. Existing graph construction methods, whether correlation-based or learning-based, often generate redundant or irrelevant edges due to the noisy nature of EEG data. This significantly impairs the quality of graph representation and limits downstream task performance. Motivated by the remarkable reasoning and contextual understanding capabilities of large language models (LLMs), we explore the idea of using LLMs as graph edge refiners. Specifically, we propose a two-stage framework: we first verify that LLM-based edge refinement can effectively identify and remove redundant connections, leading to significant improvements in seizure detection accuracy and more meaningful graph structures. Building on this insight, we further develop a robust solution where the initial graph is constructed using a Transformer-based edge predictor and multilayer perceptron, assigning probability scores to potential edges and applying a threshold to determine their existence. The LLM then acts as an edge set refiner, making informed decisions based on both textual and statistical features of node pairs to validate the remaining connections. Extensive experiments on TUSZ dataset demonstrate that our LLM-refined graph learning framework not only enhances task performance but also yields cleaner and more interpretable graph representations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [Online Monotone Metric Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27059)
*Christian Coester,Yichen Huang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出在线单调度量嵌入新范式，通过允许嵌入距离单调递减来放松传统在线嵌入的不可撤销约束。该方法成功突破经典 Ω(min(n, log n log Δ)) 下界，静态场景达 O(log² n) 失真度，动态场景概率版本达 O(l log l) 失真度，逼近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线嵌入要求点到达时立即且不可撤销地嵌入，导致 Ω(min(n, log n log Δ)) 的强失真度下界，限制了算法设计空间。通过引入单调性松弛（距离只能缩小不能扩大），可在保持与现有算法兼容的同时突破此下界障碍。

Method: 提出在线单调度量嵌入框架，允许嵌入目标空间中点间距离随时间单调递减；采用概率方法处理动态场景中点的到达与离开。

Result: 静态场景：嵌入到 HST 实现 O(log² n) 失真度；动态场景：概率单调嵌入实现 O(l log l) 失真度（下界为 Ω(l)）。

Conclusion: 通过提出在线单调度量嵌入这一新范式，成功突破了传统在线嵌入的 Ω(min(n, log n log Δ)) 下界障碍，实现了 O(log² n) 的失真度；在动态场景下，概率单调嵌入可达 O(l log l) 失真度，逼近 Ω(l) 的下界，几乎最优。

Abstract: Metric embeddings into structured spaces, particularly hierarchically well-separated trees (HSTs), are a fundamental tool in the design of online algorithms. In the classical online embedding setting, points arrive sequentially and must be embedded irrevocably upon arrival, resulting in strong distortion lower bounds of $Ω(\min(n, \log n\log Δ))$, where $n$ is the number of points and $Δ$ their aspect ratio.
  We propose a novel relaxation, \emph{online monotone metric embeddings}, which allows distances between embedded points in the target space to decrease monotonically over time. Such relaxed embeddings remain compatible with many online algorithms. Moreover, this relaxation breaks existing lower bound barriers, enabling embeddings into HSTs with distortion $O(\log^2 n)$.
  We also study a dynamic variant, where points may both arrive and depart, seeking distortion guarantees in terms of the maximum number $l$ of simultaneously present points. For traditional embeddings, such bounds are impossible, and this limitation persists even for deterministic monotone embeddings. Surprisingly, probabilistic monotone embeddings allow for $O(l \log l)$ distortion, which is nearly optimal given an $Ω(l)$ lower bound.

</details>


### [274] [Improved Approximation Algorithm for Maximum Balanced Biclique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27141)
*Pasin Manurangsi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了最大平衡二分子图（MBB）问题，提出一个多项式时间近似算法，将近似比从 $O(n/(\log n)^2)$ 改进至 $O(n/(\log n)^3)$，从而在近似比层面与 Feige (2004) 的最大团问题结果仅差 $O(\log\log n)$ 因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答了 Chalermsook 等人 (2020) 提出的公开问题，即能否改进最大平衡二分子图问题的多项式时间近似算法。

Method: 通过设计新的近似算法，突破了此前 $O\left(\frac{n}{(\log n)^2}\right)$ 的近似比界限，将分母中的 $(\log n)^2$ 提升至 $(\log n)^3$。

Result: 给出了一个多项式时间的 $\frac{n}{\widetilde{\Omega}((\log n)^3)}$-近似算法，比此前最好的 $\frac{n}{\Omega((\log n)^2)}$-近似算法更优。

Conclusion: 改进了此前 Chalermsook 等人 (2020) 的结果，缩小了与最大团问题近似比之间的差距（在 O(log log n) 因子内）。

Abstract: We study the Maximum Balanced Biclique (MBB) problem: Given a bipartite graph $G$ with $n$ vertices on each side, find a balanced biclique in $G$ with maximum size. We give a polynomial-time $\left(\frac{n}{\widetildeΩ\left((\log n)^3\right)}\right)$-approximation algorithm for the problem, which improves upon an $\left(\frac{n}{Ω\left((\log n)^2\right)}\right)$-approximation by Chalermsook et al. (2020) and answers their open question. Furthermore, our approximation ratio matches that of the maximum clique problem by Feige (2004) up to an $O(\log \log n)$ factor.

</details>


### [275] [New Diameter Approximations via Distance Oracle Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27142)
*Yael Kirkpatrick,Liam Roditty,Richard Qi,Virginia Vassilevska Williams*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了图直径近似与距离预言机之间的内在联系，利用Thorup-Zwick距离预言机的技术去随机化了CGR直径近似算法，首次获得确定性直径近似权衡，并进一步将去随机化技术推广到最短路径近似领域的其他问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前直径近似算法仅使用了距离预言机研究中的一小部分技术，而这两类问题在本质上都是关于最短路径的近似计算，自然存在结合的可能性。此外，已知的最佳直径近似算法是随机化的，且没有已知的类似确定性算法。

Method: 该工作首先建立了CGR直径近似算法与Thorup-Zwick的(2k-1)近似距离预言机之间的紧密联系；然后对这些核心技巧进行去随机化处理，并将其应用于直径近似问题；最终将这些技术推广到最短路径近似领域的其他问题。

Result: 1. 去随机化了CGR算法，获得了首个确定性直径近似权衡曲线；2. 去随机化了距离预言机中的其他核心技术，获得了新的确定性直径近似算法；3. 展示了这些技术可用于去随机化最短路径近似领域中的多个已知最佳结果。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了图的近似直径计算与距离预言机之间存在深刻联系，为最短路径近似领域提供了新的确定性算法工具，证明了距离预言机研究中的许多核心技巧可以迁移到直径近似问题中。

Abstract: Computing the diameter of a graph is a problem of great interest both in general algorithms research and specifically within fine-grained complexity, where it is a cornerstone hard problem. Recent work has achieved a full conditional lower bound tradeoff curve for both directed and undirected graphs. However, the best known upper bounds do not match the lower bounds. In particular, the best known approximation scheme for undirected graph diameter has not been improved. Moreover, this scheme is randomized and no similar deterministic scheme is known.
  Another fundamental field of research in shortest paths computation is the construction of approximate distance oracles. Thorup and Zwick [JACM'05] provided the first such distance oracle with constant query time and (conditionally) optimal space, and in the years since many advances have led to a vast toolbox of techniques and data structures.
  These two areas of research seem natural to combine since they both concern approximating shortest paths. However, the known diameter approximation algorithms only use a small subset of the techniques used in distance oracles research.
